# Convergence State — projectsites.dev

> Durable brain for the `.claude/loop.md` convergence loop. Convergence MAP, not a journal.
> Reload at the start of every iteration. Statuses: UNREVIEWED · AUDITING · REFACTORING · VERIFYING · CONVERGED · BLOCKED.
> **Per-iteration detail lives in git history — the commits ARE the journal. This file is the MAP.** (Compressed iter 25; iters 1-24 folded into "Converged arcs" below.)

## Repo shape (authoritative)
- **Worker** (`apps/project-sites/src` + `libs/features/*`) — CF Workers + Hono + D1/R2/KV/DO. Jest (`.cjs`, `@swc/jest`). Run from `apps/project-sites` (NOT repo root → babel trap). `npm test` ≈ 670 suites / ~10.7k tests.
- **Frontend** (`apps/project-sites/frontend`) — Angular 21 standalone, signals, Karma+Jasmine units, Playwright E2E. Spartan/helm partial. `npm run build:prod`, `npm run deploy:production` (R2).
- **Feature modules** `libs/features/<slug>/` — manifest (`feature.manifest.ts` OR `manifest.ts`) + flag. Gates: `validate-feature-drift.mjs` + `validate-feature-manifests.mjs` (both have `GROUPED_FLAG_KEYS` + `__core__` exemptions).
- **Flags**: `src/modules/feature_flags/registry.ts` (runtime SoT) mirrors `docs.ts`. Prod D1 `feature_flags` keys on `flag_name` (NOT `key`); overrides on `flag_key`. After a flag change, re-check for constant-based `FLAG_KEY` drift.
- ⚠️ Deploy worker needs Docker + GLOBAL CF key (`get-secret CLOUDFLARE_API_KEY`, email `blzalewski@gmail.com`). Frontend = R2, no Docker. **ALWAYS pass `--config wrangler.toml`** — bare `wrangler deploy` misfires monorepo framework auto-detection (pnpm workspace). `npx wrangler deploy --env production --config wrangler.toml`.
- ⚠️ **Shared working tree, 7+ concurrent sessions + crons.** Stage EXPLICIT paths, never `git add -A`. `pull --rebase --autostash`. Concurrent sessions periodically prettier-reformat my just-shipped files (clean fast-forwards). Sidebar/admin-shell BLOCKED (concurrent session owns `admin.component.*` + `nav-icon` + `admin-navigation-responsive.e2e.ts`).

- **Rate-limit SSOT** (`middleware/rate_limit.ts` `RATE_LIMIT_RULES` + `applyRateLimits` at index.ts:420): comprehensive per-IP budgets on auth/search/contact/feedback/create/forms/ai/bolt(vision/transcribe/suggest/chat)/oauth/mcp + now media-generate (iter 41). KV-counter, fails-OPEN on KV outage. When adding a public/cost endpoint, add its rule HERE (the test imports the array → no drift).
- **FE↔worker contract** (iters 26-27, SWEPT): a FE call to a NON-existent worker route (or response-key mismatch) silently shows empty. iter 26 fixed the missing `/api/voice/conversations/:id` (transcript detail). iter 27 SWEPT the rest — clean: onboarding (exists, flag-gated), posts/analytics, logs/tail, webhooks/deliveries, mcp/connections (`{data:{connections}}` matches both readers) all EXIST + match; api.service `/*` = generic-wrapper false-positives. ⚠️ The unwired-endpoint gate has false-negatives on FE dynamic paths that prefix-match a list route (missed voice/conversations/:id).
- **Incomplete surfaces (FE built, backend missing — focused-session builds, NOT cron slices):**
  1. **site-mcp tokens/tools/tool-usage** (feature #29, `/admin/sites/:id/mcp-server`): 5 endpoints missing (`GET/POST /mcp/tokens`, `DELETE /mcp/tokens/:id`, `GET /mcp/tools`, `/mcp/tool-usage`). Flag `site_mcp_server` is DARK (not in registry → 404s regardless), so the page is dark — NOT a live bug. Table `site_mcp_tokens` EXISTS (0514). Tractable core = tokens CRUD (assertSiteOwned + SHA-256 hash, raw shown once). Build ON promotion per CLAUDE.md's dormant-features guidance, NOT speculatively.
  2. **voice conversation downloads + playback** — ✅ DONE (iters 27-28), feature CLOSED: worker download route (txt/vtt/mp3/mp4) + FE authed-blob `download()` (27); detail route now looks up `voice_recordings` → `has_recording`/`has_video` so the mp3/mp4 buttons enable + `<audio>/<video>` play from an authed blob (28, `64cb3a0e`). Nothing left here. (⚠️ Aside, NOT a bug: the conversation LIST route omits `has_recording`/`has_video`, so the list REC/VID badges stay hidden — a cosmetic list-enrichment, not part of the detail feature. Only pursue if a per-row recording indicator is wanted; costs an N-row recordings lookup or a JOIN in the list query.)

## Lessons (hard-won)
- **CHAOS E2E (Brian directive, 2026-08-16, `34b7ad3a`+`b9670669`/deploy `a0143620`): 5 adversarial prod-E2E journeys → found+fixed a soft-404.** Built `frontend/e2e/chaos-{1..5}-*.e2e.ts` + `chaos-helpers.ts` — 5 homepage-first hostile journeys (Skeptical Visitor / Impatient Builder / Locked-Out User / Power Admin / Chaos Monkey) run vs PROD via `playwright.prod.config.ts` (real Chromium; the Browserbase MCP proved token-unviable — `navigate`=10K CDP noise, `extract` token-exploded on the SPA whitespace). **38/38 green.** The site is ROBUST: no XSS executed on ANY surface (search/create/signin/settings/query-string), no pageerrors, no 5xx across all 16 admin sections, honest 400 validation on hostile search, security headers + responsive (6bp) clean. **BUG FOUND+FIXED: soft-404** — the base-domain marketing serve returned `200 marketing/index.html` for EVERY non-file path (`/garbage9999`→200, SEO/AI-crawler junk). Fix: `src/marketing_routes.ts` SSOT (`KNOWN_MARKETING_PREFIXES`+`isKnownMarketingRoute`) + `index.ts` serve returns the shell with a real 404+`noindex` for unknown top-level routes (known routes incl. every `/admin/*`+`blog/:slug` stay 200; real HTML files never soft-404'd). **Drift-guarded**: `marketing_routes.test.ts` PARSES `app.routes.ts` + fails if a real route is missing from the SSOT (would 404 a live page). Verified live: `/garbage9999`→404+noindex, real routes→200 (+WAF 403s `/wp-admin`,`/.env`). NON-bugs ruled out: PWA SW "not registering" = headless-Playwright limitation (files deployed 200 + config correct); `/admin/docs` 404 = benign sub-resource (its `/api/admin/docs/openapi.json` data endpoint is 200). ⚠️ Jest `expect(v,'msg')` 2nd-arg is Playwright/vitest-only → "Expect takes at most one argument" in jest; use `console.error`+`expect(v)`.
- **CHAOS pass-2 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, `fe874e22`, cron `a6815c11` hourly):** hardened the console-hygiene DoD. Extended `chaos-helpers` to capture `console.warn` + same-origin static-asset 404s; added HARD gates to all 5 journeys: `console.error=0`, `console.warn=0`, `notFoundAssets=0` (+ existing pageerror/5xx/XSS). **PROVEN vs prod: console.warn=0** across homepage/create/signin + ALL 16 admin sections; zero missing same-origin assets. KEY: Chromium's URL-less `"Failed to load resource"` console.error is NOISY (fires for cross-origin font-aborts `net::ERR_ABORTED` on fast nav) → delegate to the PRECISE network gates (`notFoundAssets` carries the URL, `serverErrors`=5xx) instead of text-matching; filter the generic line + gstatic. 38/38 green. Admin console is genuinely clean (0 app err/warn) — the earlier `/admin/{analytics,billing,seo}` "404" console.errors were intermittent cross-origin/transient, not same-origin asset 404s.
- **CHAOS pass-3 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, worker fix `f82a1841` deploy `dfd6581f` + E2E `12975666`): press-every-button found a LIVE 502 — the LLM fallback bug.** The suite was 96/96 green at the "renders-alive + hostile-input" level, so per the mission I added a DEEP M2 journey that PRESSES buttons render-sweeps never touch: the Social Auto-Pilot config dialog (`/admin/social` → `social-auto-pilot-prompt-btn`). Exploring it (real Chromium, in-page authed fetch — headless POST is BFM-challenged) proved the **cadence edit→save→hard-reload→persist flow WORKS** (validates iter-107 `upsertAutoPilotConfig`), BUT pressing **"Generate preview" returned 502** `AI_GENERATION_ERROR: OpenAI API error 404 — model 'claude-sonnet-4-6' does not exist`. **ROOT CAUSE (`external_llm.ts` `callExternalLLM` [primary,fallback] loop):** model was `options.model ?? DEFAULT_MODELS[provider]` → the caller's EXPLICIT model (`claude-sonnet-4-6`, meant for `provider:'anthropic'`) was applied to EVERY provider incl. the OpenAI fallback → OpenAI 404s the Claude model → 502. `generateAutoPilotPostForNetwork` requests anthropic+claude; when anthropic is skipped (no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`) it fell to OpenAI carrying the claude model — **broke BOTH the preview endpoint AND the cron draft generation** (same fn, sibling of the iter-42 DeepSeek cross-provider routing bug). **Fix:** the explicit model belongs to the PRIMARY provider only → `provider === primary ? (options.model ?? DEFAULT_MODELS[provider]) : DEFAULT_MODELS[provider]`; the fallback uses its OWN default (`gpt-4o-2024-11-20`, which SUCCEEDS). Applied to both `callExternalLLM` + `callExternalLLMWithVision` (same class). **+1 behavioral RED→GREEN** (anthropic+claude-model+no-anthropic-key → OpenAI fallback body.model === `gpt-4o-2024-11-20`, not the claude model) + **the chaos-4 M2 journey** (cadence persist + preview-no-5xx, self-cleaning via API). **VERIFIED LIVE:** preview `POST /api/social/auto-pilot/preview` now **200** with a real generated post (was 502); chaos-4 auto-pilot journey **1 passed**. worker tsc 0, 80/80 llm+auto-pilot suites. ⚠️ Same button-press-finds-silent-5xx lesson as [[flag-off-frontend-must-match-worker-404]]: render-sweeps pass while a meaningful button 5xxes — only pressing it + asserting the network catches it.
- **CHAOS pass-4 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, E2E `d9134e52`): thorough press-every-button sweep → NO new product bug (honest convergence) + a Social composer M2 journey added.** Hunted unpressed action buttons across the surface: (1) **Compose** — typed content, pressed "Post now" with 0 connected accounts → honest **"Select at least one platform to publish"** toast (not a dead button, not a 5xx) + the draft is PRESERVED on the failed submit; "Discard" is a deliberate action-armed confirm toast ("Discard this post?" → toast Discard action → `resetComposer()`), NOT a one-click wipe. (2) **Broad safe-action-button sweep** across `/admin/{seo,deliverability,domains,mcp,apps,voice}` (pressed Check-deliverability / Refresh / Search-with-AI / Regenerate) → **ZERO 5xx / console.error / pageerror**. (3) **`/admin/seo`** → an INTENTIONAL redirect to `/admin/site-features` (no standalone SEO section; app.routes.ts:410 — search-readiness toggles live under Features) — the "No features yet" is honest (`/api/site-features` returns `{features:[],plan:'free'}` for the free-plan test org; key matches, NOT a lying-empty). **Encoded the compose flow as a deterministic M2 journey** (Post-now honest-validation + draft-preservation + toast-confirm Discard, full console/network DoD). **⚠️ The ONLY "failure" was a wrong TEST assumption** (one-click Discard vs the real toast-confirm) — corrected the test, not the product. **1 passed vs prod.** So this pass CONVERGED: after pressing compose + 6 sections' action buttons + probing site-features, the reachable surface is robust (the auto-pilot 502 was pass-3's find; pass-4 found none). ⚠️ E2E-org constraint still bounds deep mutation coverage: 0 connected social accounts + 1-seat + free-plan → the richest untested mutations (real publish, custom-hostname provision, paid features) need a Browserbase-as-brian super-admin session or a seeded multi-account/multi-seat org. Next browser target: a super-admin Browserbase pass over those gated surfaces, OR the READ-side lying-empty class ([[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]]).
- **CHAOS pass-5 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, frontend fix `af0c5951`, R2 deploy `main-ETR7FNSN.js`): press-every-button found a LIVE cross-device persistence bug — the display name was WRITE-ONLY to the server.** Baseline: all **50 chaos tests GREEN** vs prod (17 public + 33 authed), so per the mission added a DEEP M2 journey on the ONE untested mutate→persist primary action left — the **user-profile display name** (`/admin/user`). Exploring `saveDisplayName()` surfaced the bug: it PATCHes `/api/admin/profile` (server) AND writes localStorage `ps_display_name` (local-first), AND `GET /api/auth/me` already exposes `display_name` — **but NOTHING on the frontend ever READ it back** (`displayName` computed = localStorage override → email-derived default; no code called `/api/auth/me`). So on a fresh device / cleared cache the saved name silently reverted to the email-derived default — the write was UI-invisible everywhere the local override was absent. **This is the read-side "server has the data, UI shows a placeholder" class — a "lying-default" cousin of [[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]] + [[verify-against-source-of-truth]].** **Root cause = a COMPLETED backend + an incomplete frontend read (a stub the mission says to finish); fix is frontend-only:** added a `serverDisplayName` signal fetched from `/api/auth/me` in `ngOnInit`; the `displayName` computed falls back to it BEFORE the email-derived default (the local override still wins → local-first preserved); seeds the input draft so a no-edit Save is a no-op. **TDD RED→GREEN:** new chaos-4 M2 journey — save a distinctive name → assert PATCH **2xx** + "Saved" affordance + heading updates → hard-reload persists (local-first) → **clear ONLY the local cache (fresh device) → reload → assert the heading shows the SAVED name from the server, not "Test"**; app-context `fetch` (real fingerprint, not BFM-challenged) reads+restores the account name in `finally`. **RED confirmed pre-fix** (heading resolved to `<h3 title="Test">Test</h3>` 20×), **GREEN post-deploy**. **Verified:** Karma **1632/1632** · SPA **tsc 0** · frontend built + R2-deployed, served `main-ETR7FNSN.js` hash-matched local · chaos-4 **30/30 green** vs prod. Worker untouched (frontend-only). ⚠️ **NEXT browser target (unchanged, still open):** the READ-side lying-empty class where a LIST surface hides real rows (worker returns `{items}`/`{assets}`, FE reads `.data`) — needs a surface with real rows for the E2E org (or a seeded one); OR a super-admin Browserbase pass over the flag-gated/leads surfaces the 1-seat free-plan test-org can't reach.
- **CHAOS pass-6 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, test-only `5f9375e2`): pressed 2 untested primary surfaces → both VERIFIED WORKING end-to-end (no new bug; honest convergence + coverage).** Baseline **51/51 green** vs prod. Added 2 rigorous journeys on previously-untested surfaces: **(M3) notification-prefs cross-device round-trip.** DIRECTLY hunted CHAOS-5's write-only-field class — my [[write-only-field-local-first-cache-masks-missing-server-read]] memory flagged the sibling notification prefs use the SAME local-first pattern as the (buggy) display name. **VERIFIED they are built CORRECTLY (not write-only):** `hydrateNotificationPrefs()` DOES GET `/admin/notifications` (server read-back exists), the worker route (`api.ts:11112` GET / `11137` POST) persists the `Record<string,boolean>` pref map to the per-user memory store (`getMemory`/`setMemory`, `scope_kind='user'`, no new table), Zod-validated, keys align (`{data:{prefs}}`). Encoded as an M3 journey: toggle the "Weekly summary" switch → assert debounced POST **2xx** + immediate flip → local-first reload persists → clear ONLY `ps_notification_prefs` (fresh device) → wait for the hydrate **GET** → assert the switch reflects the SERVER value (not the `true` default). Self-restoring. **GREEN.** **(M4) delete-account destructive guard.** Double-guarded (`[disabled]` until the EXACT phrase `delete my account` + `performDelete()` early-returns on mismatch). Verified: disabled w/ no phrase → WRONG phrase stays disabled + no ready state → EXACT phrase enables + shows ready → **Cancel** (NEVER clicks "Delete forever") → account survives a reload (`us-profile-card` still renders). **GREEN.** Full chaos suite **53/53 green** vs prod, parallel-safe. Test-only (E2E not deployed → no R2/worker deploy). Committed `5f9375e2`. **So this pass CONVERGED:** the write-only-field class does NOT recur in the sibling notification prefs (built correctly w/ server read-back + persistence), and the destructive guard holds. **⚠️ NEXT browser target:** the reachable untested-with-real-mutation surface is thinning for the 1-seat free-plan E2E org — remaining candidates: (1) SELECT-omission round-trip on Settings-General fields ([[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]] P0.54 class — a live PUT→GET reconcile per `e2e/admin-verify/settings-roundtrip.mjs`; ⚠️ a concurrent session may own settings); (2) the display-vs-store reconciler `e2e/admin-verify/reconcile-surfaces.mjs` against the real account; (3) super-admin Browserbase for the flag-gated/leads surfaces the test-org can't reach.
- **CHAOS pass-7 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, test-only, frontend `chaos-1`): probed 5 suspected-bug leads → ALL cleared (false-alarm/intentional); found + fixed a VACUOUS-test stub + added the missing M1 press-every-nav-control coverage. No new product bug (honest convergence).** Baseline: full 5-journey chaos suite **53/53 green** vs prod (chaos-1 6/6, chaos-2/3/5 15/15, chaos-4 32/32) — so per the mission I hunted where the GREEN suite could be hiding a real defect (a passing test that asserts nothing, or an unpressed control). **5 leads chased, all cleared:** (1) **`CHAOS1/links: []`** — the homepage nav/footer internal-link test collected `[]` and asserted nothing → looked like a missing-nav bug; PROVEN it's a **timing stub** (the anchors `/developers /blog /privacy /terms /content` hydrate AFTER the test's bare 1500ms wait → the 5xx check ran over an empty set). (2) **"marketing hero bleeding into every /admin section"** (body.innerText started with the hero copy on all admin routes) → FALSE ALARM: `heroVisible:false` — a hidden SSR/prerender block in DOM order, not rendered into admin. (3) **`/admin/seo` → `/admin/site-features` redirect** → INTENTIONAL (`app.routes.ts:410`, documented "no standalone SEO section"). (4) **"sites list shows 1 of 4"** — `/api/sites` returns **4** (`urban-fitness`/`lakeside-dental`/`acme-bakery`/`green-thumb`) but a probe found only `urban-fitness` in the DOM → FALSE ALARM on TWO counts: `/admin/sites` isn't a real route (only `sites/:id`; the list is the switcher, in the BLOCKED `admin.component`), the switcher renders sites by **`business_name`** not slug (my slug-search missed them), AND the dashboard screenshot proves the app correctly shows **"4 sites in your account"** + a "4 / 1 SITES" badge — no lying-empty. (5) **"Get Started" primary CTA appears dead** (click → stays on `/`, no URL change) → FALSE ALARM: it **focuses the hero business-search input** (`activeElement` → INPUT "Search for your business…") — the real zero-to-value funnel-start; "ES" toggle correctly flips `<html lang>` to `es`. **DELIVERED (test-only, product is correct):** (a) **hardened the vacuous link stub** — now `waitForFunction` for ≥4 hydrated anchors + asserts `unique.length >= 4` + treats **4xx** (broken link) not just 5xx as failure (verified all 6 links 200) → can never pass empty again (RED at 1500ms → GREEN with the wait); (b) **added an M1 "press EVERY homepage nav control + ASSERT the business result" journey** — Features/Pricing/FAQ must scroll (`scrollY>50`), Get Started must focus the hero search, Sign In must route to `/signin`, ES must set `<html lang>=es` — the exact button-based controls the link-crawl can't reach (the mission's "press every button" mandate for M1). chaos-1 **6→7 green** vs prod; full suite **54/54**. Test-only (E2E not deployed → no R2/worker deploy). **So this pass CONVERGED: after probing marketing nav + admin routing + the sites data surface + the primary CTA, the reachable surface has no new product defect — the only gap was a false-confidence test stub, now closed.** ⚠️ **NEXT browser target (unchanged constraint):** the 1-seat free-plan E2E-org bounds deep-mutation coverage; the remaining real bug-surfaces need either a **super-admin Browserbase-as-brian** session (flag-gated/leads/multi-seat surfaces) or the **display-vs-store reconciler** (`e2e/admin-verify/reconcile-surfaces.mjs`) run against a real account with real rows — a green render + a passing test still can't see a wrong-source read.
- **CHAOS pass-8 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, shared `d92…` + worker + frontend `chaos-4`): pressed the Cmd+K AI feature → found + fixed a LIVE audit-trail DROP for every non-UUID org (server-side, invisible to render/console gates).** Baseline: full suite **54/54 green** — so per the mission I pursued the iter-118-tail lead (a defect NO browser gate can see: a server-side audit error). Pressing the **Cmd+K AI palette** (`POST /api/admin/ai/stream/palette`, writes a fire-and-forget `cmdk.ai.answered` audit) reproduced it: `writeAuditLog` → `createAuditLogSchema.parse` → **THREW `Invalid uuid`** because the shared schema required `org_id`/`actor_id`/`target_id` to be UUIDs, but the E2E key (and seed orgs) resolve to a real D1 row `org_id: 'e2e-test-org'` (the columns are **TEXT**, not UUID). The `.parse` throw → the outer try/catch logged "Audit log write threw unexpectedly" and **silently DROPPED the entry** → EVERY audit action by any non-UUID org was lost (a compliance-trail gap, same swallow-class as [[swallowed-sql-error-masks-schema-drift-as-404]] but for a Zod throw). **ROOT-CAUSE FIX (shared package, the contract):** replaced `org_id/actor_id/target_id: uuidSchema` with a shared `auditIdSchema = z.string().min(1).max(128)` in BOTH `createAuditLogSchema` (write) AND `auditLogSchema` (read — kept consistent so a future `getAuditLogs` row-validation can't re-introduce the drop). A UUID is still valid — this only WIDENS to the real TEXT id set (seed/test orgs, `system` actors, slug/hostname targets). Dropped the now-unused `uuidSchema` import. **TDD RED→GREEN:** worker `audit.test` (RED: non-UUID `org_id` → `dbInsert` 0 calls → GREEN: inserts) + shared `schemas-extended.test` (non-UUID create accepted). **Browser M3 regression:** a new `chaos-4` journey presses the Cmd+K AI (app-context fetch — headless POST is BFM-challenged) → asserts 200 + a real streamed answer → **polls `/api/audit/rows` for a FRESH `cmdk.ai.answered` row (created_at > pre-trigger max)** — the causal action→audit-trail cross-system proof. shared **tsc 0 / 210 tests** · worker **tsc 0 / audit 44** · deploy `00a3717b` · chaos-4 **32→33 green**. **VERIFIED LIVE end-to-end:** tail shows ZERO "threw unexpectedly" after the fix (was firing every call); **ground-truth `/api/audit/rows` shows the fresh `cmdk.ai.answered` entries LANDED** (top of the trail) — [[verify-against-source-of-truth]] causal proof (action → store records it). ⚠️ **NEXT:** the `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` detector (premature-terminal class at 2); OR super-admin Browserbase for the flag-gated/leads/multi-seat surfaces the 1-seat free-plan E2E org can't reach.
- **CHAOS pass-9 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, worker migration + detector, shared tree): sealed the iter-121-surfaced SYSTEM-org audit-FK drop — every pre-auth SECURITY audit (`auth.magic_link_requested`, OAuth starts) was being discarded by a D1 FOREIGN KEY reject.** Baseline: full chaos suite **55/55 green** (no regression from the iter-121 email P0) — so I closed the highest-value browser-triggered lead from the iter-121 tail. **ROOT CAUSE:** pre-auth events are audited with `org_id:'system'` (the user may not exist yet — `api.ts` magic-link/OAuth handlers 276/582/620/758, INTENTIONAL per the in-code comment), but `audit_logs.org_id` is `TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES orgs(id)` and **no `system` org row existed** → EVERY system-scoped `writeAuditLog` failed `D1_ERROR: FOREIGN KEY constraint failed` and the security-audit entry was silently dropped (a compliance-trail gap invisible to render/console/E2E gates; pass-8 relaxed the Zod schema so the write REACHED the DB, then the FK rejected it — this is the layer beneath pass-8). **FIX (`migrations/0613_seed_system_org.sql`, pure DATA — no worker code, no redeploy):** seed a sentinel `system` org `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO orgs (id,name,slug,…) VALUES ('system','System (internal)','system',…)`, seeded **soft-deleted-from-birth** (`deleted_at`=epoch) so it's a pure FK-satisfier: the FK only checks PK existence (ignores `deleted_at`) → satisfied; every app query is `WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` (weekly_digest, org listings/counts) → the sentinel never leaks into any user-facing surface; the audit READ (`GET /api/audit/rows` `WHERE org_id=<caller>`, NO orgs JOIN) never surfaces `system` rows anyway (no user is a member of `system`). **Applied to BOTH prod D1s** (`project-sites-db-production` ea3e839a + default `project-sites-db` f5b59818). **CAUSAL PROOF ([[verify-against-source-of-truth]], WAF-free):** the live magic-link POST is CF-managed-challenge-blocked to curl (403 "Just a moment" — the challenge-skip covers `/api/mcp`+`/oauth/*`, NOT `/api/auth/*` per [[waf-mcp-skip-rule]]), so I reproduced writeAuditLog's EXACT DB op against prod D1 — [PROOF] `INSERT INTO audit_logs (org_id='system', action='auth.magic_link_requested')` now **SUCCEEDS** (`rows_written:7`; row confirmed landed) where it FK-failed before; [NEGATIVE CONTROL] the same insert for a NONEXISTENT org **still fails** `FOREIGN KEY constraint failed [code 7500]` (the identical iter-121 error) → proves the FK is genuinely enforced and the `system` seed is precisely what fixes it (not FKs disabled); probe rows cleaned up (append-only log stays clean). **DURABLE REGRESSION (detector, per [[audit-arc-detector-finds-bugs]]):** `scripts/check-system-org-seed.mjs` + 9-case node:test — a repo-level cross-check that FLAGS iff code writes `org_id:'system'` sentinel audits (2 call-site files today) AND no migration seeds the `system` org; guards a future migration squash/reset or a fresh-project copy from re-introducing the drop. Wired `check:system-org-seed` + into `test:scripts` (**48/48 green**); clean live (`--ci` exit 0). ⚠️ **NEXT:** continue fresh-subsystem behavioral audits (email→P0, audit-FK→this — the iter-53/121 pivot keeps surfacing server-side defects NO browser gate can see); OR super-admin Browserbase for the flag-gated/leads/multi-seat surfaces the 1-seat free-plan E2E org can't reach.
- **CHAOS pass-10 (`/loop` browser-TDD, 2026-08-16, frontend E2E-only): thorough browser hunt → NO new product bug (honest convergence); advanced M2 with a live settings-persist journey + hardened the console-noise filter.** Baseline: full chaos suite **55/55 green**. Per the mission I hunted where the green suite could hide a bug + advanced the least-covered persist paths. **(1) NEW M2 journey — `chaos-4` "Settings › General business phone round-trips to the SERVER":** the render/tab sweeps never SAVE; this presses `general-save` after mutating `business-phone`, then **nav-away → return → HARD RELOAD** and asserts the server echoed the value (write-only/lying-success guard), then restores the original. **Verified the wiring is CORRECT first** (`saveGeneral`→`api.updateSite`(site record)+`PUT /sites/:id/ai-settings`(contact_email); `loadGeneral` reads business fields from `selectedSite()` and contact_email from `GET ai-settings` — read source = write source) AND the ai-settings PUT schema `z.union([literal(''),null,email]).optional()` already accepts `null` (the `optional-field-null-rejected` class is handled) — then PROVED it live (round-trip persists; **passes 15.9s**). Net-new coverage on a real gap, NOT a bug. **(2) Exploratory button-press probe** (throwaway, deleted) over 7 untested frontiers (`domains`/`seo`/`snapshots`/`forms`/`deliverability`/`billing`/`mcp`) pressing ~10 non-destructive buttons each while watching the network: **ZERO app defects** — no non-2xx `/api` (excl 401/404), no app console errors, no 5xx, no erroring dead buttons. **(3) Filter hardening (`chaos-helpers.ts`):** the probe surfaced a benign `w-credentialless-staticblitz.com` "<link preload> not used" warning — StackBlitz's WebContainer CDN, emitted by the bolt editor iframe `BoltEmbedService` pre-boots on admin surfaces. The IGNORE list had `stackblitz` but MISSES `staticblitz` (StackBlitz's actual CDN domain) → a future interactive chaos test could false-fail on it. But `test-console.component.ts sendSms` read `r.data?.reply` + `r.data?.conversation_id` — the worker returns `simulateInbound`'s shape at the **TOP level** (`c.json(result)`, NO `data` wrapper; key is `replyText`, not `reply`) → `r.data.reply` undefined → the console appended **"(no reply)"** on every send despite a real backend reply ([[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]]). The `conversation_id` round-trip was fully DEAD too — worker Zod (`testSmsBody`) accepts `{siteId, body}` ONLY (strips from_number/conversation_id) and `simulateInbound` is a stateless dry-run (returns none). **FIX (FE-only):** read `r.replyText`; deleted the dead `smsConvId` field + the `from_number`/`conversation_id` request fields. **TDD RED→GREEN:** Karma unit (mock `post→of({replyText})` → `sendSms()` → assert the agent thread shows the reply not "(no reply)"; deterministic RED — no `data` key). FE tsc 0, **full Karma 1638/1638** (gotcha #9). - **RENAME (Brian directive, 2026-08-15, `2c8ab694`/deploy `6543337d`): vibe-named grab-bags → descriptive.** `services/brilliant.ts` → `services/experimental_features.ts` (10 features); `services/big_bets.ts` → `services/advanced_features.ts` (30 features) — + their test files + the `import * as B` alias → `experimentalFeatures`. Any stale reference/memory pointing at `brilliant.ts`/`big_bets.ts` now uses the new names. DB tables were ALREADY descriptive (`site_mcp_subscriptions`/`cold_tier_state`/`ghost_routes`/… — no `brilliant_*` table) so no migration. Migration FILENAMES `0502_brilliant_ten.sql`/`0503_big_bets.sql` KEPT (applied → renaming breaks wrangler `d1_migrations` tracking). Rule extended: `[[naming-no-transient-prefixes]]` now also bans vibe/hype adjectives (brilliant/big_bets/awesome/magic) as identifiers, not just wave/sprint prefixes.
- **`db.batch([stmt,stmt])`** is THE atomic-multi-write pattern (implicit txn, rejects+rolls-back on any failure). Convention ref: `credits.ts`. Test it by giving the D1 mock a capturing `prepare`+`batch` jest.fn (+ `mockRejectedValueOnce` for the atomicity/rollback assertion).
- When a source change alters a function's D1 access pattern (e.g. dbUpdate→batch), grep **ALL** `*.test.ts` calling it + update every mock; verify each **STANDALONE** — co-scheduled `--runInBand` runs mask which suite actually fails (iter-19 regression misdiagnosed as a "flake", caught iter 22).
- **Config-tested ≠ behavior-tested (iter 42 DeepSeek routing).** A green test suite can hide a live bug when it tests the CONFIG (`chooseProviderForTier` returns 'deepseek', the base-URL CONSTANT is 'api.deepseek.com') but never the actual ROUTING (does a call ACTUALLY hit api.deepseek.com?). The DeepSeek tier's config was 100% correct + tested, yet every call went to OpenAI's URL because the dispatch dropped the provider. **When auditing a routing/dispatch/selection service, don't trust "config resolves to X" tests — add a BEHAVIORAL test that mocks the network seam (`gatewayFetch`) and asserts the request went to X.** (Sibling of the [[verify-against-source-of-truth]] render-vs-endpoint blind spot: selection-vs-dispatch.)
- **Re-pathing an FE call must update its Karma SPEC assertions too, not just call sites (iter 38 caught iter 32).** iter 32 re-pathed the integrations DELETE `/integrations/` → `/integration-providers/` (code + 2 call sites) but left `site-detail.component.spec.ts:389` asserting the old path. It stayed RED from iter 32→38 because the FULL frontend Karma suite wasn't run to completion (state claimed "Karma 1592/1592 iter 4" — stale). **Lesson: (a) when touching any FE `/api` path, grep the SPECS for the old string + update assertions; (b) a frontend change's verification MUST run the WHOLE `npm run test:ci` (1610 tests, ~2min) — a scoped run hides stale specs elsewhere. Adding ANY spec = run the full suite (gotcha #9 order-flake + surfaces long-hidden reds).**
- **Angular control-flow migration = official schematic, not hand-edits (iter 37).** `ng g @angular/core:control-flow --path=<dir> --interactive=false` migrates `*ngIf`/`*ngFor`/`*ngSwitch` → `@if`/`@for`/`@switch` AND is SMART: it removes the now-unused `CommonModule` import + swaps in the SPECIFIC pipe (`CurrencyPipe`) a template still needs — the DELETE pass is done for you. `--path` scopes it (avoid the BLOCKED admin-shell). Dry-run first to confirm scope. After: the only residual `*ngIf`/`*ngFor` are STALE PROSE COMMENTS (perl `s/\*ngIf/@if/g` cleans them — templates are already migrated so it only hits comments). Verify via `ng build` (AOT compiles EVERY template even tree-shaken ones → a broken migration or wrongly-dropped CommonModule = loud compile error) since site-kit has 0 Karma specs. ⚠️ `\| number` grep false-positives on TS union types (`x: string \| number`) — read context.
- **"Saturation" is premature while whole SUBSYSTEMS remain unexamined (iter 53).** iters 51-52 declared "terminal saturation" — but that was measured against the surfaces I'd ALREADY audited (services/routes/dead-code). iter 53 pivoted to a genuinely-unexamined subsystem, `build_validators.ts` (the product-core quality gates, LIVE, never behaviorally audited), and immediately found a real bug (metaDescLength apostrophe truncation) firing on the flagship test business. **Lesson: when a loop feels saturated, the saturation is usually LOCAL to the audited surfaces — PIVOT to a genuinely-unexamined subsystem (grep for services/routes with 0 prior refactor-state mentions) BEFORE concluding global saturation.** Unexamined-but-live product-core code (validators, build pipeline, middleware chain) is where the next real bug hides. Do NOT emit "saturated, honest checkpoint" until every LIVE subsystem has ≥1 behavioral audit recorded.
- **knip's "unused files" misses self-referential module+tests islands — BUT the "0-production-caller" sweep is a false-positive MINEFIELD, not a bulk deletion tool (iters 50-51).** iter 50 found `confidence.ts` (906-line `transformToV3` v3-research pipeline, orphaned by the container-arch refactor) that knip missed because its 52K of tests count as "consumers." Real win: 2224 lines deleted. **BUT iter 51 ran the 0-caller sweep at SCALE and it flagged ~220 services — including `turnstile` (definitely live: better-auth/forms/listmonk import it), i.e. a broken-grep false positive — AND even the sharper "0-caller + has-test" variant false-positived `plan_migration` (imported by `plan_migration_log`).** The repo has a LARGE INTENTIONAL built-ahead surface (~220 uncalled services from the "40-list build wave" + platform fan-outs per [[dead-code-scan-baseline]]). **So: `0 production callers` alone is NOT a delete signal — it's dominated by built-ahead.** A module is safely-deletable ONLY with confidence.ts's FULL signature: (1) 0 non-test importers across `src/`+`libs/`, (2) a DEDICATED test file is the sole consumer, AND (3) **`git log -S` proves it served an architecture that was EXPLICITLY REPLACED** (built-BEHIND), not parked-for-future. iter 51's 4 narrowed candidates (`outbox_processor`=built-ahead "spine", `plan_migration`=has-caller, `theme_polarity`=documented "Logo Luminance" feature, `voice_twiml`=active voice area) ALL failed condition (3) → KEPT. ⚠️ Do NOT build a `check-orphaned-modules.mjs` DELETION gate off this — per [[validator-precision-discipline]] it would be a false-positive machine. confidence.ts was a rare clearly-replaced-arch case, NOT a generalizable vein.
- **A source-grep "moot / dead / safe-to-delete" verdict MUST be prod-verified before any route/handler deletion (iter 47 near-miss).** iter 33 concluded (from reading code) that the 2nd `GET /` handler was moot and instructed a future session to DELETE it. iter 47 prod-curled the actual hosts first and found it serves FIVE live 200 landing pages — the delete would have 404'd llm/analytics/logs/webhooks/links. The grep-analysis conflated two similarly-named hosts (`llm.projectsites.dev` vs `llm.megabyte.space`) and assumed container-interception that didn't exist. **Before deleting ANY route/handler a prior analysis called dead: curl every host/path it could serve, in prod, and confirm something ELSE already serves them.** A stale "safe to delete" note is worse than no note — it launders a guess as a verified fact. Sibling of [[grep-closed-not-resolved-redirect-hidden-request]] + [[verify-against-source-of-truth]]. When correcting such a note, fix it IN the durable artifact (here: the detector allowlist comment) so the bad guidance can't mislead again.
- **Hand-rolled duplicate maps drift silently from their canonical (iter 44).** The same lookup table (a MIME map, a config map, a provider list) copied inline at N call-sites WILL drift — one copy gains an entry the others lack, and the gap is a silent wrong-output bug (a `.webp` served as `octet-stream`, never an error). iter 44 found THREE content-type maps, all divergent from `getContentType`. **Fix = export the ONE canonical, route every call-site through it, delete the copies.** When you see a second inline copy of a lookup that already exists as a function, that's the bug — collapse to the SSOT, don't patch the copy. Sibling of [[aggregate-endpoint-drifts-from-per-item-truth]] (rollup re-implements per-item probe → diverges) — same class, config-map form. Grep for the class (`Record<string, string>` inline maps, repeated key literals like `'image/png'`) before claiming a serving path converged.
- **Grep-close the CLASS before claiming a sweep (iter 35).** Fixing the instance in front of you under-delivers when the same bug shape lives in sibling files. iter 35 started with 2 instances found by local reading (site-generation inline + cron); the class-grep (`FROM memberships`/`JOIN memberships` minus `deleted_at`, then READ each multi-line context) surfaced **3 MORE** in separate service files (`notify_site_built`, `notify`, `build_limits`) that the initial reading never touched. The tree-wide grep + per-hit context-read is what converts "fixed a bug" into "swept the class." ⚠️ Single-line grep is only a CANDIDATE list — multi-line queries carry `deleted_at IS NULL` on the NEXT line (`weekly_digest`/`auth_org`/`wallet` were false-positive "misses"); always read the surrounding lines before judging.

## ⚠️ SURFACED — Brian decisions (approval-tier, NOT auto-fixed)
- **🔴 Google Places billing DISABLED — create-funnel search DEAD in prod** (iter 90, P0): `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY`'s GCP project has billing OFF → legacy Places API says *"You must enable Billing on the Google Cloud Project"*, new API 403 PERMISSION_DENIED. Business + address search (the funnel's first step) return honest `_error` (degrade gracefully, NOT lying-empty — the arc's payoff), but NO user can search their business until billing is enabled. **Unblock:** enable billing at `https://console.cloud.google.com/billing` for the project owning the key (verify the key has "Places API (New)" enabled + no over-restrictive IP/referrer restriction blocking the Worker). Approval-tier = billing/payment.
- **Wallet credit double-spend** (iter 18 surfaced → ✅ LIVE PATH FIXED iter-176, deploy `7fdeacba`): the surfaced analysis looked at `creditWallet`'s `wallet_transactions.stripe_event_id` TOCTOU (plain INDEX not UNIQUE, `0036:80`) IN ISOLATION and missed the real first-line gate. `webhook_events` DOES have `UNIQUE(provider,event_id)` (`0001:196`/`0579:32`) — but it was DEFEATED: `dbInsert` RETURNS `{error}` (never throws), `storeWebhookEvent` returns `{id:null,error}`, and `webhooks.ts:181` **ignored `stored.error`** (`const stored=…; webhookEventId=stored.id??undefined`) → the concurrent-duplicate LOSER of the UNIQUE race fell through to step-5 processing → double-credit / double-activate. **FIX (code-only, NO migration):** the Stripe handler now branches on `stored.error` — `UNIQUE constraint failed` → `200 {duplicate}` (do NOT process), any other error → `500` via `internalError` (Stripe RETRIES, never silently drop). Same fix applied to `livekit_webhooks.ts:133` (same swallowed-error class; benign today — processing is just a log — but future recording/transcript side-effects would double-fire). TDD RED(2)→GREEN in `webhook_route.test.ts` (concurrent-dup loser doesn't process + non-uniqueness store-fail 500s); **544/544 webhook+billing green**, tsc 0, prod `/webhooks/stripe` bad-sig→401 (route live). Class = `dbinsert-lying-success` at the webhook idempotency gate ([[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]]). **RESIDUAL (defense-in-depth, approval-tier, LOW urgency now the first-line gate holds):** read-only prod dup-count (iter-176) = **0 dup groups in BOTH `webhook_events(provider,event_id)` AND `wallet_transactions(stripe_event_id)`** → the double-spend NEVER fired historically (fix is preventive) + the additive unique-index is dup-risk-free. BUT a `UNIQUE(wallet_transactions.stripe_event_id)` index ALONE is INSUFFICIENT: `creditWallet` does the balance `UPDATE wallet_accounts SET balance+=?` BEFORE the ledger `INSERT`, so a ledger-unique-index wouldn't stop a balance double-increment — the true Layer-2 fix is insert-ledger-FIRST-as-idempotency-claim + then balance (money-path refactor, approval-tier). Moot for the Stripe path now the webhook gate holds; only matters if a NON-webhook concurrent `creditWallet` caller appears.
- **🔑 CF_API_TOKEN lacks zone-analytics-read** (iter 119): the `/api/analytics/:siteId` CF-zone fallback query is now schema-VALID but the token errors `Actor '…token…' does not have permission 'com.cloudflare.api.account.zone.analytics.read' for zone 9ceaa211750dd31899fd5d1bf8d1ec46`. **Unblock:** add the **Zone Analytics: Read** permission to the `CF_API_TOKEN` at `https://dash.cloudflare.com/profile/api-tokens` (edit the token → add `Zone · Analytics · Read` for projectsites.dev), or mint a new token with that scope → `wrangler secret put CF_API_TOKEN`. Low urgency — the D1 `visitor_events` primary (`first_party_edge`) serves real analytics; this only enables the edge-traffic fallback tier. Approval-tier = secret/token scope.
- **hey@ unlimited?** (iter 11): 1-line in `isUnlimitedOrgOwner` (build_limits.ts) — grants FREE UNLIMITED COMPUTE, a cost decision.
- **site_benchmarks schema** (iter 17, low): lacks `updated_at`+`deleted_at`. Runtime self-healing retry already fixes the data-loss bug; a migration is cosmetic convention-alignment (prod-apply to BOTH D1s).

**THE PROGRAM (loop executes these, in order):**

2. **Run the REAL create→build E2E** (`create-edit-publish-flow.spec.ts`, `E2E_REAL_BUILD=1`) — ⚠️ **DELIBERATE, human-gated run, NOT loop-fired.** `create-from-search` immediately spins the full `SITE_WORKFLOW` (research → **container build** → publish) = one ~$5-15 / ~40-min build with no cheap draft-only path (search.ts:669-687). 3. **Add one real-backend full-flow E2E per complex flow** via the loop (domains, billing, media, snapshots, social, publish, auth) — real backend, not mocked. **This + cheap real-flow bug-hunting is the loop's sustainable job** (paid builds are step-2 deliberate-only).

**iter 59 (deployed `16df43b3`, verified live):** 🐛 **SEO bug fixed** — `serveSiteFromR2` served the branded "Building..." placeholder (any site with `current_build_version === null`, i.e. its ENTIRE ~40-min build window) as **HTTP 200 with NO robots directive** → Googlebot could index "Building your website" as the site's content. Added `<meta name=robots noindex,nofollow>` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow` (kept 200 so the auto-refresh UX is intact). 3 unit tests; 89/89 site_serving green. Verified live: `mock-restaurant.projectsites.dev` (placeholder) now `noindex`; `megabytespace` (real) still `index,follow`. Defect class contained (only indexable-200 placeholder in the serve path; other Responses are real assets or 404s).

**iter 60 (deployed `622197d9`, verified live):** 🐛 **Social-preview bug fixed** — the build template emits an ABSOLUTE `twitter:image` but a ROOT-RELATIVE `og:image` (`content="/og-image.png"`). FB/LinkedIn/Slack/Discord fetch OG tags out of page context → a relative `og:image` silently breaks the link-preview image on EVERY generated site. New serving-layer pure transform `absolutizeSocialImages()` (sibling of `asyncifyRenderBlockingFonts`, runs in `buildSiteResponse` for all plans) rewrites relative `og:image`/`og:image:url`/`og:image:secure_url`/`twitter:image` → absolute using the page's own `og:url`/canonical origin. Additive+safe (absolute + protocol-relative untouched; no-op without a base). 7 unit tests; 96/96 site_serving green. Verified live: `megabytespace` `og:image` now `https://megabyte.space/og-image.png` (was `/og-image.png`). **site-serving SEO/social subsystem = CONVERGED** for the indexable-placeholder + relative-og:image classes.

**iter 61 (deployed `56036492`, verified live):** 🐛 **Soft-404 eliminated** on every generated site. Generated sites are pure client-side SPAs — `serveSiteFromR2` served the SAME `index.html` HTTP **200** for any extensionless path (real route OR junk) → search engines indexed nonexistent URLs. Fix: the SPA fallback (site_serving.ts) now derives the real route set from the site's own `sitemap.xml` (`parseSitemapRoutes` — exported/pure, cached in KV via `getKnownRoutes` keyed by immutable `version`) and returns **HTTP 404** for a path the sitemap doesn't list — still shipping the shell (so the SPA 404 view renders) + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow` on the 404. **Fail-open (200) when no sitemap exists** so a real route is never wrongly 404'd (also preserves the existing SPA-fallback test). 11 unit tests; full worker suite **671 suites / 10,734 tests green**. Verified live: `megabytespace` real routes `/`,`/about`,`/services`,`/community` → 200; junk `/this-page-does-not-exist-xyz`,`/random-junk-999` → 404+noindex, shell still present. **site-serving SEO/social/soft-404 subsystem = CONVERGED** (3 classes fixed: indexable placeholder [59] · relative og:image [60] · soft-404 [61]).

**iter 62 (deployed `496bc0fb`, verified live + reconciled):** 🐛 **Contact-form lying-FAILURE fixed** (the #1 conversion action). `POST /api/contact-form/:slug` persists the durable `contacts` row FIRST (already error-checked, `updated_at` present — NOT a lying-success), but then still returned **400** when the site had no `contact_email` (skipping the in-app bell) and **500** when the email provider threw — BOTH after the lead was captured, so the visitor saw an error for a submission that reached the owner. ⚠️ **ALL 4 published prod sites have empty `contact_email`** (prod D1 count) → every contact submission was erroring. Fix: email is now BEST-EFFORT — wrapped in try/catch + gated on a to-address; missing-config / provider-failure logs but returns **200**; the persisted lead + bell are the durable success. 2 regression tests; full worker suite **671 suites / 10,736 green**. Verified live end-to-end (per [[verify-against-source-of-truth]]): real POST to `mock-restaurant` (no contact_email) → `200 {success:true}` (was 400) → ground-truth query confirmed the `contacts` row landed (name/source=form/site_id, both created_at+updated_at set); test row then cleaned from prod. **contact-form ingestion flow = CONVERGED.**

Ensure all of these tests become a priority for the loop… use them to ensure Test Driven Development."* → **`apps/project-sites/e2e/FLOW_CATALOG.md`** (authored iter 63) is now the loop's authoritative backlog. **iter 68 — 🐛 search honest-error codes + 🔑 DIAGNOSED a Brian-gated outage (`8c02a1fc`).** Probed the create funnel's FIRST step: `GET /api/search/businesses` (homepage business search) returned **200 `{data:[]}` masking a Google 403** → a guest sees "no businesses found" when Places is actually down. **Root cause (Google-side, BRIAN-GATED — not code/secret-fixable): BOTH keys broken at the Google Cloud project level** — `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY`'s project has **billing DISABLED** ("You must enable Billing"), `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`'s project has the legacy API disabled + New-API 403. Both get-secret keys fail on both old+new APIs. **🔑 FIX (Brian): enable Billing + the "Places API (New)" on the key's Google Cloud project at https://console.cloud.google.com/billing** (or mint a fresh key on a billed project → `wrangler secret put GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY`). Code fix shipped: kept 200 (a 5xx fires the frontend `ApiService.handleError` rethrow → console noise; the create flow degrades to manual entry) but added STABLE `_error.code` (`SEARCH_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`/`_NOT_CONFIGURED`) so it's diagnosable + the UI can show "search unavailable, enter manually". Guard for unset key (no blind Google call). 2 tests; 23/23 search_routes green; verified live (`_error.code: SEARCH_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE | status 403`). **Follow-up ✅ DONE iter 69.**

**iter 81 — 🚨 FOUND + FIXED a CRITICAL LIVE bug: magic-link login (primary passwordless auth) was 404-broken in prod for ALL users; deployed + verified 200 live.** The "marginal-diffs-only" prediction was WRONG — probing the last unverified no-build core flow (AUTH) found the biggest bug of the arc. Probed `POST /api/auth/magic-link` live → **404** (empty body, `cfWorker;dur=651`). Root cause is a **two-bug chain, both masked**: **(1) BA-swallow (`index.ts:435`)** — the embedded Better Auth middleware `app.use('/api/auth/*')` intercepts everything not in its `legacyPaths` fall-through allowlist; `better_auth` is **globally ON in prod** (`flag_overrides` `{scope:global, enabled:true, rollout:100}`) and the frontend's sign-in calls the LEGACY `POST /api/auth/magic-link` (api.service.ts:340) + the emailed link hits `GET …/verify` — but ONLY `/magic-link/peek` was allowlisted, so the REQUEST + VERIFY were swallowed → BA's own 404 → **login totally broken**. (The peek entry's own comment documents this exact swallow; the real login endpoints were missed.) **FIX 1:** added `/api/auth/magic-link` + `/api/auth/magic-link/verify` to `legacyPaths`. That un-masked **(2) a lying-500 in the legacy handler** — `createMagicLink` (auth.ts:348) persists the `magic_links` row (the credential) then `await sendEmail()` **UNCAUGHT**, so any provider error (SES sandbox reject on the `example.com` test recipient, transient 5xx) surfaced as a **500** — despite the route's documented "email is best-effort / fail-open" contract. **FIX 2:** wrapped the send in try/catch (log + fall open) so a mail error never 500s login. **TDD:** new `better_auth_passthrough.test.ts` (structural — asserts the allowlist keeps the login paths + other legacy passthroughs) + rewrote `auth.test.ts` "throws on email failure" → "is BEST-EFFORT, resolves" (the corrected contract). 36/36 auth+passthrough · tsc 0 · eslint 0 · worker deployed (Versions `ad57f787`→`394b1a2e`). **VERIFIED LIVE end-to-end:** `POST /api/auth/magic-link` → **200 `{expires_at}`** (was 404→500) → `magic_links` row created (credential persisted) → cleaned up. **⚠️ Follow-on (Brian-gated / separate): email DELIVERABILITY** — email IS configured (SES + Resend + SendGrid secrets present) but SES may be in SANDBOX (only verified recipients) → real users might still not RECEIVE the link even though the flow now 200s (best-effort logs `magic_link_email_send_failed`). Verify SES is out of sandbox / send a real link to a verified address. **Lesson: a cutover flag flipped globally ON silently shadows every legacy endpoint not in the fall-through allowlist → probe auth flows after any auth-provider cutover** ([[admin-mutations-need-causal-probes]] sibling). **NEXT: sweep the OTHER legacy `/api/auth/*` endpoints the frontend/emails use for the same swallow (they should be in the allowlist): `/api/auth/magic-link` ✅ + `/verify` ✅ now; double-check `/api/auth/logout`, any password-reset, any `/api/auth/*` the Angular ApiService calls — grep `frontend .../api/auth`. Then contact #36 / build #1 stay Brian-gated on SITE_BUILDER.****🔎 BUILD-ENGINE re-enable — de-risking intel (iter 68 CF-API read, read-only/safe):** the deployed `project-sites` worker has **`migration_tag: None` + ZERO DO/container bindings**; `SiteBuilderContainer` is **NOT** a registered DO namespace (13 orphan namespaces from prior deploys persist: ActivityHub/TraceHub/ConversationHub + *Container — harmless). So re-adding `SiteBuilderContainer` is a **new-class** path (NOT a 10074 "already-exists" conflict). Recipe (still a DELIBERATE turn — DO migration is a hard-to-undo one-way door + needs a paid build): uncomment the `[[env.production.containers]]` block (~296-301) + add `[[env.production.durable_objects.bindings]]` `name=SITE_BUILDER class_name=SiteBuilderContainer` + `[[env.production.migrations]]` `tag="v1_sitebuilder" new_classes=["SiteBuilderContainer"]` (classic backend per the "stays classic" note) → Docker deploy (a FAILED deploy leaves prod intact) → real `/create`→build <15 min (buildPrompt is already lean/template-first, iter 66).

**iter 83 — ✅ FIXED the editor "Logs" tab (real broken/lying surface in the bolt.diy app, `app/`) — it now shows real deploy logs; was 401-dead in prod + wrong-shaped in code.** Took the durable state's own "Loop-safe alternative … editor-endpoint contracts" cue. The bottom **Logs** tab (`LogsTab.tsx`) POLLS `GET /api/bolt-tabs/logs?project={p}` every 5s expecting `{lines: LogLine[]}` + a `since` cursor. The endpoint (`app/routes/api.bolt-tabs.logs.ts`) had TWO bugs: (1) returned `{tails:[...]}` (a CF tail-SESSION list the client never reads — response-key mismatch, [[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]]) AND (2) **prod returned HTTP 401 `missing_credentials`** because `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN`/`ACCOUNT_ID` were UNSET on the bolt-diy Pages env → the tab had NEVER worked. **Root cause of the design mismatch:** CF Workers **Tail is WebSocket-only** (un-pollable, no HTTP line buffer) — the poll-for-`{lines}` client can't be fed by a tail-session endpoint. **FIX:** rewrote the loader to query **Workers Observability** (`/workers/observability/telemetry/query`, which DOES return recent structured events over HTTP) filtered by `$metadata.service = project`, mapping each event → `LogLine` (http_request events → `GET /path → 200 (68ms)`; else `msg`/`message`/`eventName`/JSON). Honors `since` (window starts +1ms to avoid boundary dupes), sorts oldest→newest for the client's append+cursor. **Pure core extracted + TDD:** `mapEventToLogLine`/`buildObservabilityQuery`/`computeWindow`/`sortLinesAscending`/`toLogLevel` (15 vitest, all green). **Provisioned** the bolt-diy Pages env with a **least-privilege `Workers Observability Read` token** (minted via CF API, permission-group `66c1ed49…`, verified working) + `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`. root tsc 0 · vitest 15/15 · committed `187522e7` · **Pages deploy `62594182` (bolt-diy, --branch=main)**. **VERIFIED LIVE:** `GET https://editor.projectsites.dev/api/bolt-tabs/logs?project=project-sites` → **200** (was 401) `{project, lines:[200 real events]}` — correct `{lines}` shape, `GET /chunk-… → 200 (68ms)` messages, `[info]` levels, source `project-sites`, ascending w/ valid cursor. Editor Logs tab now shows real logs. **⚠️ Deploy note:** this is the ROOT `app/` (bolt.diy Remix) → Pages `bolt-diy` (NOT the worker); build `npm run build` → `wrangler pages deploy build/client --project-name=bolt-diy --branch=main` w/ GLOBAL key (unset token). See [[bolt-diy-pages-deploy-recipe]]. **Minor follow-on (cosmetic, non-blocking):** the observability dataset emits both a raw CF request event AND the app's structured `http_request` log for the same request → each request shows ~twice in the tab; could filter to app-scope-only later, but it's honest + functional. Top tabs are now the clean bolt.diy `Code | Preview`. root tsc **0** (proves no dangling refs — nothing else referenced the `'functions'`/`'data'` view values), no tests referenced them. Committed `e41deccd`+`9530953f` · **Pages deploy `1cdc4a41` (bolt-diy)**. **VERIFIED LIVE:** prod-served content-addressed chunk `workbench-CUPfALcP.js` → **HTTP 200** with **0 `bricklabor`** occurrences (that marker was UNIQUE to the two deleted panels → its absence proves they're gone from prod). Editor Functions/Data mockups are dead. **⚠️ git gotcha (broke origin/main for ~seconds):** after `git rm A B`, a `git add C D A` (re-listing the already-rm'd A) **aborts the WHOLE `git add`** on the pathspec error → the modified files C/D DON'T stage → the deletion commits WITHOUT the code that removed the imports → origin/main briefly imports deleted files. FIX: stage deletions via `git rm` and modifications via `git add <modified-only>` — NEVER re-list an rm'd path in the follow-up `git add`. (Refined the "stage EXPLICIT paths" rule.)

**iter 90 — ✅ a REVERT-CHECK of the honest-degradation arc surfaced a LIVE P0 (create-funnel search DEAD in prod) + fixed an RDAP cache-poisoning bug it exposed.** Per [[stale-deploy-reverts-verified-fix]], curled the 4 shipped honest-degradation surfaces to confirm no revert — and the contract WORKING is what surfaced the outage: `/api/search/businesses` + `/api/search/address` both return `_error:{SEARCH_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE, 403 PERMISSION_DENIED}` **right now** → the create-funnel's FIRST step (search your business) is dead in prod. **Root cause DEFINITIVELY isolated** (tested the key directly against Google): legacy Places API returns verbatim *"You must enable Billing on the Google Cloud Project"* → the GCP project behind `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY` has **billing DISABLED**, cascading to the new API's 403. **Brian-gated** (billing + payment method — approval-tier; `https://console.cloud.google.com/billing`). The arc pays off: all 3 FE consumers (homepage iter-86, search, create iter-87) surface the honest nudge + both endpoints emit `_error` → the funnel fails GRACEFULLY, not catastrophically (a lying-empty "your business isn't findable"). **Second finding — RDAP:** the same curl showed `/api/domains/search` all-`unknown`; a FRESH uncached domain proved RDAP actually works **8/10 TLDs** — the all-unknown was **POISONED CACHE** (`unknown`/error cached at the SAME 3600s TTL as definitive answers → a momentary registry/egress throttle stuck a domain as "? couldn't check" for a full hour). **FIX (`rdap_availability.ts`): split cache TTL** — definitive `available`/`taken` keep 1h; non-definitive `unknown` caches only 60s so a transient failure self-heals in a minute. **+3 jest** (short-TTL for rate-limit + network-throw; long-TTL for definitive) · worker tsc 0 · rdap 28/28 · committed `8a4db236` · **worker deploy `e180fb5a` (Docker + GLOBAL key)**. **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200; fresh domains/search 6/10 resolved (`_error=False`, per-item `status` intact, NO regression — the *which*-TLDs-unknown set rotates probe-to-probe, confirming the intermittent shared-egress throttle the split-TTL targets); Google Places `_error` still honest. **LESSON: a graceful honest-degradation contract makes a provider outage INVISIBLE-but-graceful — a periodic revert-check must probe the UNDERLYING provider health (curl the real endpoint), not just assert the nudge renders. The contract that hides the outage from users also hides it from monitoring.** ([[verify-against-source-of-truth]] sibling.)

**iter 91 — ✅ hardened the RDAP domain-availability flow (retry + PROVEN the root cause via new observability) — continuing iter-90's RDAP thread.** OBSERVE: the domain-picker (real owner-facing flow, iter-89) resolved only ~0-8/10 TLDs; the *which*-TLDs-fail set rotates probe-to-probe. Diagnosed in layers: (1) rdap.org from a CLEAN laptop IP is per-TLD-variable NOW — `.com` 3s, `.io`/`.xyz`/`.ai` >8s timeouts — so the Worker's 5s `AbortSignal` aborts slow-but-working registries → `unknown`. (2) But the Worker got **0/10** including laptop-FAST TLDs → deeper than timeout. (3) **The status fall-through in `probeOnce` was a SILENT `unknown`** (no log) — a `structured-logging` gap. Added a `console.warn` capturing `res.status`, redeployed, fired probes → **Workers-Observability PROVED it: rdap.org returns `429` ×8 + `403` ×1 to the Worker** — its Cloudflare edge is **rate-limiting the shared CF Worker egress IP** (a Worker→CF subrequest), NOT timing out. **FIXES (3 commits, `rdap_availability.ts`):** (a) `8a4db236`-follow-on `f0f8d179` — extract `probeOnce` + retry a transient `unknown` once; (b) `4203950e` — log the actual non-ok status (self-diagnosing, per `structured-logging` — this is what proved the 429); (c) `363227eb` — **gate the retry to TIMEOUT/network only** (`probeOnce` returns `{result, retryable}`; retryable=true ONLY for the catch path) — retrying a 429 hammers the rate-limit, so a status-refusal is `unknown`-but-NOT-retried. **+6 jest (34 total)** · worker tsc 0 · domain_search 13/13 clean · **worker deploys `e180fb5a`→…→`02ad58f5`**. **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200; a fresh probe post-fix resolved **9/10** (rdap.org's rate-limit burst had passed — confirming intermittency), per-item `status` intact, `_error` correct (only when all-unknown), NO regression. **Net: the picker now handles BOTH rdap.org modes correctly — good periods (retry recovers timeout-variance → 9/10), bad periods (429-gated so we don't deepen the limit + honest "couldn't check" iter-89 + 60s self-heal iter-90).** **RDAP is an ACCEPTABLE HONEST FLOOR now (self-healing, self-diagnosing, non-hammering).** ⚠️ **RDAP next-target (nice-to-have, NOT urgent):** the durable fix for bad-window 0/10 is a source NOT subject to rdap.org's CF-egress rate-limit — either **direct-to-registry RDAP** (hardcode the picker's 10-TLD→registry-base map from the IANA bootstrap `data.iana.org/rdap/dns.json`, bypassing the throttled aggregator; risk: registries MIGHT also rate-limit CF egress, unverifiable without deploy) or a **CF-native domain-availability API** if one exists (per `cloudflare-lock-in-is-leverage`). Deferred — the current honest floor is fine + the fix is uncertain.
