**iter 93 — ✅ closed the `dbInsert` lying-success class on the user-facing route layer (12 bare writes wrapped; 68→56 bare calls, 0 HIGH).** OBSERVE (per [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]] + the shipped `scripts/check-unchecked-dbinsert.mjs` detector): `dbInsert` returns `{ error }` and does NOT throw — a bare `await dbInsert(...)` silently DROPS the row on a genuine DB failure while the handler returns a lying `200/201`. The detector listed 68 bare calls (0 HIGH ingestion — donations/newsletter/leads already fixed iter-ingestion). **SLICE — the coherent "user-facing create/save route handlers surface persistence failures" theme:** wrapped every bare write in the ROUTE layer where a user action expects persistence, with `const { error } = await dbInsert(...); if (error) throw internalError(...)` so a real failure reaches the caller + Sentry (via `error_handler`→`AppError` 500) instead of faking success. **12 sites / 6 files:** `forms.ts` (newsletter_integrations), `experiments.ts` (experiments + variants), `agents.ts` (agents + agent_runs), `templates.ts` (template_installs + templates), `voice.ts` (voice_agent_settings ×2 — file already had precedent at :242), `agency.ts` (agency_invitations + orgs + membership — tenancy-critical, import already present). Added `internalError` to the shared import of agents/templates/voice/experiments (agency already had it). worker **tsc 0** · **149 route jest green** (agents/templates/voice_mcp_attachments/voice_agent_config/agency_routes/forms_routes/experiments — these exercise the REAL handlers in-process via the Hono app, so the happy-path 201s ARE the behavioral proof the wraps don't break creates) · detector re-run **68→56** (−12, 0 HIGH) · committed `0c04e9a8` · **worker deploy `c136a2a9` (Docker + GLOBAL key)** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 (worker serving new version); the touched authed POSTs curl-probe to CF managed-challenge 403 (Bot-Fight on headless curl — the request never reaches the worker, NOT a handler regression; real browsers pass). Change is purely additive (error===null → no throw → happy path identical). **⚠️ dbInsert NEXT-TARGET (next iteration — this class is NOT closed, only the route-layer user-facing slice is):** 56 bare calls remain, all `low`-tagged but some genuinely high-harm: (1) **`api.ts`** (6 sites — feedback/github_backup_states/github_integrations/spend_alerts + 2 IDENTICAL `site_urls` backfill-on-read that must **log-and-continue NOT throw** — a GET must not 500 on a backfill miss; api.ts is a 10k-line HOT shared file → stage carefully); (2) **`services/wallet.ts`** (4 — wallet_accounts + wallet_transactions ×3: a dropped money-movement row is a FINANCIAL lying-success — arguably the highest-harm remaining despite the `low` tag; the detector's HIGH is ingestion-only so it under-ranks financial writes); (3) `dashboard.ts` (calendar ×4), `super_admin.ts` (refunds/broadcasts/announcements/impersonation ×4), `agency.ts` already done; (4) `src/auth/better-auth.ts` (users/orgs/memberships ×3 — SENSITIVE auth, possibly dark behind `BETTER_AUTH_*`; review carefully, don't rush). Services/* (auth.ts sessions/oauth_states, billing.ts, etc.) are a separate class — some intentionally fire-and-forget; convert per-site with judgment, NOT blind. **Detector stays the enforcement** — every remaining site is a known, tracked, `check-unchecked-dbinsert.mjs`-listed target.

**iter 94 — ✅ fixed the HIGHEST-HARM dbInsert lying-success (financial): `services/wallet.ts` — balance-mutation now COMPENSATES on ledger-write failure, not just error-checks.** SELECT (per iter-93's own next-target #2): wallet is the money surface — the detector under-ranks it `low` (its HIGH tag is ingestion-only). OBSERVE found the class is WORSE here than a plain create-write: each of `chargeWallet` (debit), `creditWallet`, `manualAdjustment` **UPDATEs `wallet_accounts.balance_cents` FIRST, then a bare `dbInsert` writes the `wallet_transactions` ledger row.** A silent ledger drop ⇒ **money MOVED with NO ledger record** — and worse: a debit failure leaves the user *charged*, so a retry **double-charges**; a credit failure leaves the balance up but the `stripe_event_id` dup-guard row absent ⇒ a webhook replay **double-credits**. So a bare `{error}`+throw is INSUFFICIENT (throwing after the balance moved still leaves balance↔ledger divergence). **SLICE — compensation (saga), not just error-surfacing:** new `reverseBalance(env, walletId, deltaCents, ctx)` helper (`UPDATE balance_cents + ? WHERE id`, try/catch→error-log, never swallowed); every ledger insert now `const {error}=…; if(error){ await reverseBalance(…signed reversal…); throw internalError(…) }` — debit reverses `+amount`, credit/adjustment reverse `-amount` — so the balance is restored and retries are safe + idempotent. Also wrapped the 4th bare call (`ensureWalletRow`'s `wallet_accounts` insert — already guarded by a read-after-write `throw`, but now throws the REAL insert error immediately + clears the detector). **TDD:** wrote 3 failure-compensation tests FIRST, **watched RED** (current code resolved `{ok:true}` despite `dbInsert→{error:'ledger down'}` — the exact lying-success), then implemented → **GREEN**. worker **tsc 0** · **97 wallet jest green** (3 new RED→GREEN + 94 existing across wallet/wallet_purchase/wallet_routes/wallet_webhook/wallet_adapter — the existing happy-path suite proves the additive change didn't regress the money math) · detector **56→52** (all 4 wallet.ts inserts cleared, `wallet.ts` count now 0) · committed `a8e48ed0` · **worker deploy `bde854bf` (Docker + GLOBAL key)** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 + `/api/health` `{status:ok, kv ok, r2 ok}` 87ms. Wallet fns are internal (billing/webhook/super-admin callers) so no direct HTTP probe — the 97 in-process tests (real `chargeWallet`/`creditWallet`/`manualAdjustment` with the compensation path) ARE the behavioral proof; happy path is byte-identical (error===null → no reversal). **LESSON (folds into [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]]): for a write that mutates OTHER state before the ledger insert (balance, counter, status), the lying-success fix is COMPENSATION (reverse the prior mutation) — a bare throw leaves the two stores diverged. The detector's severity tag is a heuristic; a `low`-tagged financial write can be higher-harm than a `HIGH`-tagged ingestion one — rank by blast-radius, not the tag.** **⚠️ dbInsert NEXT-TARGET (unchanged, minus wallet):** 52 remain — `api.ts` (6; ⚠️ 2 `site_urls` backfill-on-read must **log-not-throw**; HOT 10k-line shared file), `dashboard.ts` (calendar ×4), `super_admin.ts` (refunds/broadcasts/announcements/impersonation ×4), `better-auth.ts` (users/orgs/memberships ×3 — SENSITIVE, possibly dark). Apply the iter-94 lesson: any of these that mutate prior state before the insert need compensation, not a bare throw. Services/* (auth sessions/oauth_states, billing) = separate class, some fire-and-forget → per-site judgment.

**iter 95 — ✅ closed `super_admin.ts` dbInsert lying-success (4 writes) — and surfaced a THIRD fix-pattern beyond throw/compensate.** SELECT: super-admin is the next-highest-harm remaining (financial refunds + security-audit impersonation). OBSERVE classified all 4 by harm + prior-state: (1) **refunds** — a REAL Stripe refund is issued (`POST /v1/refunds`) BEFORE the D1 insert, so money is ALREADY out by the time the ledger writes; compensation is impossible (can't un-refund) and Stripe's `Idempotency-Key: id` already blocks a double-refund on retry ⇒ the correct fix is a **THIRD pattern: capture-recoverable-trace + throw** — on insert failure write a `refund_record_failed` audit preserving `stripe_refund_id`, then throw (never a lying 201 hiding an un-recorded refund). (2) **impersonation_sessions** — the row IS the security audit trail, and a token binding `sid:id` was minted AFTER the insert ⇒ a silent drop = a super-admin impersonating a user with a valid token but **ZERO audit record** (untraceable impersonation). Fix throws BEFORE the token mint → un-auditable impersonation is BLOCKED. (3+4) **broadcasts + announcements** — plain create-writes → `{error}`+throw. **TDD:** wrote 4 failure tests FIRST (refund→500 + asserts the `refund_record_failed` audit preserved the id via the D1-stub `_stmt.bind` calls; impersonate→500 + NO token even with the JWT secret SET; broadcast/announcement→500), **watched RED** (impersonate returned lying 200 with a token), implemented → **GREEN**. ⚠️ Also had to flip the test harness default `mockDbInsert.mockResolvedValue(undefined)` → `{ error: null }` — otherwise destructuring `{error}` on `undefined` throws + breaks every existing happy test (a required, behavior-neutral harness fix any dbInsert-wrap in a `jest.fn()`-mocked suite needs). worker **tsc 0** · **65 super_admin jest green** (4 new + 61 existing/gate) · detector **52→48** (all 4 super_admin.ts inserts cleared) · committed `cc8d615c` · **worker deploy `9ecb2d40` (Docker + GLOBAL key)** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 + `/api/super-admin/refunds` (no auth) → **401** (router mounted, gate intact, the `internalError` import didn't break module load). **LESSON (folds into [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]] — now THREE patterns): a lying-success fix is picked by what happened BEFORE the insert — (a) nothing → plain throw; (b) reversible local state (balance/counter) → COMPENSATE then throw (iter-94); (c) IRREVERSIBLE external effect already committed (Stripe refund, sent email, minted token) → capture the recoverable identifier in a durable trace (audit) + throw, and for effects gated ON the insert (token mint), throw BEFORE the effect so it never fires un-recorded.** **⚠️ dbInsert NEXT-TARGET:** 48 remain — `api.ts` (6; ⚠️ 2 `site_urls` backfill-on-read must **log-not-throw** — a GET must not 500 on a backfill miss; HOT 10k-line shared file → stage explicit), `dashboard.ts` (calendar ×4), `better-auth.ts` (users/orgs/memberships ×3 — SENSITIVE, possibly dark behind `BETTER_AUTH_*`; classify each by the 3 patterns first). Services/* (auth sessions/oauth_states, billing) still a per-site-judgment class. ⚠️ Consider PIVOTING after 1-2 more dbInsert files — 3 consecutive iters (93/94/95) on this class; per [[loop-arc-economics]] watch for diminishing returns, though each file so far has been genuinely distinct high-harm (route/financial/security).

**browser-E2E pass (2026-08-16) — ran the 5 MASTER chaos journeys + an authed admin INTERACTION sweep vs prod; NO new meaningful bug; advanced CHAOS-4 from render-only → interaction.** Ran `chaos-{1..5}-*.e2e.ts` vs prod via `playwright.prod.config.ts` (local Chromium, `E2E_API_KEY` seeded): **76/76 passed** (0 console.error, 0 console.warn, 0 5xx, 0 pageerror, 0 XSS across marketing + create funnel + auth + all 16 admin sections + resilience). Then a scratch authed interaction probe clicked every SAFE button across `/admin` + `/admin/sites` + `/admin/settings` + `/admin/seo` (~46 clicks: settings tabs, pins, actions, filters) → **clean** (the only `requestfailed` were benign `ERR_ABORTED` nav-races on `/cdn-cgi/rum` + `/api/auth/me` + lazy chunks, not app defects). The E2E account has a REAL populated site ("Urban Fitness Co", `urban-fitness.projectsites.dev`, draft) so admin renders real data, not empty. **ADVANCEMENT (committed `d7520a61`):** folded a permanent CHAOS-4 test that switches all **9 settings lazy sub-view tabs** (Team/AI Chat/MCP/AI Env Vars/Webhooks/Email/Domains/API Tokens/General — each a distinct lazy chunk + data fetch a render-only sweep never enters) and asserts zero app-controlled console.error/warn + pageerror + 5xx + missing-asset; **verified 9/9 clean in isolation (21.9s)**. Test-only (E2E not deployed) → no worker/R2 deploy. ⚠️ **Observed (NOT a product bug, test-infra):** the full parallel prod run leaves a Playwright worker hanging on teardown (`worker did not exit within 300000ms after stop, force-killed`) — a lingering prod keepalive/SW connection; tests PASS, only process-exit hangs (slows the CI `prod-e2e-frontend` job). Isolated single-test runs exit clean. **⚠️ NEXT E2E TARGET — the deepest unverified layer is DATA-RECONCILIATION (display-vs-store), which render+interaction sweeps STRUCTURALLY cannot catch** (a section can render clean + error-free while showing WRONG/empty data — [[verify-against-source-of-truth]] + [[response-key-mismatch-lying-empty]] + [[stale-route-fake-empty]]). The reconciler already exists: `e2e/admin-verify/reconcile-surfaces.mjs` (D1 ground-truth COUNT per concept vs the authed UI's endpoint). A future pass should run it against the real E2E account + causal probes (create→read→delete) for the mutation surfaces. Also unverified: true PERSISTENCE (mutate → navigate → hard-refresh → verify) — blocked from the create→build path (SITE_BUILDER CF-10074 off, see below) but testable on safe reversible admin prefs.

**iter 96 — ✅ `dashboard.ts` calendar dbInsert (4 writes) — and it was NOT mechanical: found a REAL behavioral bug (booking slug-collision = lying 201, 409 path DEAD).** SELECT: 4th dbInsert file but distinct (calendar CRUD, live/auth-only, NOT flag-gated). OBSERVE classified the 4: (1) `POST /api/calendar/events` bootstraps a default "Personal" calendar (282) when the user has none, THEN inserts the event (293) referencing it — 282 is a PREREQUISITE, so a silent 282 failure must throw BEFORE the event insert (else an FK-orphan event / self-failure); (2) event (293) + (3) calendar-create (403) are plain create-writes. (4) **`POST /api/calendar/bookings` (480) was the BUG:** it wrapped `dbInsert` in a `try/catch` expecting a THROWN UNIQUE error → 409. But `dbInsert`→`dbExecute` CATCHES + returns `{error}` and **never throws** (confirmed `db.ts:131` dbExecute try/catch), so the 409-CONFLICT path was **DEAD CODE** and a duplicate slug returned a **lying 201** with a `public_url` for a booking that was never created. Fixed by keying the 409 off the returned `{error}` (`/UNIQUE/i.test`) + throw on any other error; **DELETED the dead try/catch**. **Test-reality mismatch that HID it:** the two existing booking tests used `mockDbInsert.mockRejectedValueOnce(...)` — i.e. they mocked dbInsert to THROW, a path prod can never hit — so they went green against the dead catch. Made both realistic (`mockResolvedValueOnce({error})`) → they went RED (lying 201) against current code, GREEN after the fix; + added 3 sibling failure tests (event/calendar/bootstrap → 500, bootstrap asserts `dbInsert` called ONCE so no orphan event). Flipped the harness default `mockDbInsert.mockResolvedValue(undefined)`→`{error:null}` (same destructure-safety fix as iter-95). worker **tsc 0** · **37 dashboard jest green** (5 RED→GREEN + 32) · detector **48→44** (all 4 dashboard.ts inserts cleared) · committed `9ed6700e` · **worker deploy `766aebbd` (Docker + GLOBAL key)** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 + `GET /api/calendar/events` (no auth) → **401** (router mounted, guard intact, `internalError` import didn't break load). **LESSON: a `try/catch` around a helper that returns `{error}` instead of throwing is DEAD — it silently converts every real failure into a lying success. When wrapping any `dbInsert`/`dbExecute` caller, GREP the surrounding handler for a `catch` that expects a thrown DB error (UNIQUE/constraint) — that catch is dead + must be rewritten to key off the returned `{error}`. And a test that mocks the helper to THROW is testing an impossible path — mock it to RETURN `{error}` (matches real behavior).** Detector for a future arc: flag `try { … dbInsert/dbExecute … } catch` blocks that test `/UNIQUE|constraint/i` on the caught error. **⚠️ dbInsert NEXT-TARGET (44 remain) — but STRONG PIVOT recommendation:** 4 consecutive iters (93/94/95/96) on this class; per [[loop-arc-economics]] the remaining tail (`api.ts` 6 incl. the log-not-throw `site_urls` pair; `better-auth.ts` 3 sensitive/possibly-dark; services/* fire-and-forget) is lower-harm + higher-friction (hot file / sensitive auth / intentional fire-and-forget). **Next iter should PIVOT** to either (a) the DATA-RECONCILIATION E2E target above (reconcile-surfaces.mjs — finds wrong-data/lying-empty, a DIFFERENT + high-value class), or (b) a fresh code surface. The `try/catch-over-{error}-helper` detector is a cheaper, higher-yield dbInsert-adjacent follow-up than grinding the remaining bare-insert tail.

**iter 97 — ✅ PIVOTED to the `dbUpdate` lying-success class (fresh sibling): shipped its detector + fixed the highest-harm slice — another DOCUMENTED-but-DEAD 404 (calendar edit/delete).** SELECT (honoring iter-96's pivot rec — NOT a 5th dbInsert file): `dbUpdate(db,table,updates,where,params)` returns `{ error, changes }` and (via dbExecute) NEVER throws — an unswept class with TWO silent-failure modes: `error!=null` (edit not applied → lying 200) AND **`changes===0`** (WHERE matched NO row → nothing updated, still 200 — AND it masks an authz gap: editing another org/user's row silently no-ops but "succeeds", IDOR-adjacent). **Built `scripts/check-unchecked-dbupdate.mjs`** (mirrors check-unchecked-dbinsert; HIGH = bare dbUpdate in a `routes/`+`handlers.ts` user-facing edit): **67 bare callers, 21 HIGH.** **SLICE — the clearest HIGH (completes iter-96's calendar CRUD correctness):** `PATCH` + `DELETE /api/calendar/events/:id` (`dashboard.ts` 339/353) each ran a bare `await dbUpdate(…'id=? AND user_id=?')` then returned `{data:{id}}` WITHOUT checking `changes` — so editing/deleting a non-existent OR another user's event matched 0 rows yet returned a **lying 200**, and the **JSDoc-documented `@throws 404 NOT_FOUND when the event id doesn't belong to the caller` NEVER FIRED** (same shape as iter-96's booking: documented behavior dead because the helper's result is ignored). Fixed: `const {error,changes}=…; if(error) throw internalError; if(changes===0) return 404`. **TDD:** +3 failure tests (PATCH→404 on no-match, PATCH→500 on error, DELETE→404 on no-match), **watched RED** (all lying 200), GREEN after fix; flipped the harness default `mockDbUpdate.mockResolvedValue(undefined)`→`{error:null,changes:1}` (destructure-safety, same as iter-95/96). Detector `check:dbupdate` added to package.json **report-only — NOT wired into the `check` aggregator/CI** (19 HIGH remain; per [[audit-arc-maturity-ladder]] promote to a `--ci` gate only when HIGH→0). worker **tsc 0** · **37 dashboard jest green** (3 new + 34) · dbUpdate detector **67→65** (both dashboard.ts calendar_events cleared) · committed `56272459` · **worker deploy `0f5c9816` (Docker + GLOBAL key)** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 + `GET /api/calendar/events` (no auth) → **401** (redeployed dashboard router loads clean). **LESSON (4th dbUpdate dimension for [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]]): for `dbUpdate` the lying-success has a SECOND axis beyond `error` — `changes===0`. An edit-by-id handler (PATCH/PUT/soft-DELETE scoped `WHERE id=? AND {org|user}=?`) MUST `return 404` on `changes===0`, else it lies "saved" AND silently no-ops the ownership check (an IDOR mask). Grep signal: a bare `await dbUpdate(...)` in a `routes/` file whose WHERE includes `user_id`/`org_id` + a `@throws 404` JSDoc = a dead-404.** **⚠️ dbUpdate NEXT-TARGET (65 remain, 19 HIGH) — the map is `check:dbupdate`:** highest-harm HIGH clusters = `apps.ts` (5 app_instance edits), `agents.ts` (2 agent PATCH), `agency.ts` (3 org/invite), `voice.ts` (3), `experiments.ts` (1), `super_admin.ts` (1 cost_categories); `voice_webhooks.ts` (4) are WEBHOOK status-updates (idempotent, changes:0 likely OK → lower priority, verify before 404-ing). Next iter: fix one HIGH route file's edits (apps.ts is the biggest cluster) applying the changes===0→404 rule; when HIGH→0, promote `check:dbupdate:ci` into the `check` aggregator + `feature-architecture.yml` (next to check:dbinsert).

**browser-E2E pass (2026-08-16 #2) — the PERSISTENCE layer finally paid off: a browser mutate→refresh probe FOUND + fixed a real create-bug, and CHAOS-4 gained the M2 round-trip it was missing.** SELECT: prior E2E passes proved render+interaction clean; the untested layer was PERSISTENCE (mutate → nav → hard-reload → verify) — no chaos test did it. Picked **AI Env Vars** as the safest reversible UI-backed CRUD (org-scoped config, namespaced test key, API-deletable). OBSERVE probe (seed auth → settings → AI Env Vars → Add → fill key+value → submit) surfaced a **REAL BUG immediately: the create POST returned 400** even with the submit button ENABLED. Root-caused via an in-page authed fetch (bypasses CF Bot-Fight): `description:null` → **400 `VALIDATION_ERROR: description: Expected string, received null`**; omitted/`""` → 200. The `env-vars-manager` form sends `description: draft.description || null` (→ null when empty), but `POST /api/env-vars` used `description: z.string().optional()` which REJECTS null — so **every env-var create with an empty description (the common case) 400'd**. The PATCH schema already used `.nullable().optional()`; the POST schema didn't (an inconsistency no test caught — the existing create tests all OMIT description). **FIX (`routes/env_vars.ts`): `.nullable().optional()` + coerce `body.description ?? undefined` at the setEnvVar boundary** (DB column nullable; tsc caught the `string|null` vs `string|undefined` mismatch). **TDD:** +1 RED→GREEN test (`description:null` → 200); worker **tsc 0** · **47 env_vars jest green** · committed `afd179bc` · **worker deploy `8963c3ae`** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** re-ran the probe → create now **200**, appears, **persists across nav-away+back AND hard reload**, 0 console.err/warn/5xx; also verified `DELETE /api/env-vars/:id` → 200 (delete works; the probe's earlier "not gone" was an unconfirmed ConfirmService dialog) + cleaned up all 3 stray `E2E_*` test vars. **ADVANCEMENT (committed `4724360b`):** folded a permanent **CHAOS-4 M2 persistence round-trip** — create an org env var via the UI (asserts 200, regression-guards the null-desc fix) → nav away + back → HARD RELOAD → assert the row persists each time → API-DELETE cleanup in `finally`; **verified 2/2 green vs prod (34.2s)**. **LESSON: the persistence layer is where enabled-button→rejected-submit bugs hide — a render/interaction sweep clicks the button but never checks the POST STATUS or whether the mutation PERSISTED. A mutate→refresh probe on ONE safe CRUD found a 400 that ~5 prior clean E2E passes missed. Also: an "optional" field that a form sends as `null` needs `.nullable()`, not just `.optional()` — grep POST Zod schemas for `z.string().optional()` fields whose FE sends `x || null`.** **⚠️ NEXT E2E TARGET:** apply the same mutate→persist probe to the OTHER safe CRUDs (calendar events via the widget — verifies iter-96/97 fixes end-to-end; media upload; api-tokens create) — each may hide a similar create/edit-payload mismatch. Data-reconciliation (reconcile-surfaces.mjs, brian's account + Browserbase) still the deepest unrun layer.

**iter 98 — ✅ ran the null-reject HUNT (clean negative), then fixed the dbUpdate error-surfacing slice on load-then-update handlers — and CORRECTED the detector's default advice.** First OBSERVE = the memory-directed hunt for MORE instances of the env-var `.optional()`-rejects-null class: grepped the FE for fields sent as `x || null` / `?? null` (~11), cross-checked each against its worker schema. **Clean negative — the env-var bug was ISOLATED:** the others are STRIPPED (field not in the schema → non-strict Zod drops it, e.g. voice `conversation_id` → `testSmsBody` only has siteId+body), OMITTED when empty (share-link `password` — FE does `if (pw) body.password = pw`), or field-name mismatches (FE `quality` vs schema `content_quality`). **Key rule learned: the null-reject bug fires ONLY when the field is DEFINED in the schema as a non-nullable `.optional()` AND the client sends it as `null`** — a bare extra null field is harmless (stripped). Then SELECT the dbUpdate slice. **Major OBSERVE correction:** iter-97's next-target (`apps.ts`) + `agents.ts` + `experiments.ts` are ALL "load-and-validate-ownership-first (throws 404) → then `dbUpdate` by id" handlers — so **`changes===0` is PRE-GUARDED (unreachable), and the detector's default `404 on changes===0` advice DOES NOT APPLY.** The correct fix for load-then-update handlers is pure error-surfacing (a genuine D1 write failure → lying `{ok:true}`). **SLICE — the clean uniform ones (request-path, has test harness):** `agents.ts` PATCH (157) + DELETE (248), `experiments.ts` promote (322) → `const {error}=await dbUpdate(...); if(error) throw internalError`. (Skipped `apps.ts` this iter — its 5 updates are nuanced: 4 are post-container-op status-flips, 2 in BACKGROUND `waitUntil`/IIFE where you can't throw → they need capture+structured-warn, not throw; the 1 user-data write is the env-override at :523. A distinct, non-uniform follow-up.) **TDD:** +3 RED→GREEN (PATCH/DELETE agent + promote experiment with `dbUpdate→{error}` → 500 not lying `{ok:true}`), watched RED; both files already import `internalError` (iter-93). worker **tsc 0** · **31 agents+experiments jest green** · dbUpdate detector **65→62** (agents.ts + experiments.ts cleared, HIGH 19→16) · committed `c67a79c2` · **worker deploy `425c724d`** · **VERIFIED LIVE:** health 200 + homepage 200 + `GET /api/sites/x/agents` (no auth) → **402** (Pro-gated guard fires — router loaded clean). **LESSON: `changes===0`→404 is ONLY correct when the `dbUpdate` WHERE is the SOLE ownership guard (like calendar iter-97, no pre-load). When a handler `loadX()`-validates + throws 404 FIRST, `changes===0` is an unreachable race → the fix is error-surfacing ONLY. Classify each dbUpdate by "is ownership pre-guarded upstream?" before applying the detector's blanket 404 advice.** **⚠️ dbUpdate NEXT-TARGET (62 remain, 16 HIGH):** `apps.ts` (5 — the nuanced status-flip/background cluster: capture+warn for the 4 status writes, throw for the :523 env-override user-data write), `agency.ts` (3), `voice.ts` (3), `super_admin.ts` (1 cost_categories — CHECK if it's a direct edit-by-id = changes===0→404, or pre-guarded). Promote `check:dbupdate:ci` into the `check` aggregator when HIGH→0.

**iter 115 — ✅ fixed a LIVE custom-domain bug: `verifyPendingHostnames` stranded pending hostnames at `verification_failed` forever (premature-fail + no-retry).** Executed iter-114's NEXT (fresh-subsystem audit) across `domains`/`weekly_digest`/`churn_prediction` + re-verified the P0.54 SELECT-omission class. **Ruled out:** `churn_prediction` is unwired (0 callers) but a built-ahead compute LIBRARY (`computeChurnRisk`/`classifyRiskLevel`) whose "nightly cron" is a big feature — deferred, NOT a quick-wire like iter-114's `applyExpiredDefaults` (keep, per [[dead-code-scan-baseline]]); `weekly_digest` IS wired (`index.ts:1890` cron + a route); the **P0.54 settings-General SELECT-omission is CLEAN** — all 17 response `row.X` reads (chat_persona…drive_last_synced_at) are in the SELECT (`ai_admin.ts:432-437`), fix complete, no residual, and the `row?.X ?? null` wide-config surface is concentrated in that one verified handler. **FOUND the real bug in `domains.ts` `verifyPendingHostnames` (LIVE — runs on EVERY cron fire):** it flipped a pending custom hostname to `verification_failed` on the FIRST check returning ANY CF `verification_errors`, then **excluded `verification_failed` from every future sweep** (`WHERE status='pending'`). But a freshly-added hostname returns verification_errors until the owner's DNS (CNAME + DCV) propagates — often minutes — so a domain checked too early was **stranded at `verification_failed` FOREVER, even once DNS landed**: a paid custom-domain that silently never activates. **FIX:** a 1h grace window — young hostnames with errors stay `pending` (retried next sweep → self-heal on propagation); only fail after the window (genuinely-persistent errors still terminate, as before). **iter 116 — ✅ fixed a LIVE outbox retry bug (2nd instance of the premature-terminal class) — the transactional-event dispatcher never retried failed events.** Ran iter-115's [[premature-terminal-status-plus-no-retry-strands-rows]] HUNT tree-wide (grep sweeps with `WHERE status IN ('pending','processing',…)` + a terminal write). Two candidates: `event_bus.ts` outbox (highest-value — transactional event delivery) + `social.ts` scheduled-posts poll. **FOUND the bug in the outbox** (`drainOutbox`, LIVE 5-min cron `index.ts:1816`): `readPendingOutbox` read `WHERE status='pending'` ONLY. On a dispatch failure `markFailed` sets `status='failed'`, `attempts+1` — but the next drain (pending-only) NEVER re-read it → a single transient failure **STRANDED the event forever**: never retried, never dead-lettered (`attempts` stuck at 1 < MAX 5), recoverable only by a manual admin `requeueFailedOutbox`. Meanwhile `outboxStats` bucketed it as `retrying` ("will retry next drain") and `assessDrainHealth` logged "retrying → dead-letter" — **both lies**; the `MAX_OUTBOX_ATTEMPTS=5` cap was meaningless (attempts never incremented past 1). Same class as the iter-115 domains hostname bug. **FIX:** `readPendingOutbox` now reads `status='pending' OR (status='failed' AND attempts < MAX)` (bind `MAX_OUTBOX_ATTEMPTS`) → failed rows are re-dispatched → `attempts` increments each drain → they genuinely retry and dead-letter at the cap. Makes the retry→DLQ lifecycle + the `outboxStats`/`assessDrainHealth` buckets REAL. **TDD RED→GREEN:** strengthened the reader test to assert the retryable-failed clause (RED: SQL was pending-only; `Received: …WHERE status='pending' ORDER BY…`) → GREEN. worker **tsc 0** · **6 outbox suites / 81 tests green** · committed `8320a49c` · **worker deploy `5d247f89`** · health 200 (drain is `*/5`-gated so the tail window didn't span a fire, but the SQL change is deterministically unit-tested + tsc-checked). **⚠️ The premature-terminal class now has 2 instances (domains + outbox) → a `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` detector is now WARRANTED per the audit-arc ladder** (grep each `WHERE status='pending'`-style sweep for a paired terminal write with no re-read of retryable-terminal rows). **⚠️ NEW LEAD (incidental, tail-surfaced — NEXT):** a LIVE swallowed-SQL error `no such column: media_size_bytes` on `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(media_size_bytes),0)/1048576.0 AS total_mb FROM sites` ([[swallowed-sql-error-masks-schema-drift-as-404]] class) → a storage-usage surface silently shows 0/wrong. Trace `total_mb`'s consumer + either add the column (missing migration) or fix the query to the real media table. Also the `social.ts:1037` scheduled-posts poll (2nd sweep candidate — verify it re-reads on failure).

**iter 117 — ✅ closed iter-116's tail-surfaced lead: fixed a LIVE swallowed-SQL bug that made the admin "Media" storage gauge read a FALSE 0 GB for every org.** The [[swallowed-sql-error-masks-schema-drift-as-404]] class — here the swallow makes a gauge lie 0, not a 404 (same swallow, different symptom). `libs/features/usage_gauges/service.ts` computed media storage as `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(media_size_bytes),0)/1048576.0 AS total_mb FROM sites` — but `sites` has **NO `media_size_bytes` column** (confirmed absent from every migration). So D1 threw `no such column: media_size_bytes` on EVERY call → `dbQueryOne` swallowed it to `null` → `mediaGb` fell back to 0 → the Media gauge showed 0 GB regardless of actual usage, for all orgs. Real source: **`media_assets.size_bytes`** (migration 0040, `INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`; `src/services/media.ts` writes it per upload; table has `org_id` + `deleted_at`). `computeUsageGauges` is LIVE — `handlers.ts:20` → route `GET /api/usage` (`src/index.ts:679`), flag-gated `usage_gauges`. **FIX:** query now `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(size_bytes),0)/1048576.0 AS total_mb FROM media_assets WHERE org_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL`. **TDD RED→GREEN:** added a test asserting the media SQL matches `/FROM media_assets/` + `/SUM\(size_bytes\)/` and NOT `/media_size_bytes/` (RED: `Received …SUM(media_size_bytes)…FROM sites`) → GREEN. worker **tsc 0** · **usage_gauges 5/5 green** · committed `32be7218` (pushed origin/main) · **worker deploy `fcebcd71`** · health 200. **VERIFIED LIVE (not just unit):** authed `GET /api/usage` → 200 with `{"metric":"media_gb","used":0,"limit":1,"unit":"GB","pct":0}` — `used:0` is now the HONEST value (the e2e-test-org has 0 `media_assets` rows; the query no longer errors) while the sibling `sites` gauge shows `used:4` = real org data, proving the endpoint reads live D1. **Tail-confirmed the swallow is gone:** `wrangler tail` while hitting `/api/usage` ×3 → ZERO `no such column: media_size_bytes` (was firing every call before). **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) the `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` detector — premature-terminal class still at 2 instances (domains iter-115 + outbox iter-116), warranted per audit-arc ladder; (2) `social.ts:1037` scheduled-posts poll — 3rd sweep candidate, verify it re-reads on failure; (3) continue the swallowed-SQL sweep — grep remaining `dbQuery`/`dbQueryOne` call sites for column refs and diff against the live D1 schema (fresh subsystems: rag / google_drive / image_generation).

**iter 118 — ✅ fixed a LIVE swallowed-SQL bug: an unescaped-wildcard `LIKE` crashed the create-funnel's site search (`pattern too complex` → swallowed → lying-empty "no sites found").** OBSERVE first cleared the flagged 3rd premature-terminal candidate: **`social-publish.ts` is NOT a 3rd instance** — the workflow does bounded 3× retry (`retries:{limit:3,backoff:'exponential'}`) BEFORE writing terminal `'failed'` (l.335), and the poll (`social.ts:1037`) re-selects `status='scheduled'` each tick → proper bounded-retry→dead-letter, not a premature strand (class stays at 2: domains+outbox). Then ran the iter-116/117 live-bug method — **`wrangler tail` for D1/SQL errors across cron fires** — which surfaced `D1_ERROR: LIKE or GLOB pattern too complex` on `SELECT … FROM sites WHERE business_name LIKE ?` (the pre-built-site search, homepage create funnel). **ROOT CAUSE:** the user's OWN `%`/`_` were embedded in the `` `%${q}%` `` pattern → (a) wildcard-**injection** (searching "50%" is a match-anything wildcard → wrong rows) and (b) a wildcard-heavy term trips SQLite's "too complex" → `dbQuery` swallows it → **lying-empty** on the funnel's first step. **Grep-closed the CLASS** (per the sweep doctrine): 4 user-free-text-wrapped-in-`%…%` sites — `search.ts` ×2 (`/api/sites/search` + ⌘K site search), `super_admin.ts` (operator org search), `media.ts` (media library search). `ai_admin_features.ts:321` is NOT the bug (it deliberately opts INTO LIKE only when `val.includes('%')` — an intentional admin wildcard filter). **⚠️ KEY LESSON (verified live, not assumed):** I FIRST shipped the textbook fix — escape `%`/`_`/`\` + `ESCAPE '\'` (deploy `2a7172c0`) — but the LIVE tail PROVED it insufficient: **D1's SQLite raises "too complex" on the raw `%`/`_` byte count BEFORE resolving escapes** (a `%_`×30 term still errored WITH the `ESCAPE '\'` clause deployed — the 200 `{data:[]}` was still the swallowed-error path). Pivoted to **STRIP** the metacharacters (`sanitizeLikeTerm` removes `%`/`_`/`\` → zero wildcards → the `%…%` pattern can never be complex nor wrong-match). Housed in a NEW standalone `src/services/like_pattern.ts` module (NOT `db.ts`) precisely so a `jest.mock('…/db.js')` in the 6 handler tests that mock the D1 layer can't stub it to `undefined`. **TDD RED→GREEN:** `search_routes.test` (strip param + no ESCAPE) + a `like_pattern.test` unit suite. worker **tsc 0** · **8 affected suites + db.test = 264 tests green** · deploy `397a542f` · **VERIFIED LIVE:** wildcard-heavy `/api/sites/search?q=%_×30` now returns REAL sites (`acme-bakery`/`e2e-site-1` — was swallowed-empty), normal + `%`-containing searches all 200, and `wrangler tail` shows **ZERO** "pattern too complex" post-deploy (was firing before). **⚠️ ALSO SURFACED in the tail (NOT fixed — noted as next leads):** (1) **`GET /api/analytics/:siteId` → `CF GraphQL errors: unknown field "requests"`** — fires on EVERY analytics call; the `cloudflare_zone_analytics` fallback query references a nonexistent GraphQL field → the CF-zone fallback is BROKEN (real live bug, high-frequency); (2) **audit-log Zod rejects a non-UUID `org_id`** (`'e2e-test-org'`) → "Audit log write threw unexpectedly" drops the audit trail for non-UUID orgs; (3) `better-auth migrate skipped D1_ERROR: SQLITE_AUTH` (KNOWN per [[better-auth-d1-needs-static-schema-migration]] — runtime migrate fails on D1, gracefully skipped, custom auth is the live path). **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) the analytics CF-GraphQL `unknown field "requests"` malformed fallback (highest-frequency live bug); (2) audit-log non-UUID rejection; (3) `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` detector (class still at 2).

**iter 119 — ✅ closed iter-118's tail-surfaced lead: fixed the CF zone-analytics GraphQL query that threw `unknown field` on EVERY `/api/analytics/:siteId` call (a dead fallback + constant log noise).** `cloudflare_analytics.ts loadSiteTraffic` (the `/api/analytics/:siteId` **second fallback**, after GA4, before the D1 `first_party_edge` primary) queried `httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups` but with the **LEGACY `httpRequests1dGroups` field selection** — someone renamed the dataset but left `sum { requests pageViews }`, `orderBy [sum_requests_DESC]`, `uniq { uniques }`, and `$host: string!` (lowercase). CF rejected every call → the zone fallback NEVER returned data (fell through to D1 — which DOES serve real analytics, so no user-facing outage, but a dead tier + a warn on every call). Same config-vs-reality blind spot as iter-42: the **test `cloudflare_analytics.test.ts` MIRRORED the bug** — its `fullZonePayload()` mock used the wrong `sum:{requests,pageViews}` shape, so it stayed green while CF rejected the real query. **AUTHORITATIVE FIX (not guess-and-deploy — introspected CF's live schema):** `GET __type(ZoneHttpRequestsAdaptiveGroups)` → fields are `avg/confidence/count/dimensions/quantiles/ratio/sum` (NO `uniq`, NO `requests`); `sum` has `visits`+`edgeResponseBytes` (NO `pageViews`); dimensions confirm `clientRequestHTTPHost`/`clientRequestPath`/`clientCountryName`/`date`. So: request count = top-level **`count`** (not `sum.requests`), page-views proxy = **`sum.visits`** (adaptive has no pageViews), `orderBy [count_DESC]`, `$host: String!`, and **`uniq` REMOVED** (adaptive can't do per-host unique visitors → `unique_visitors: 0`; the D1 `visitor_events` primary carries real uniques). Updated query + TS types + parser + the test's mock/assertions to the real adaptive shape + a query-shape guard (`count`+`visits`, NOT `pageViews`/`requests`/`uniq`, `String!`). **TDD RED→GREEN** (RED: no `count` in query) · worker **tsc 0** · **5 analytics suites / 97 tests green** · deploy `522a7808`. **VERIFIED LIVE (2 guess-deploys narrowed it — `requests`→`count` cleared error 1, revealed `uniq`; introspection then nailed the rest):** `wrangler tail` on `/api/analytics/{e2e-site-1,2,3}` → **ZERO `unknown field` errors** (was firing every call); the query is now CF-schema-VALID. **🔑 SURFACED (Brian-gated, NOT code — see SURFACED below):** the query now fails on a TOKEN-PERMISSION error (`CF_API_TOKEN` lacks `zone.analytics.read` for zone `9ceaa211…`) — the schema bug had MASKED it. The zone fallback works once the token is scoped; meanwhile the D1 primary serves real data. **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) audit-log Zod rejects non-UUID `org_id` (drops the audit trail for non-UUID orgs — a real live drop, seen in the iter-118 tail); (2) `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` detector (premature-terminal class at 2); (3) continue the tail-for-SQL/error method on fresh subsystems.

**iter 120 — ✅ CODIFIED the premature-terminal / no-retry class as a precise detector (audit-arc "Detect+Surface" step) after a clean tail + exhausted hunt confirmed the class is swept.** OBSERVE: (a) a broad `wrangler tail` sweep (8 authed endpoints × cron fires, ~5.7k lines) for D1/SQL/validation/throw errors came back **CLEAN** — the iter-116→119 fixes (media gauge, LIKE, analytics GraphQL, audit drop) all HOLD; only the known-benign `rdap probe failed` (CF-egress-rate-limited, [[cf-worker-egress-rate-limited-by-cf-fronted-third-party]]). (b) Re-ran the [[premature-terminal-status-plus-no-retry-strands-rows]] HUNT tree-wide: the class is confirmed at **2 instances (domains iter-115 + outbox iter-116), both fixed, no 3rd** — `org_transfers` (user-cancel + `expires_at`, no error-sweep), `feedback` (admin-moderated), and the task-inbox expiry sweep (expiry-gated, correct) are all NOT the class. **BUILT `scripts/check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs`** (report-only, wired `check:status-sweep` + `test:scripts`): a FILE-LEVEL heuristic — flags a file iff it has (1) a SQL `WHERE … status = '<non-terminal>'` sweep reader (the `WHERE` anchor excludes object-literal `status:'pending'`), (2) a terminal-status WRITE (`status='failed'` literal + a `dbUpdate`/`dbExecute`/`SET status` DB-write in the file — excludes in-memory result/state objects), and (3) NO guard token (`attempts <` / `created_at` / grace / retries / requeue / expire / retryable-terminal re-read). Keying on GUARD TOKENS (not an exempt-list) means the 2 fixed sites are correctly NOT flagged AND a revert that removes a guard re-flags them. **Precision-hardened per [[validator-precision-discipline]]:** the first draft FP'd on 2 files (`feature_e2e.ts` E2E result-objects + `claim_build_session.ts` event-sourced reducer — neither a SQL sweep); tightening the reader to require the SQL `WHERE` context + the write to require a DB-write helper eliminated both. **+6 fixture unit tests** (1 positive real-bug + 5 negatives: grace-guard, retry-guard, result-object, event-reducer, aggregate-CASE-WHEN). Tree-wide run **CLEAN (0 flags)** · `test:scripts` **33→39 green** · detector clean. Build-tool-only (no worker/frontend change → no deploy; ships via push + the test gate per verification-loop self-application). **⚠️ NEXT:** statically-findable classes + the tail method are at deep saturation (5 consecutive live-bug fixes now holding); the highest-value remaining loop-safe targets are a **fresh-subsystem behavioral audit** on a 0-prior-mention live service (`google_drive`/`image_generation`/`notifications`-worker per the iter-53 method) OR a **super-admin Browserbase** pass over the flag-gated/leads/multi-seat surfaces the 1-seat free-plan E2E org can't reach. The deferred WAVES (`ag-grid→TanStack`, create→build re-enable) remain highest-value but need dedicated/unblocked turns.

**iter 121 — ✅ fresh-subsystem audit of the EMAIL/notifications stack (iter-53 method) → found + fixed TWO bugs, one a P0 that broke magic-link LOGIN + all SES transactional email in prod.** Behaviorally audited `notifications.ts` (broadly live — 8 callers) + `ses_email_provider.ts`; live-exercised a magic-link send + tailed. **🔴 BUG 1 (P0 — deploy `a290b6ba`, LIVE-verified): `ses_email_provider.ts:60` `this.fetchImpl = deps.fetchImpl ?? fetch`** assigned the BARE native `fetch` to an instance field, then called it as `this.fetchImpl(url,…)` (l.111) → the Workers runtime invokes `fetch` with `this === the provider instance` → **`Illegal invocation: function called with incorrect this reference`** → SES (the PRIMARY transactional rail, ADR-0019) threw on EVERY send → magic-link login emails + all SES email silently failed (`magic_link_email_send_failed`). The unit tests ALWAYS injected `deps.fetchImpl` (a mock) so the `?? fetch` fallback path was never exercised — a green suite over a 100%-broken prod rail. **Fix = `deps.fetchImpl ?? fetch.bind(globalThis)`** (bind the native fetch to globalThis; injected mocks unaffected). **TDD RED→GREEN:** a `this`-capturing fetch spy on the fallback path proves the receiver was the provider instance (RED) → globalThis (GREEN). **VERIFIED LIVE:** re-ran the magic-link POST → `wrangler tail` shows **ZERO `Illegal invocation` / `magic_link_email_send_failed`** + `Magic link created` (was firing the error every send). **Grep-closed the class:** the only `this.<field> = …fetch` PROPERTY assignment was this one (`resilient-fetch.ts` uses a LOCAL `const doFetch = … ?? fetch` called plainly → `this===undefined`, which native fetch tolerates → NOT the bug; `crypto.randomUUID()` are static-method calls). **🐛 BUG 2 (deploy `b0208ff5`): `notifications.ts sendEmail` fell back on ABSENCE, not FAILURE.** The JSDoc promised "Resend → SendGrid fallback" + "@throws when BOTH fail", but the code `throw`ew on the FIRST configured provider's failure (SES `await` / Resend `!res.ok`) → the next rail was only reached when the prior was UNCONFIGURED. In prod (SES+Resend+SendGrid ALL set) every email was SES-only with two idle fallbacks — so BUG 1's SES outage failed the send outright. **Fix:** try each CONFIGURED rail in order, `catch`/`!res.ok` → log + push to `failures[]` + FALL THROUGH; `throw` only when EVERY configured rail failed (matching the doc). Preserved the rich per-provider failure log shape (`message`+`status`+`body_excerpt`) the existing tests assert. **+2 RED→GREEN** (SES-fails→Resend delivers; all-fail→throws + Resend attempted). worker **tsc 0** · **7 email suites / 93 tests green**. **✅ RESOLVED (CHAOS pass-9, migration `0613`): audit-log write for a SYSTEM action was dropping on a D1 FOREIGN KEY constraint** — `writeAuditLog({org_id:'system', action:'auth.magic_link_requested'})` → `D1_ERROR: FOREIGN KEY constraint failed` (`audit_logs.org_id` FKs `orgs.id`; `'system'` wasn't a real org row). Pass-8 relaxed the Zod schema so it REACHED the DB, then the FK rejected it → system-action audits still dropped (FK error, was a Zod drop before). Fixed by seeding a soft-deleted sentinel `system` org (FK checks PK existence, ignores `deleted_at`) — see the pass-9 entry above for the causal proof + the `check-system-org-seed.mjs` regression detector. **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) the audit system-org FK drop; (2) continue fresh-subsystem behavioral audits — the email audit just found a P0, so the iter-53 pivot is still highly productive (do NOT declare saturation).

**iter 122 — ✅ fresh-subsystem audit of the STRIPE WEBHOOK stack → found + fixed a REVENUE-CRITICAL idempotency/retry bug that permanently stranded failed payment webhooks (deploy `c9bf12dc`).** Adversarially read `webhook.ts` + `routes/webhooks.ts` (`ai_crypto.ts` audited first — CONVERGED: fresh 12-byte IV/encrypt, 32-byte key check, GCM tag, rotation fallback, `decryptOrPassthrough` — no churn). **THE BUG (two coupled defects on the money path):** (1) `checkWebhookIdempotency` treated ANY existing `webhook_events` row as a duplicate (read `status` but never used it) → a stored-then-FAILED event was skipped on every redelivery; (2) the route catch returned **200 on processing failure** ("to prevent retries") → Stripe NEVER retried. Net: a `checkout.session.completed` that fails processing on a transient error = **customer charged, plan never activated, event permanently lost** — and NO sweep exists (`checkWebhookIdempotency` is the ONLY `webhook_events` reader), so the only recovery (Stripe retry) was defeated. Same premature-terminal/no-retry class as iter-115/116, on revenue. A test literally locked the bug (`webhook_route.test.ts` "returns 200 … to prevent retries"). **ROOT-CAUSE FIX (coherent slice):** (a) `checkWebhookIdempotency` is now status-aware — only TERMINAL states (`processed`/`quarantined`) dedupe; a non-terminal row (`received`/`processing`/`failed`) returns `{isDuplicate:false, existingId, existingStatus, existingAttempts}` = retry-eligible; (b) route RESETS the existing row (`resetWebhookForRetry` — reuse it, since `webhook_events` is `UNIQUE(provider,event_id)` so a re-INSERT would fail) instead of skipping/duplicating; (c) catch returns **500 on transient failure** (Stripe redelivers → idempotency reprocesses the SAME row) and **quarantines + acks 200 after `MAX_WEBHOOK_ATTEMPTS=5`** (poison-pill guard — stops infinite retry that would trip Stripe's endpoint auto-disable); `attempts` now 1-based (bumped per redelivery) driving the cap. **TDD RED→GREEN:** rewrote the bug-locking 200 test → asserts 500 + `WEBHOOK_PROCESSING_ERROR`; +new route tests (quarantine-after-max acks 200; **reprocesses a prior-failed event on redelivery** — resets the row, doesn't skip); +storage tests (failed/processing = retry-eligible, processed/quarantined = duplicate; `resetWebhookForRetry` bumps attempts; quarantined mark). **Other consumer `livekit_webhooks.ts` audited SAFE** (never marks 'failed' → rows are received/processed; a non-terminal redelivery just re-logs + acks 200, store UNIQUE-violation swallowed — no crash, no regression; left focused). worker **tsc 0** · **27 webhook+billing suites / 536 tests green** (63 in the 5 core) · `check:dbexecute` clean · prettier clean. **VERIFIED LIVE (deploy `c9bf12dc`):** `/health` 200, homepage 200, and `POST /webhooks/stripe` with a bad sig → **401 WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID** (route live + reachable by Stripe + sig-verify intact; the idempotency/retry behavior is authoritatively unit-proven — a real Stripe round-trip isn't loop-triggerable). New memory [[webhook-idempotency-must-key-on-terminal-success-not-receipt]]. **⚠️ NEXT:** continue fresh-subsystem behavioral audits (crypto CONVERGED, webhook FIXED — remaining fresh 0-prior-mention live services: `rag.ts` index-write drop at :143/:299, `contact.ts` lead ingestion, `image_generation.ts`, `google_places.ts`, `confidence.ts`); the iter-53/121/122 pivot keeps finding server-side money/data bugs no browser gate sees.

**iter 123 — ✅ fresh-subsystem audit of the CONTACT/lead stack → fixed the iter-121 "fallback-on-absence-not-failure" bug DUPLICATED in the platform contact form + corrected a doc-vs-code lie (deploy `9156f3ae`).** Two contact routes exist: (a) **per-site** `POST /api/contact-form/:slug` (`search.ts:1240`) = **CONVERGED** — already hardened (persists a durable `contacts` CRM row FIRST, error-checked; emails the SITE OWNER best-effort; no lying-success/failure) — no churn; (b) **platform** `POST /api/contact` (`api.ts` → `contactService.handleContactForm`) = **the bug.** `contact.ts sendEmail` had its OWN copy of the SES→Resend→SendGrid logic and, when SES is configured (prod, ADR-0019), called `getEmailProvider(env).sendTransactional` then **`return`ed with NO fallback on SES FAILURE** — the `EmailRouter` is deliberately SES-or-Fake (no internal Resend/SendGrid fallback), so a transient SES 5xx propagated → the public Turnstile-gated contact form 5xx'd the visitor and the lead was LOST (no persistence on this route). Exact same class as iter-121's `notifications.ts` bug, in a separate un-fixed copy. The route docstring even CLAIMED "Resend → SendGrid fallback" — a doc-vs-code lie. **FIX:** `sendEmail` now wraps the SES send in try/catch → on failure pushes to `failures[]` + falls through to Resend → SendGrid (matching notifications.ts iter-121); throws only when EVERY configured rail fails. Corrected 3 stale docstrings (module header, `handleContactForm` JSDoc, `api.ts` route remark) → "SES primary → Resend → SendGrid". **TDD RED→GREEN:** +`falls back to Resend when SES FAILS (SES configured)` (mock amazonaws.com→500, assert SES attempted THEN Resend picked it up — lead delivered). All existing contact tests stay green (the SES-success ADR-0019 test at :67 uses a 200 mock). worker **tsc 0** · **22 email/contact/notifications suites / 240 tests green** · prettier clean. **VERIFIED LIVE (deploy `9156f3ae`):** `/health` 200, homepage 200; `POST /api/contact` → 403 CF managed challenge (Turnstile edge-gate working as designed — the behavioral fallback is unit-proven; a real submission needs a browser+Turnstile + would email hey@). Drift noted: SES→Resend→SendGrid send logic is DUPLICATED across ~10 files (search/forms/auth_org/ai_admin/weekly_digest/credits/contact/form_router/mcp_client/notifications) — a future consolidation onto one seam, too broad for one slice. **⚠️ NEXT:** continue fresh-subsystem audits — `rag.ts` index-write drop (:143/:299 bare `dbExecute`), `image_generation.ts`, `google_places.ts`, `confidence.ts`; OR the platform contact form's NO-PERSISTENCE gap (email-only → if all rails fail, the lead is lost; the per-site form's persist-first pattern is the model).

**iter 124 — ✅ fresh-subsystem audit of the RAG (Vectorize + AutoRAG) stack → fixed two bare-await D1 writes that swallowed `{error}` (silent Vectorize↔D1 divergence + a lying-success), on a LIVE path (deploy `0a60cc62`).** RAG is live: `RAG_INDEX` Vectorize is bound in prod (`env.production.vectorize` → `projectsites-rag`), `indexChunk` runs on EVERY site build (`rag_publish.ts:150` `indexSiteFiles` ← `site-generation.ts`), and `semanticSearch` backs `/api/sites/:id/search` (`api.ts:1597`). **BUG 1 (`rag.ts indexChunk`):** after the Vectorize `upsert` (the primary searchable write) succeeds, the D1 `rag_chunks` mirror INSERT was a bare `await dbExecute(...)` ignoring `{error}`. `rag_chunks` is the AUTHORITATIVE source `deleteIndex` + re-index read from — a swallowed mirror failure diverges the two stores: the vector is searchable but has NO mirror row, so it can never be found/deleted by `(kind, sourceId)` → an orphaned vector, invisible (the caller `rag_publish` only catches a THROW, and the mirror write never threw). **BUG 2 (`rag.ts deleteIndex`):** the `DELETE FROM rag_chunks` was also a bare await, and `return { removed: ids.length }` reported success even when the D1 rows were NOT deleted (a lying-success — [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]] class). **FIX (fail-soft per [[fail-fast-build-fail-soft-prod]]):** capture `{error}` on BOTH writes and `console.warn(JSON.stringify({level,service:'rag',message,...}))` on failure (the webhook.ts/search.ts structured-log pattern) — surfacing the divergence to monitoring WITHOUT throwing (the primary Vectorize write already succeeded; re-index/idempotent upsert self-heals — don't fail the caller's build). **TDD RED→GREEN:** +`indexChunk SURFACES a D1 mirror write failure (…) but stays fail-soft` + `deleteIndex SURFACES a D1 mirror DELETE failure instead of silently claiming success` (mock `dbExecute`→`{error}`, assert the warn fires + the op still resolves). worker **tsc 0** · **3 rag suites / 43 tests green** · `check:dbexecute` clean · prettier clean. **VERIFIED LIVE (deploy `0a60cc62`):** `/health` + homepage + `/api/health` all 200 (deploy integrity; **iter 125 — ✅ fixed the two check:dbexecute lying-successes: a bulk-DESTRUCTIVE `batchProcess` that claimed `ok:true` on a silently-failed delete, + a `platform_mcp` publish status-marker drift (deploy `91ea8178`).** The 3 remaining fresh services (`image_generation`/`google_places`/`confidence`) have NO bare-await D1 writes (clean). **BUG 1 (`libs/features/batch_operations/service.ts` — the meaty one):** the bulk rebuild/snapshot/delete loop did `await dbExecute(...)` (INSERT job / soft-delete site) then pushed `ok:true, '${action}_queued'`. `dbExecute` RETURNS `{error}` (never throws), so the surrounding `try/catch` NEVER saw a D1 write failure → a "delete 50 sites" could report every one succeeded while the rows survived (a lying-success on a DESTRUCTIVE bulk action). **FIX:** capture `{error, changes}` → report `ok:false, '${action}_failed: …'` on error; `ok:false, 'not_found_or_already_deleted'` when a delete matches 0 rows (SELECT/UPDATE race); guard the unknown-action path (never a phantom success); DRY'd the twin rebuild/snapshot INSERTs. **BUG 2 (`libs/features/platform_mcp/service.ts:382`):** after `publishSiteFiles` succeeds, the `UPDATE sites SET status='published'` was a bare await ignoring `{error}` (inconsistent — the snapshot `dbInsert` right below already did `if (!snap.error)`). **FIX (fail-soft):** capture `{error}` + `console.warn(publish_status_update_failed)` on failure — surface the status drift WITHOUT failing the deploy that already published (next publish re-sets status). **TDD:** batch_operations +5 cases (ok:false-on-error, delete-changes-0, unknown-action-no-write, independent per-site aggregation, rebuild→'build' job) — RED against the old code; **iter 128 — ✅ fresh-subsystem behavioral audit of `task_inbox.ts` (the workflow↔admin human-in-the-loop tray) → fixed a LYING-SUCCESS write-drop in `postAskUser` on the CORE build pipeline (worker deploy `c6c88f90`).** task_inbox is fully LIVE: `postAskUser` is called at `site-generation.ts:600` (the site-generation workflow), `applyExpiredDefaults` runs on the `* * * * *` cron (`index.ts:1757`), `listOpenTasks`/`resolveTask` back `/api/inbox/tasks` (`api.ts:3006/3037`). **BUG:** `postAskUser` did a bare `await dbInsert(env.DB, 'ai_task_inbox', {…})` **ignoring the returned `{error}`**. `dbInsert` returns `{error: string|null}` and NEVER throws — db.ts:246-249 documents this EXACT class ("any caller that ignores the returned error returned a lying-success and silently DROPPED the row"). On a transient INSERT failure, `postAskUser` still returned `{id, expiresAt}` — a **phantom id**. The workflow then does `step.waitForEvent('task-resolved-${id}')` on a row that WAS NEVER INSERTED → the tray (`listOpenTasks`) never shows it + the `applyExpiredDefaults` cron never finds it → the build **STALLS the full timeout window** (30-min default), AND the workflow step's automatic retry is **defeated** (the step "succeeded" with a phantom id). **FIX (fail-fast per [[fail-fast-build-fail-soft-prod]] — the post is a CRITICAL precondition for the `waitForEvent`; a workflow step should RETRY a transient insert failure, not proceed blind):** capture `{error}`, `throw` a diagnosable error on failure. **TDD RED→GREEN:** +`postAskUser THROWS on a dropped insert instead of returning a phantom id` (mock `dbInsert`→`{error}`, assert `.rejects.toThrow(/failed to persist task/)`) — RED vs the old bare-await, GREEN after. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · prettier clean · **task_inbox 28/28 green** · **DEPLOYED `c6c88f90`** + live-verified (`/api/health`+`/` 200; `/api/inbox/tasks` unauth→401, authed→`{"tasks":[]}` 200 — the changed module loads+serves via its live route; a real D1-insert-failure isn't on-demand triggerable, so the throw itself is unit-proven). **Sibling audit findings (NOT bugs — correctly built):** `resolveTask` guards double-resolve atomically (`WHERE resolved_at IS NULL` + `changes===0` check) + fans the workflow event fail-soft (a `sendEvent` failure never rolls back the resolution); `listOpenTasks` is org-scoped (no IDOR); `applyExpiredDefaults` is idempotent (a false→row stays unresolved→re-swept next minute). **⚠️ minor observability nit (NOT fixed — non-lying):** `resolveTask`'s `dbUpdate` `{error}` is ignored — on a transient UPDATE error it returns `false` (HONEST: "not resolved" → retriable), but conflates transient-error with already-resolved (no log). A warn-log would aid debugging; not a lying-success so left for a spillover turn. New memory candidate: "postAskUser bare-dbInsert lying-success strands the build waiting on a never-posted task." **⚠️ NEXT:** continue fresh behavioral audits — `ai_workflows.ts` (1093-line core pipeline, biggest live surface, has a test), `domains.ts` (977 lines, CF hostname provisioning writes), `notifications.ts` (545); OR the `resolveTask` observability nit if a quieter slice is wanted.

**iter 129 — ✅ fresh-subsystem behavioral audit of `domains.ts` (CF-for-SaaS custom-hostname provisioning) → fixed a two-instance LYING-SUCCESS-with-orphaned-side-effect in BOTH provision paths, with a saga-compensator (worker deploy `ec1af128`).** domains is LIVE: `provisionFreeDomain` runs on every site create (default `{slug}.projectsites.dev`), `provisionCustomDomain` backs `POST /api/sites/:siteId/hostnames`. **BUG (2 instances — `provisionFreeDomain:263`, `provisionCustomDomain:356`):** each calls `createCustomHostname(env, hostname)` (CF API — a real side-effect) and THEN does a bare `await dbInsert(db, 'hostnames', {…})` **ignoring the returned `{error}`**. `dbInsert` returns `{error}` and never throws (db.ts:246-249), so a dropped tracking INSERT returned a **phantom success**: (a) the CF custom hostname exists but has NO D1 row → an **orphaned CF hostname** invisible to `getSiteHostnames` + the `verifyPendingHostnames` cron; (b) WORSE on the custom path — `if (isFirstCustomDomain) setSolePrimary(db, siteId, hostnameId)` (line 372) then runs its atomic batch (`UPDATE …is_primary=0 WHERE site_id=?` then `…is_primary=1 WHERE id=hostnameId`) against a `hostnameId` that was NEVER inserted → clears every primary + sets none → a **primary-less site** (broken canonical-host resolution — the EXACT failure `setSolePrimary`'s own docstring warns about, reintroduced via the dropped insert). **FIX (saga/compensating-action per CLAUDE.md §8.2 + fail-fast per [[fail-fast-build-fail-soft-prod]]):** new private `rollbackHostnameInsert(env, cfId, hostname, insertError)` — capture `{error}`; on failure best-effort `deleteCustomHostname` (undo the CF side-effect → no orphan; 404-idempotent), log if the compensation itself fails (orphan needs manual cleanup), then THROW. Both paths call it before proceeding, so the custom path throws BEFORE `setSolePrimary` → no primary-less site. **TDD RED→GREEN:** +2 (`provisionFreeDomain` + `provisionCustomDomain` each: mock CF-create OK → `dbInsert`→`{error}` → mock compensating DELETE OK → assert `.rejects.toThrow(/failed to persist hostname/)` + the 2nd fetch is the CF DELETE; custom also asserts `mockBatch` NEVER called). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · prettier clean · **domains 37/37 green** · **DEPLOYED `ec1af128`** + live-verified (`/api/health`+`/` 200; `/api/sites/e2e-site-3/hostnames` authed→`{"data":[]}` 200 — the changed module loads+serves; a real D1-insert-failure isn't on-demand triggerable, so the compensate+throw is unit-proven). **Sibling audit (NOT bugs — correctly built):** `setSolePrimary` already atomic (`db.batch` clear+set, docstring-documented fix); `deleteCustomHostname` 404-idempotent; `provisionCustomDomain` enforces the domain-limit + duplicate-hostname conflict BEFORE the CF call. **⚠️ NEXT:** continue fresh behavioral audits — `notifications.ts` (545, email — though sendEmail already SES→Resend→SendGrid-hardened in iter-121/123; audit the OTHER functions), `media.ts` (1038, upload/stock/generate write paths), `ai_workflows.ts` (1093, core pipeline); OR the `resolveTask` observability nit (iter-128).

**iter 130 — ✅ ran the `check:dbexecute` detector tree-wide (11 bare-await writes) → fixed the highest-IMPACT one: a SAFETY lying-success in `abuse_takedown` resolveAbuseReport (worker deploy `8b99f75b`).** The detector rates all 11 LOW (heuristic = "not a HIGH user-facing mutation"), but LOW≠not-a-bug — audited by TABLE SEMANTICS + impact. **BUG (`libs/features/abuse_takedown/service.ts` `resolveAbuseReport`, LIVE via `POST /api/abuse/reports/:id/resolve`):** a `takedown` did TWO bare `dbExecute` UPDATEs — (1) `abuse_reports SET status='upheld_takedown'` then (2) `sites SET status='archived'` (the ACTUAL takedown of the abusive site). Both ignored `{error}` (dbExecute returns `{error}`, never throws) AND were non-atomic + in the WRONG order. If the site-archive (2) dropped, the fn returned the report as `'upheld_takedown'` (success to the moderator) while the **abusive site stayed LIVE ('published')** — a silent SAFETY failure (DMCA/illegal-content stays served). WORSE: a retry found the report no longer `pending`/`reviewing` (already marked resolved by (1)) → the SELECT returned null → **the site was NEVER archived** (retry-hole). **FIX (reorder + fail-fast, retryable):** do the safety-critical site-archive FIRST + error-check it → `throw` on failure BEFORE marking the report resolved, so a failed archive leaves the report `pending` (retryable, site correctly still-not-falsely-resolved); then the report-status UPDATE, also error-checked + throw. If the archive succeeds but the report-status write fails, the site IS archived (takedown effective) + the report re-queues (idempotent re-resolve). Chose reorder over `db.batch` atomicity to keep the dbExecute-based tests intact — both are correct; reorder is the minimal safe slice. **TDD RED→GREEN:** +`takedown whose site-archive DROPS throws + leaves the report unresolved` (route dbExecute `UPDATE sites`→`{error}`, assert `.rejects.toThrow(/failed to archive site/)` + the `UPDATE abuse_reports` NEVER fired); updated `beforeEach` `mockDbExecute`→`{error:null}` (real shape, was `undefined`). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · prettier clean · **abuse_takedown 18/18** · detector **11→9** · **DEPLOYED `8b99f75b`** + live-verified (`/api/health`+`/` 200; `POST /api/abuse/reports/x/resolve` unauth→403, authed(e2e non-super-admin)→403 — super-admin-gated, module loads clean, NOT 500; the throw is unit-proven, a real D1-write-failure isn't on-demand triggerable). **Remaining 9 detector LOWs re-triaged:** `index.ts:1685` (janitor marking stuck sites 'error' — idempotent, re-swept, `console.warn`'d → correctly best-effort); `task_inbox.ts:330` (deleteTask — test-cleanup, void); `auth.ts` oauth_states ×2 + `claim_links`/`db_shards`/`sms_agent`/`analytics_annotations`/`batch_operations` — mostly best-effort/dark or void-return; none carry abuse_takedown's safety+lying-success+retry-hole combo. **⚠️ NEXT:** the write-drop sweep across LIVE-safety paths is now done; resume fresh behavioral audits (`media.ts` upload/generate write paths — has {error}-checked inserts but audit the stock-save + video-callback paths; `ai_workflows.ts` orchestration) OR the deferred deployable waves.

**iter 138 — ✅ executed the iter-137 NEXT(a) → fixed the SIBLING (inverse) entitlement-drift: a PAST_DUE org kept its paid site-features tier (worker deploy `53d20827`).** `features.ts:394 readOrgPlan` — which resolves the site-features tier AND is the toggle handler's **server-side entitlement gate** (`features.ts:498`: `entitled = PLAN_RANK[plan] >= requiredPlan; if (enabled && !entitled) return 403`) — read the latest non-deleted subscription with **NO status filter** (`ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1`). A past_due org (payment failed → `handlePaymentFailed` sets `status='past_due'` but LEAVES `plan='paid'`; the handler's own comment calls a dropped flip a "revenue leak — keeps paid entitlements despite non-payment") resolved to the **`'enterprise'` tier** → could enable EVERY paid site-feature. This is the exact **inverse of iter-137** (there `trialing` was wrongly EXCLUDED from paid; here `past_due`/`canceled` were wrongly INCLUDED) — and it's inconsistent with the build-quota gate, which already drops past_due to free. **SEVERITY: LATENT today** — the SITE_FEATURE_CATALOG was fully removed 2026-08-13 (empty catalog → the toggle 404s every key BEFORE the entitlement gate; only the DISPLAYED tier is currently wrong) — but real drift that goes live the moment the catalog is repopulated. **FIX (SSOT consolidation):** routed `readOrgPlan` through the iter-137 **`resolveActiveOrgPlan`** (`status IN ('active','trialing')`) → past_due/canceled/unpaid → no active sub → `'free'` tier. Now BOTH entitlement planes (build-quota + site-features-tier) resolve through the ONE status-gated resolver + honor the documented past_due-revokes-entitlements intent; the `'paid'→'enterprise'` legacy-value bridge + pro/business/enterprise mapping are preserved. **TDD (+1):** a SQL-capturing regression test — `GET /api/site-features?org_id=` drives `readOrgPlan` → asserts the subscriptions-plan query gates on `status IN ('active', 'trialing')` (guards exactly this leak; `?org_id=` injects the org since readOrgPlan short-circuits to 'free' without one). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · features_routes **16/16** · broad `features|flag|billing|entitlement|advanced_features|experimental` **1015/1015** (69 suites — no ripple) · **DEPLOYED `53d20827`** (Docker up) · homepage 200 + health ok + **`/api/site-features` 200** (readOrgPlan→resolveActiveOrgPlan healthy live; the past_due path is latent + not E2E-reachable → the 1015 tests + the SQL guard are authoritative). commit `f5200c91`. **→ subscription plan-resolution is now fully SSOT'd** — `resolveActiveOrgPlan` is the SINGLE status-gated resolver for BOTH entitlement planes; no raw `SELECT plan FROM subscriptions` remains outside it. **⚠️ NEXT:** iter-136 leftovers — (a) `auth/better-auth.ts:104/112/118` DARK non-atomic 3-insert (same `db.batch` fix when it lights up); (b) non-auth write-drop findings (`api.ts:9227 github_integrations`, `10449 spend_alerts` — read-in-context); (c) the `auth_sessions.ts` vs `auth.ts` session-revoke DEDUP; OR (d) a fresh subsystem (`analytics.ts` funnel/usage, `image_generation.ts`).

**iter 137 — ✅ pivoted off the (saturated) write-drop vein to a fresh subsystem → found + fixed a billing ARCHITECTURAL-DRIFT bug: `trialing` subs were silently dropped to the free quota by 4 duplicated queries that had drifted (worker deploy `7c0d0fba`).** First audited 3 candidates CLEAN (recorded, not bugs): `domains.ts:658` (a self-healing cron status-update — stays `pending` + re-processed next sweep on a dropped write, not a lying-success), the claim build-session store (event-sourced, retry-self-healing), and **`build_limits.ts`** (pure + correct end-to-end — the Stripe webhook writes `subscriptions.plan='paid'`/`'free'` exactly matching `checkBuildLimit`'s `plan==='paid'` gate; comprehensively covered by 4 test files). **Then found the real bug — a `trialing`-handling inconsistency:** the codebase treats a `trialing` subscription as PAID-active in `subscriptionEventType` (emits `subscription.active` → the orchestration plane provisions paid capabilities) AND in `api.ts:2050` (the AI spend-cap grants the paid $50), but **4 IDENTICAL hand-rolled quota queries** (`search.ts:651` + `api.ts:1033/1924/11407`) gated on `status = 'active'` ONLY → a trialing org would get paid capabilities + the paid spend-cap but the **FREE 1-site quota** (half-provisioned/inconsistent). Currently LATENT (the checkout sets no trial → subs start `active`; `trialing` is only reachable via Stripe-side trial config) but genuine drift, and the codebase's own intent is clearly trialing=paid. **FIX (SSOT + DRY + correctness, per the doctrine's "eliminate architectural drift" + "never document both approaches"):** new **`resolveActiveOrgPlan(db, orgId)`** in `build_limits.ts` gating on `status IN ('active','trialing')`; routed all 4 duplicated queries through it (net deletion; `checkBuildLimit` stays pure — the caller still owns plan). This also DRYs 4 byte-identical copies so they can't drift again. **⚠️ RIPPLE (tsc-caught):** removing the local `const sub` broke downstream `sub?.plan` refs in each handler's over-limit audit log → replaced with the resolved `plan`. **TDD RED→GREEN (+2):** `resolveActiveOrgPlan` returns the plan AND asserts the SQL includes `trialing` (regression guard for exactly this drift) + returns null when no active/trialing sub. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · 11 quota suites **144/144** · broad `api|search|billing|create_site|import|quota` **730/730** (47 suites — no ripple) · confirmed only the helper + the SEPARATE `features.ts:401` (site-features pro/business/enterprise tier plane — different query/model) hold a raw subscriptions-plan SELECT · **DEPLOYED `7c0d0fba`** (Docker up) · homepage 200 + health ok + create-endpoint 403-WAF (handler intact; the trialing path is latent + WAF-gated → the 730 tests are authoritative). commit `dced4330`. **⚠️ NEXT:** (a) **`features.ts:401`** reads the LATEST subscription with NO status filter (`ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1`) then maps pro/business/enterprise→tier — audit whether a canceled/past_due sub's stale tier leaks paid site-features (a sibling of this drift, different plane). (b) iter-136 leftovers: `auth/better-auth.ts` DARK 3-insert batch; non-auth write-drop findings (`api.ts:9227 github_integrations`, `spend_alerts`); the `auth_sessions.ts` vs `auth.ts` session-revoke dedup. (c) a fresh subsystem (`analytics.ts`, `image_generation.ts`).

**iter 136 — ✅ executed the iter-135 NEXT → hardened the ENTIRE pre-auth login-path write vein: atomic account creation + credential/state writes that can't lie + single-use magic-link CAS (worker deploy `64f7fb77`).** Read every `auth.ts` login-path write in context; fixed 6 (the highest being an ATOMICITY/data-corruption bug): **(1) `findOrCreateUser` (the top find — doctrine #2 data corruption)** created the first-login account as THREE non-atomic bare-await inserts (users→orgs→memberships): a dropped `users` insert still ran the orgs+memberships inserts (membership FK-references a now-absent user → cascade fail + orphaned org) and returned a `user_id` whose row never persisted → `createSession` then minted a session for a PHANTOM user. **Fixed → a single `db.batch` (implicit txn, rejects+rolls-back — the iter 17-22 atomicity pattern)** → first-login is all-or-nothing. **(2) `createMagicLink` (334)** bare `dbInsert(magic_links)` — the row IS the credential; a drop → a sign-in email mailed for a token with no DB row → "invalid link" dead-end. Throw BEFORE the (correctly best-effort) email. **(3) `createGoogleOAuthState` (483) + (4) `createGitHubOAuthState` (662)** bare `dbInsert(oauth_states)` → redirect the user to the provider with a state that can't validate → "Invalid OAuth state" AFTER they authenticated. Throw BEFORE returning the authUrl. **(5) both callback consume-DELETEs** (`dbExecute DELETE oauth_states`) → capture+warn (best-effort — the auth code is single-use at the provider; don't 500 a good login on a cleanup blip). **(6) `verifyMagicLink` mark-used (451)** was a bare `dbUpdate(used=1)` AFTER a `SELECT used=0` — a TOCTOU race (two concurrent verifies both pass the SELECT → two sessions from one link) + a swallowed error left the link REPLAYABLE within its 24h expiry. **Fixed → an atomic compare-and-swap** (`UPDATE … WHERE id=? AND used=0` + changes check: `error`→throw, `changes===0`→throw "already been used") → genuinely single-use. **TDD RED→GREEN (+8):** findOrCreateUser (existing-user no-write · atomic 3-stmt batch · batch-rejects→throws) · createMagicLink throw · Google+GitHub state throws · verifyMagicLink CAS (already-used + dropped-write). **⚠️ iter-19 RIPPLE handled:** `auth_test_login.test.ts` reached the new `db.batch` path with a bare `{}` mock → gave it `prepare()`/`batch()`. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · auth.test **42/42** · broad `auth|session|sso|claim|oauth|login|magic` **587/587** (44 suites) · all 3 write-drop detectors cleared every real `auth.ts` finding (only the intentional `getSession:1001` fire-and-forget `last_active_at` bump remains, LOW/correct) · **DEPLOYED `64f7fb77`** (Docker up) · **LIVE:** homepage 200 + health ok + **`chaos-3-auth` 6/6 green** (magic-link input, OAuth start, unauthed-bounce, auth-security sessions panel all intact). Batch/CAS happy paths can't be triggered headlessly (need a new-user first-login / a magic-link click) → the 587 unit tests are authoritative. commit `e5a7f2fc`. **⚠️ NEXT:** the auth login-path write vein is now fully hardened + atomic. **iter 135 — ✅ continued the write-drop LOW-finding sweep → found + fixed a HIGH-severity AUTH lying-success vein: session-lifecycle writes claimed success on dropped D1 writes (worker deploy `22d1a104`).** Read the detector's `auth.ts` findings in context (doctrine priority #3 — a dropped auth write breaks login/logout silently, invisible to every render/console gate): **2 REAL bugs + 1 correct-fire-and-forget.** (1) **`createSession` (auth.ts:849, `dbInsert sessions`)** — bare await ignoring `{error}` → on a transient D1 failure it returns a valid-looking token for a session that was **NEVER persisted** → the user "logs in" (token set, "Session created" logged) then **401s on every subsequent request** — a broken-token dead-end with no visible error (callers: magic-link verify + OAuth callback, api.ts:337/436/696/812). (2) **`revokeSession` (auth.ts:941, `dbUpdate sessions.deleted_at`)** — bare await → a failed revoke still logs "Session revoked" + returns void while the session **stays VALID** — a SECURITY lying-success (failed logout / failed revoke-of-a-compromised-session both read as done). (3) **`getSession:921`** (last_active_at bump) = CORRECTLY fire-and-forget (`.catch(()=>undefined)` + "never block auth") — NOT a bug. **Compounded:** `revokeOtherUserSessions` returned `others.length` (the INTENDED count — lied on partial failure) and a mid-loop throw would strand the rest. **FIX (fail-fast for security-critical auth per [[fail-fast-build-fail-soft-prod]] — a session that degrades to a broken token / an un-revoked "revoked" session is worse than an honest 500):** `createSession` + `revokeSession` capture `{error}` + **throw** (callers' error boundary → retryable 500); `revokeSession` does NOT throw on `changes===0` (an already-revoked/absent row is idempotent success — the goal is met); `revokeOtherUserSessions` made resilient (try/catch per session, count ACTUAL revoked, throw-if-any-failed with `revoked/total`). **TDD RED→GREEN (+5):** createSession throws on dropped insert · revokeSession throws on failed revoke + stays-void on changes===0 · revokeOtherUserSessions returns actual count + throws on partial failure. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · auth.test **52/52** · broader `auth|session|sso|claim` **567/567** (43 suites — no regression from the throw) · `check-unchecked-dbinsert`+`dbupdate` no longer flag 849/941 · **DEPLOYED `22d1a104`** (Docker up) · **LIVE:** homepage 200 + health ok + **`chaos-3-auth` 5/5 green** (unauthed→bounce, session-seed, magic-link/OAuth flow intact — the auth middleware + session resolution still work).
