**iter (raw-dbExecute-INSERT lying-success hunt) — ✅ found + fixed a USER-FACING lying-success in `cloneSite`: a dropped `sites` INSERT still 201'd "site cloned" with a phantom id (worker deploy `13792276`).** The recordSpend iter's NEXT: the detectors have a STRUCTURAL blind spot — `check-unchecked-dbexecute.mjs` scopes to UPDATE/DELETE ONLY (raw `dbExecute('INSERT …')` is out of scope), and `check-unchecked-dbinsert.mjs` matches only the `dbInsert(` HELPER — so **raw `dbExecute('INSERT …')` with an unchecked `{error}` falls through BOTH**. Surveyed the tree: ~8 bare (unassigned) raw-INSERT-via-dbExecute calls. **THE BUG (`libs/features/site_clone/service.ts` `cloneSite`):** `await dbExecute('INSERT INTO sites …')` ignored the `{error}` return (dbExecute never throws), then unconditionally `return { id: newId, slug, name, pagesCopied: 0 }` → the handler **201'd "site cloned" with a fresh id that has NO row in `sites`**. A failed INSERT (schema drift / D1 outage) = a phantom clone the user believes exists (user-facing lying-success; cloneSite already throws `source_not_found`/`slug_taken`, so it had the contract to throw — it just didn't on the write). **FIX:** `const { error } = await dbExecute(...); if (error) throw new Error('clone_failed')`; handler maps `clone_failed → 500` (honest error, not a lying 201). Also corrected the test's happy-path mocks (`mockResolvedValue(undefined)` → `{ error: null, changes: 1 }` — the real dbExecute shape). **TDD RED→GREEN (empirically proven):** new test asserts cloneSite throws `clone_failed` when dbExecute returns `{error}`; neutralizing the throw (`error && false`) → the promise **resolves instead of rejecting** → RED; restored → GREEN. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **site_clone 5/5** · feature-drift PASS · deploy `13792276` · homepage/health **200**, clone route reachable (WAF-challenged to curl = expected; flag-gated `batch_operations`) → unit-verified. commit `17bfc467`. **⚠️ NEXT (same class — the raw-INSERT sweep continues):** (1) **`social_oauth.ts:214`** — the OAuth callback does a bare `dbExecute('INSERT INTO social_accounts … ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE')` then `Response.redirect(?connected=platform)` regardless → a failed token-store lies "connected!" with no row. Fix = capture `{error}`, redirect to an ERROR param (NOT throw — it's an OAuth callback; needs the FE's error-return contract). (2) classify the remaining bare raw-INSERTs (`visitor_events_core:33` hot-path beacon = likely intentional fire-and-forget but should capture+warn; `db_shards:68`; `analytics_annotations:15`; `outbound_webhooks:393`; `email_suppressions:57` — compliance). (3) THEN extend `check-unchecked-dbexecute.mjs` to scan raw `INSERT` (error-only class, report-only) so the class is detector-guarded.

**iter (fresh-subsystem write-drop audit) — ✅ found + fixed a DEAD-CATCH write-drop in `build_budget.recordSpend` (the AI-spend budget writer): a dropped spend INSERT under-counted the meter INVISIBLY (worker deploy `6b23fb65`).** Audited the pass-17 NEXT targets. `analytics.ts` CLEAN (fire-and-forget PostHog HTTP capture, no D1 writes, correct no-op-when-unconfigured; only a minor nit: a PostHog non-2xx is silently dropped since the try/catch only catches network throws — acceptable for analytics). `image_generation.ts` = R2 puts, build-pipeline-only (CF-10074 off) — deferred. **THE BUG (`recordSpend`, build_budget.ts):** it wrote `usage_events` via `await dbExecute(...)` inside a `try/catch` — but **`dbExecute` NEVER throws** (its own internal catch swallows the D1 error, logs a generic `d1_exec_error`, and returns `{error}`). So recordSpend's `catch` was **DEAD CODE** for the real failure mode: a dropped spend INSERT (schema drift / D1 outage) silently under-counted the AI-spend budget meter, and recordSpend's OWN rich billing-context warn (org/model/tokens — the useful diagnostic) NEVER fired. This is the exact write-drop/lying-success class the code comment says was "already fixed once" (dbInsert→dbExecute) — the prior fix left the RETURN unchecked, so a NEW failure mode silently recurred. Note: `check-unchecked-dbexecute.mjs` did NOT flag it — the detector treats a `try/catch` wrapper as "handled" (an accepted false-negative per validator-precision-discipline; the try/catch doesn't actually catch dbExecute's non-throwing `{error}`). **FIX:** capture `const res = await dbExecute(...); execError = res.error` (belt-and-suspenders catch kept for a binding-level throw), then fire the rich warn when `execError` — a dropped spend write is now OBSERVABLE with billing context. Stays best-effort (never throws; a spend write must not break the LLM call/build, per the token_burn_meter manifest risk note). **TDD RED→GREEN (empirically proven):** the `throwingDb` test now asserts recordSpend's billing-context warn fires; neutralizing the `{error}` capture (`res.error && null`) → `warned` **undefined** → RED; restored → GREEN. (⚠️ jest here rejects 2-arg `expect(v, msg)` — "Expect takes at most one argument"; kept the message in a comment.) **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **61/61** (build_budget + site_generation_workflow + usage_metering) · deploy `6b23fb65` · homepage/health/served **200** (recordSpend is build-path-internal, not curl-reachable → unit-verified). ⚠️ recordSpend is live only when the build pipeline runs (CF-10074 off) — the fix is correct + ships now so it's live when builds resume. commit `c940bcd9`. **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) TIGHTEN `check-unchecked-db{execute,insert,update}.mjs` to catch the `try/catch`-wrapped-but-unchecked-return pattern (the FN that hid this bug — a `try { await dbExecute(...) } catch` with no `{error}` capture is still a silent drop; needs a fixture for the try/catch case); OR (2) continue fresh-subsystem write-drop audits on the next 0-prior-mention LIVE service.

**iter 134 (billing write-drop) — ✅ fixed a REVENUE-CRITICAL dead-catch write-drop in `LagoProvider.#persistToLedger` — the LIVE DEFAULT billing provider (`BILLING_PROVIDER ?? 'lago'`): a dropped `usage_events` canonical-ledger INSERT was silently swallowed while Lago was STILL billed → ledger↔Lago divergence (worker deploy `5a4a2fe5`).** OBSERVE first RETIRED the queued target: the "voice-CALL session-persist" sibling (iter-143 SMS class) **does NOT exist in this worker** — `voice_orchestrator.ts` header documents the inbound-VOICE path moved to **LiveKit Cloud + `infra/voice-agent`** ("the worker is no longer in the voice audio path, so this module only handles inbound SMS"); `voice_agent.ts` has ZERO `dbUpdate`/`dbInsert`/persist. Pivoted to the write-drop detectors tree-wide (both report **0 HIGH**; raw `dbExecute('INSERT…')` in prod = clean, all test-file). **THE BUG (`billing_provider_lago.ts` `#persistToLedger`):** it wrapped `dbInsert(env.DB,'usage_events',…)` in a `try/catch` whose catch even special-cases `UNIQUE`/`duplicate` — PROVING it assumed `dbInsert` throws. But `dbInsert` (→`dbExecute`) **catches D1 errors internally and returns `{error}`, NEVER throws** (the exact try/catch-ineffective false-confidence class of iter-139 usage_metering + [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]]). So a real D1 failure on the ledger write was swallowed (bare `await` discards `{error}`), the doc's "canonical ProjectSites ledger" silently under-counted, `#persistAndDeliver` STILL called `#deliverToLago` → **Lago rated/invoiced usage absent from our own ledger** (`getUsageSummaryFromLedger` under-reports; `#markDelivered`'s `UPDATE…WHERE id=?` no-ops on a row that never landed). **FIX (fail-closed on the billing ledger):** `#persistToLedger` now returns `Promise<boolean>` — `const {error}=await dbInsert(...)`; `!error`→true (persisted), `/unique|duplicate/`→true (idempotent replay — already in ledger, Lago dedups by transaction_id), else warn(observable)+false. Both `#persistAndDeliver` AND `recordUsageBatch` gate `#deliverToLago` on persist success (never bill via Lago an event D1 has no record of). **TDD RED→GREEN (empirically proven):** new `billing_provider_lago.test.ts` — with the ledger INSERT failing, assert Lago fetch is NOT called (RED today: it WAS called) + batch delivers only persisted events (RED: 2→1); success-path + UNIQUE-replay still deliver (already GREEN). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **30/30** (billing_provider + new lago suite) · `check:dbinsert` billing_provider_lago **1→0** · detector self-tests **58/0** · **DEPLOYED `5a4a2fe5`** + live: homepage/health **200**, `{"status":"ok"}`, bogus `/api` **404** (internal billing logic — no public endpoint exercises the persist→deliver gate; RED→GREEN units are its proof). commit pending. **⚠️ NEXT:** the try/catch-ineffective-around-non-throwing-dbInsert class now swept across usage_metering (139) + Lago ledger (134) — grep other `try{ await dbInsert/dbExecute }catch` sites that assume a throw; OR resume fresh-subsystem audits (`media.ts` stock-save/video-callback, `ai_workflows.ts` orchestration) OR a deferred deployable wave.

Per [[loop-arc-economics]] + the doctrine ("honest checkpoints are the correct default — do NOT force marginal/risky refactors"), this is the correct no-forced-change outcome (verification-only, like iters 51/55/120/133).** **(1) R2-orphan class → CLOSED:** enumerated EVERY `SITES_BUCKET.put` site — build/publish puts (`index.ts`, `api.ts`, `search.ts:1007`, site-generation) use DETERMINISTIC `sites/{slug}/{version}/…` keys (idempotent overwrite on retry, no per-retry orphan); `assets.ts` puts to `uploads/{uploadId}/` STAGING (moved by a workflow, no 1:1 D1 row); `image-generation.ts` uses a deterministic `{concept}.png` key + intentional best-effort row (image usable via the key without the row); `voice_browse_agent` video-put is try/caught (row-without-object, handled); `generatePodcast`/`generateImage`/`saveStockToLibrary` all DELEGATE to the now-fixed `uploadAsset`. **uploadAsset (iter-137) was the ONE live unique-id-keyed 1:1-row instance — class swept.** **(2) `snapshot_restore.ts` → CLEAN** (a model: `head()`-checks the R2 build exists before restoring, captures `{error,changes}` from dbUpdate, handles `changes===0`). **(3) `duplicate_enrich.ts` + `activation_scoring.ts` → 0-caller built-ahead** (not live; per [[dead-code-scan-baseline]] don't chase). **(4) `forms.ts` `POST /api/v1/forms/submit` → HARDENED + DEFENSIBLE:** error-checks the `form_submissions` insert → throws 500 on drop (never a lying-success), reads `reply_email` from `ai_site_settings` (write-source, no drift). It dispatches-to-integrations THEN persists (a persist-first reorder would be more consistent with the iter-123 model + avoid a rare orphan-integration-delivery on persist-fail, but form-submits are low-frequency so it's a genuine Rec, not a forced change). **(5) worker jest → 43 suites / 898 tests GREEN** (no concurrent-session regression). **(6) leaked-`{placeholder}` class → CLOSED:** a concurrent session fixed the one real instance (`bf835083` pricing `{slug}`); every remaining `{slug}`/`{{business}}` is a legit interpolation or intentional API-docs/template-variable placeholder. **(7) copy-quality → CLEAN** (no `[object Object]`/`undefined`/`NaN`/`lorem`/TODO in shipped frontend copy; marketing HTML lives in R2). **NO code change, NO deploy** (verification-only; forcing a marginal reorder against the doctrine would manufacture value). commit = state only. **⚠️ NEXT (pivot OFF write-integrity — that vein is converged + concurrent-contested):** the concurrent session finding a USER-VISIBLE copy bug (`{slug}` leak) that the write-integrity lens missed signals the fertile ground is now the **VISUAL / COPY / UX / a11y dimension** (best via a browser CHAOS pass), OR a deferred deployable wave (`ag-grid→TanStack`, `social.component` split), OR the `forms.ts` persist-first reorder as a deliberate slice. Do NOT re-hunt the closed write-integrity classes without new evidence.

**CHAOS pass-19 (browser-driven — the iter-138 visual/copy/UX pivot PAID OFF) — ✅ baseline 26/26 green, then a visual/copy probe of the MARKETING surface found a HIGH-VALUE SEO de-index bug: every marketing route served the HOMEPAGE `<title>`/`<meta description>` AND `<link rel=canonical href="https://projectsites.dev/">` → the canonical=home DE-INDEXED /pricing, /search, /auth/*, /privacy, etc. (worker deploy `6ec4e2f3`, live-verified).** Baseline chaos-1/2/3/5 = **26 green** (no regression). A `_vprobe` over the M1 new-customer marketing routes (`/`, `/pricing`, `/classic`, `/search`, `/auth/sign-in`, `/auth/sign-up`) flagged `/pricing` + `/auth/sign-up` serving the DEFAULT homepage `<title>` — `curl` (crawler view) confirmed **ALL** marketing routes shipped the IDENTICAL server-rendered title + description + `<link rel=canonical href="/">`. **ROOT CAUSE:** the worker serves the static `marketing/index.html` (hard-coded homepage meta) verbatim for every route; it already injects per-route JSON-LD + a per-route `pageUrl`, but NEVER rewrote `<title>`/`<meta description>`/`<link canonical>`/`og:*`. The Angular client `MetaService` fixes titles POST-hydration — invisible to non-JS crawlers/unfurlers. The canonical=home on every non-home route tells Google the homepage is their canonical → **de-indexes them** (the frontend doctrine SAYS "per-route `<head>` is owned server-side by the Worker's HTMLRewriter" but that pass DIDN'T EXIST). **FIX:** new PURE `applyMarketingMeta(html, path, pageUrl)` in `marketing_routes.ts` (co-located with the known-route SSOT) — ALWAYS rewrites `<link canonical>` + `og:url` to the route's own `pageUrl` (the de-index fix, applies to every route), + `title`/`description`/`og:title`/`og:description` from a new `MARKETING_META` map (mirrors frontend `PAGE_META`; titles ≤60, descs 120-160; covers the gaps /pricing, /search, /classic, /auth/sign-up the client map lacked). Wired into the worker's marketing serve. **TDD RED→GREEN (Python no-op toggle proved it):** +4 in `marketing_routes.test.ts` — canonical/og:url rewrite, title/desc/og rewrite, home-keeps-default, SEO length-gate. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **31/31** (marketing_routes) + **177** across marketing/serve suites (incl. `marketing_serve_injection`) · **DEPLOYED `6ec4e2f3`** · **LIVE (curl, the crawler view):** `/pricing`→canonical `…/pricing` + "Pricing — Plans for Your AI-Built Website"; `/search`→`…/search`; `/auth/sign-up`→`…/auth/sign-up` + "Sign Up — Build Your AI Website Free"; `/privacy`→`…/privacy` (correct `&amp;` escaping); home unchanged; health 200. **Every route is now self-canonical with its own title.** commit `dea13039`. **⚠️ NEXT:** (1) `MARKETING_META` (worker) + `PAGE_META` (frontend) are TWO maps for one concept — a future slice should extract to `packages/shared` (one SSOT both import) to prevent drift (cf. the ai_site_settings↔sites lesson). (2) The worker still doesn't rewrite `og:image` per-route (all routes share one OG card) — per-route OG images are a real polish. (3) Continue the visual/copy/UX/a11y CHAOS dimension (it just yielded a real bug) — sweep the SERVED-SITE output, the auth/2fa flow, keyboard-a11y, or a deferred wave.

**iter 139 (complete the pass-19 per-route SEO meta — twitter cards + sitemap) — ✅ closed the two gaps the pass-19 canonical/og fix left: `twitter:*` cards + `/pricing` in the sitemap (worker deploy `acf29c77` + surgical R2 sitemap upload, both live-verified).** OBSERVE (the queued #1 "extract MARKETING_META + PAGE_META to a shared SSOT"): the FRONTEND does NOT import `@project-sites/shared` (only mirror-COMMENTS; no shared dep in package.json, no tsconfig path — a deliberate mirror-not-import pattern, cf. `email.ts` mirrors `emailSchema`). A shared-import SSOT would need a risky cross-build config change, and the drift is LOW-impact (the worker is now AUTHORITATIVE for crawlers; the client MetaService is cosmetic post-hydration) → NOT worth forcing. Instead verified pass-19 was COMPLETE by curling the crawler artifacts — found TWO real gaps: **(1) `twitter:title`/`twitter:description` were STILL homepage copy on /pricing** — `applyMarketingMeta` rewrote `property="og:*"` but `name="twitter:*"` are SEPARATE tags → an X/Twitter share of /pricing showed the HOMEPAGE card. **(2) `sitemap.xml` was MISSING /pricing entirely** — a key conversion page with no crawl signal (compounds the canonical bug even after the pass-19 fix). **FIX:** (1) extended `applyMarketingMeta` to also rewrite `twitter:title`+`twitter:description` (mirror the og rewrites); (2) added `/pricing` (priority 0.9) to `frontend/public/sitemap.xml`. **TDD RED→GREEN (Python twitter-regex-break toggle):** +1 in `marketing_routes.test.ts` (twitter card rewrites to the route copy; homepage twitter copy gone; RED when the twitter regex is broken). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **32/32** (marketing_routes) · **DEPLOYED worker `acf29c77`** + **surgical R2 upload** of `marketing/sitemap.xml` (`wrangler r2 object put …` — no frontend build/overlap risk per [[feedback_deploy_r2_reliability]]) · **LIVE:** `/pricing` `twitter:title`="Pricing — Plans for Your AI-Built Website" (was homepage) + per-route twitter:description; `sitemap.xml` now lists `<loc>…/pricing</loc>`; health 200. commit `8b0a2801`. **⚠️ NEXT (SEO/crawler dimension still has clean follow-ups):** (1) per-route `og:image` — all routes STILL share ONE OG card (a /pricing share shows the homepage image); needs per-route 1200×630 cards (bigger — media pipeline). (2) `/blog/:slug` posts fall through to the homepage title (no MARKETING_META entry for dynamic posts) → the worker should inject the POST's title/desc from its data. (3) the MARKETING_META↔PAGE_META dual-map (low-impact, deferred). OR continue the visual/copy/UX/a11y CHAOS dimension.

**iter 141 (queue-fallback contract drift — RESOLVED the "is V2 legacy?" next-target AND fixed a latent published→404 in the fallback) — ✅ the `runSiteGenerationWorkflowV2` deletion investigation resolves to KEEP (it's the queue-fallback generator, not dead), and the fallback's producer/consumer contract had a real slug-drift bug (worker deploy `64777626`).** OBSERVE traced V2's sole caller: `default.queue()` (index.ts:1543), the consumer for `generate_site` jobs. `search.ts:749` is `if (SITE_WORKFLOW) {create()} else if (QUEUE) {send()}` — so V2 is the **deliberate graceful-degradation generator** for when the container Workflow binding is absent (local/degraded), NOT legacy. (Both QUEUE bindings are commented out in wrangler.toml → dormant today, but WIRED — deleting would remove the fallback; cf. [[knip-unused-not-always-dead]]. Last iter's V1 deletion was still correct: V1 had 0 callers, fully superseded by V2.) Ran the full detector suite tree-wide first: **ALL green** (idor-handlers/idor-gates/status-sweep/trialing/system-org/unwired/duplicate-routes/hardcoded-admin/local-error-helpers clean; 3 unchecked-write detectors 0 HIGH). **THE BUG (producer/consumer contract mismatch on the dormant-but-wired fallback):** the producer (`search.ts` QUEUE.send) **omitted `slug`, `business_address`, `business_phone`** — but the site row was inserted with the AUTHORITATIVE `slug` from `ensureUniqueSlug` (may carry a `-N` uniqueness suffix). The consumer then recomputed slug from `business_name` (`payload.slug ?? payload.business_name`) → uploaded the build to `sites/${wrong-slug}/${version}/…` while the serving path resolves by the D1 `sites.slug` → the "published" site **404s**; address/phone were also dropped from V2 research; and the consumer's `dbUpdate(status='published')` ignored `{error}` (a lying-success — bundle in R2 but row still 'generating'; the exact index.ts:1599 detector `low`). **FIX:** producer forwards `slug`+`business_address`+`business_phone` (matching SITE_WORKFLOW.create's params); consumer uses `payload.slug` VERBATIM (no re-slugify of a canonical slug) + checks the `dbUpdate` return (throw on error → catch calls `message.retry()`). **TDD RED→GREEN (proven):** +1 in `search_routes.test.ts` asserting the queue message carries authoritative `slug`+`business_address`+`business_phone` (RED: received msg had only `{job_name,site_id,business_name,google_place_id,additional_context}` → GREEN). Existing queue-send tests used loose `objectContaining` subsets → never caught the drop. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **44/44** (search_routes+ai_workflows) + **90/90** neighbors (create_site_quota+site_serving+malformed) · `check:dbupdate` index.ts **16→15** (1599 resolved) · **DEPLOYED `64777626`** · smoke: `/` + `/api/health` **200** + HSTS, create-from-search **403** (wired, not 500), bogus `/api` **404** (fallback is dormant/unexercisable live — edits touch only the `else if(QUEUE)` branch + dormant consumer, so hot-path behavior is provably unchanged; RED→GREEN unit is the proof). commit `1a050744`. **⚠️ NEXT:** the queue-fallback is now contract-correct; a stronger guard would be a shared queue-message Zod schema imported by BOTH producer (validate before send) and consumer (parse on receive) so the contract can't silently drift again — but it's dormant, so lower-priority than the deferred deployable waves (ag-grid→TanStack, create→build engine #1) or the visual/copy/UX/a11y CHAOS dimension.

**iter 142 (CHAOS pass — full suite 68/68 green → EXPLORED the under-covered create funnel (M2) → found business search DEAD in prod + an info-leak; fixed the leak, surfaced the P0) — ✅ scrubbed a raw-upstream-provider-error leak from `/api/search/*` (worker deploy `1f69112b`); the business-search outage itself is a Brian-gated GCP-billing P0.** Ran all 5 chaos journeys vs prod: **68/68 green** (chaos-1/2/5 = 19, chaos-3/4 = 49) — deeply converged, so pivoted to browser EXPLORATION of the thinnest journey (chaos-2-builder, the M2 create funnel). Drove the funnel live (Playwright, real Chromium): the 3-step wizard (Business/Details/Brand-assets) validates honestly ("Create site" enabled-with-empty → `submitBuild()` toasts "Business name is required" + jumps to step 1; "Auto-Populate" gated on a name — both correct, NOT dead-ends). **THE FINDING (business-result semantic the suite never asserted):** typing "Starbucks" → `/api/search/businesses` returns **`200 {data:[], _error:{code:'SEARCH_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE', status:403}}`** + the UI shows `business-search-unavailable`. Root cause: `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY` (`AIzaSyCaVv…`, same in get-secret AND deployed) is **403-denied on BOTH Google APIs — GCP BILLING IS DISABLED** (legacy: "You must enable Billing…"; new: `PERMISSION_DENIED`). So the create funnel's marquee "search→auto-fill" feature is DEAD in prod (degrades honestly to manual entry, which is why 68/68 stayed green — [[graceful-degradation-hides-outages-from-monitoring]]). **P0 fix is Brian-gated (billing=money, approval-required):** enable billing at console.cloud.google.com/project/_/billing/enable. Nominatim fallback TESTED + REJECTED (returns chains but `[]` for small local businesses — the actual customer). **THE AUTONOMOUS FIX I shipped — an info-leak:** `/api/search/*` piped the RAW upstream Google body into the client `_error.message` at 3 sites (business + address×2) → leaking "enable Billing…", GCP console URLs, `PERMISSION_DENIED` JSON, provider identity (OWASP info-disclosure; the raw text was ALREADY logged server-side). Scrubbed all 3 → generic "…temporarily unavailable"; kept `code`+`status`. **TDD RED→GREEN:** strengthened both provider-unavailable route tests to assert `_error.message` never matches `/billing|permission|google|cloud|{/` (RED: was the billing body) → GREEN; added a chaos-2 BROWSER test locking no-5xx + no-leak + honest-affordance live. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **37/37** (search_routes) · **DEPLOYED `1f69112b`** · **LIVE-verified** both endpoints now return the generic message (`LEAK: False`) · new chaos-2 test GREEN vs prod. commit `65958728`. **⚠️ NEXT:** (a) **surface the GCP-billing P0 to Brian** (business search + auto-fill dead until billing enabled — the single biggest create-funnel gap). (b) The upstream-error-leak class is worth a tree-wide grep: any `fetch(vendor)` → `_error.message`/`error.message: <rawUpstreamBody>` passthrough (Stripe/Resend/RDAP/etc.) — same OWASP scrub. (c) Chaos suite is 68/68 + the M2 funnel is now browser-asserted; remaining frontiers = the create→build engine (CF-10074-blocked) + served-site interactive surfaces (contact form round-trip to the owner inbox, per [[contact-form-leads-land-in-unread-contacts-table]]).

**iter 143 (upstream-error-leak class — TREE-WIDE SWEEP → 4+ more live instances at the OAuth callbacks; fixed at the SINK) — ✅ closed the iter-142 NEXT: swept the raw-upstream-provider-error-to-client class tree-wide, found + fixed 4+ live leaks through the OAuth callbacks (worker deploy `15ba1c6c`).** OBSERVE: the exact high-severity variant (raw `.text()` body → client `c.json` message) was search.ts-only (fixed 142; all 51 other `await X.text()` captures are log-only). But the broader class — a caught upstream `err.message` echoed to the client — had MORE: the OAuth exchange adapters bake the RAW upstream body into the thrown error (`mcp_client.ts:773` airtable `${await res.text()}` UNCAPPED, `:988` vercel; `social_publishers/twitter.ts:106` + `linkedin.ts:53` `${(await res.text()).slice(0,200)}`), and the callbacks echoed `err.message` straight to the client (`mcp_oauth.ts:216/262` vercel+generic, `social_oauth.ts:228` → `c.json({error:{message}},502)`) → leaking invalid_grant reasons, token hints, internal URLs, provider bodies. **Verified already-safe (NOT leaks, so left alone):** `error_handler.ts` (the central sink — unknown errors → generic "Internal server error"; typed AppError/Zod messages are author-safe by design); the google-drive callback (`api.ts:10172`) REDIRECTS on failure (raw error console-logged, never client); social-publish PUBLISH raw-body throws (tiktok/facebook/bluesky) go to the background workflow status/log, not a sync client response. **FIX at the SINK (defense-in-depth, robust to every current+future adapter — scrubbing each throw site is whack-a-mole):** the 3 callback catch-blocks now `console.warn` the raw error server-side + return a GENERIC client message ("Connection failed — please try…") + stable `code:'OAUTH_EXCHANGE_FAILED'`. **TDD RED→GREEN:** new `social_oauth_routes` test asserts a raw-body exchange error never reaches the client (RED: received the full `twitter_exchange_failed:400:{…invalid_grant,secret_hint…}` body → GREEN generic); UPDATED the two existing 502 tests that had LOCKED the leak (`toMatch(/invalid_grant/)`, `/bad_verification_code/`) to the generic contract. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **48/48** (social_oauth + mcp_oauth) · **DEPLOYED `15ba1c6c`** · smoke: `/`+`/api/health` 200, both callbacks live (invalid-state → clean 400 `OAUTH_EXCHANGE_FAILED`/`state expired`, not 500 — routes wired; the leak-scrub is unit-proven since a real upstream exchange failure isn't triggerable without a valid state + failing OAuth). commit `34f9ec5a`. **⚠️ NEXT:** the upstream-error-leak class is now SWEPT + CLOSED (search + oauth sinks; error_handler + google-drive + publish-workflow verified safe). A cheap regression FENCE could grep for `c.json(.*err(or)?\.message` in route handlers that AREN'T the central error_handler (the sink pattern), but the class had few instances so a detector risks false-positives ([[validator-precision-discipline]]) — lower priority. Pivot to a fresh vein: served-site interactive surfaces (contact-form owner-inbox round-trip [[contact-form-leads-land-in-unread-contacts-table]]), or a deferred deployable wave.

**iter 144 (served-site lead-capture — CAUSALLY VERIFIED the flow works + LOCKED the contacts-row shape; the owner READ-surface is the remaining design-gated gap) — ✅ real-flow verification + regression fence (test-only, no deploy — the live write was proven in prod D1).** OBSERVE (the iter-143 NEXT — contact-form owner-inbox): the generated-site contact form (`search.ts:1249`) persists a durable `contacts` row (schema `0531_contacts_core.sql` — insert cols all match: org_id/site_id/name/email/phone/source/metadata, no column-drop bug) and the insert IS error-checked (persist-first, never a lying-success). `leads.component` is the SUPER-ADMIN outbound lead-SCANNER (`/api/admin/leads`, Places no-website scan) — a DIFFERENT concept, not the inbound contact inbox. `contacts` has ZERO detail-read endpoints (only a COUNT bySource in analytics) → the owner can't REVIEW captured leads in detail. Detector suite ALL green (idor/status-sweep/trialing/system-org/unwired/duplicate-routes; 3 write-detectors 0 HIGH — the new dbInsert findings are better-auth DARK + concurrent github/spend, all low). **THE ACTION (loop real-flow mandate [[feedback_loop_verifies_real_flows_not_programs]] + [[verify-against-source-of-truth]]):** causally proved the marquee lead-capture flow END-TO-END — in-page fetch (curl is WAF-challenged) `POST /api/contact-form/acme-bakery`→**200 {success:true}**, and the `contacts` row **LANDED in prod D1** (org='e2e-test-org', site='e2e-site-1', source='form', metadata={message,slug}); cleaned up the test row. Then FENCED it: the existing persist-first tests use a generic D1 stub that never asserts the INSERT SHAPE (a refactor could silently change source/drop site_id/corrupt metadata → break lead attribution + analytics bySource, all tests green). Added a SQL-capturing-stub test locking the authoritative shape (1 contacts INSERT, org+site scoped, source='form', message+slug in metadata). **TDD RED→GREEN (proven):** perl-neutralized `source:'form'→'BROKEN'` → RED (Expected "form", Received "BROKEN") → restored (search.ts byte-identical) → GREEN. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **81/81** (contact_form+contact+search_routes) · search.ts UNCHANGED (test-only). commit `c0f838fb`, pushed. NO deploy (zero runtime change; live behavior already prod-verified). ⚠️ Concurrent session owns `social.ts`/`social_routes.test.ts` (left untouched). **⚠️ NEXT (Rec — DESIGN-GATED, needs Brian's call):** give site owners a way to REVIEW captured contact leads — either a `GET /api/contacts` + a new `/admin/leads`-style inbox reading `contacts`, OR sync form-source contacts into the Twenty CRM (`crm.projectsites.dev`) where owners already review, OR unify with `/admin/forms`. **iter 145 (CHAOS pass — full suite 69/69 green → EXPLORED the served customer site (M1) → found + fixed a CROSS-TENANT SEO leak) — ✅ platform discovery routes (robots/llms/accessibility/.well-known) leaked onto EVERY customer subdomain; gated them (worker deploy `b5de72c2`).** Ran all 5 chaos journeys vs prod: **69/69 green** (deeply converged), so browser-EXPLORED the most under-covered frontier — the SERVED customer site itself (chaos-1 only tests the marketing homepage + funnel endpoints, never a real generated site's UX). Findings: (a) `e2e-test-org` seed sites (acme-bakery/green-thumb) are `published` in D1 but have NO R2 bundle → honest **503 "Site unavailable"** (correct degradation, not a bug); (b) **megabytespace (Brian's real site) serves clean** — 200, 34 imgs 0 broken, 5 JSON-LD, per-route canonical/og:url correct (the iter-118 de-index fix holds), all sub-pages 200, unknown path 404. **THE BUG:** `megabytespace.projectsites.dev/robots.txt` + `/llms.txt` served **byte-identical PLATFORM content** (`Sitemap: https://projectsites.dev/sitemap.xml`, "Project Sites" branding) — SHADOWING the site's OWN R2 robots (which points to `megabyte.space/sitemap.xml`). Root cause: `features.use('*')` host middleware was a **NO-OP** (both branches `return next()` — computed `isMarketingRoot`, acted on nothing), so all 6 platform-discovery routes (robots/llms/llms-full/accessibility/.well-known/mcp+oauth) served on EVERY host. **FIX:** real `isMarketingHost(c)` — reads `new URL(c.req.url).hostname` (HTTP/2-safe; the `Host` header is absent under `:authority` + in Hono's `.request()` harness, which broke both the fix AND the tests until I switched to URL-hostname) — gates each discovery handler; customer subdomain → `return next()` → falls through to site-serving → serves THAT site's own copy (or honest 404). Left `/api/openapi.json` un-gated (host-agnostic API spec). **TDD RED→GREEN:** `features_routes.test.ts` mounts `features` + a sentinel `app.all('*')`, asserts customer-host discovery routes fall through while apex still serves (RED PROVEN — neutralized a guard → the robots customer-subdomain test failed 1/28). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **28/28** (features_routes) · **DEPLOYED `b5de72c2`** · **LIVE:** `megabytespace/robots.txt` now serves `Sitemap: https://megabyte.space/sitemap.xml` (its OWN, text/plain), `/llms.txt`→404 (build has none, correct — not the platform's), apex `projectsites.dev/*` unchanged 200. commit `139c86f7`. ⚠️ ⚠️ `git checkout src/routes/features.ts` for the RED proof BLEW AWAY the whole fix — had to re-apply all 7 edits (lesson: neutralize with perl+restore-from-backup, NEVER `git checkout` a file mid-fix). **⚠️ NEXT (Rec — generation-gated):** generated sub-pages ship the HOMEPAGE `<title>`/`<h1>`/`<meta description>`/`<og:title>` (canonical/og:url ARE per-route, but title/desc are the homepage's on every page → duplicate-title SEO defect). Root cause = generation quality + `build_validators.ts` checks title/desc LENGTH not per-page UNIQUENESS → needs a paid rebuild + a `validate-unique-page-titles` gate (can't fix at serve time — per-route title is CONTENT). Per [[platform-discovery-routes-leak-onto-customer-subdomains]].

**iter 146 (per-page title-uniqueness build gate — closed the iter-145 NEXT's ACTIONABLE half) — ✅ added `validateUniquePageTitles` to `build_validators.ts` so the homepage-title-on-every-page generation defect is CAUGHT going forward (worker deploy `ac102b71`).** The iter-145 finding (generated sub-pages ship the homepage `<title>`/`<meta description>`) has two halves: the LIVE megabytespace fix needs a paid rebuild (Brian-gated cost, deferred), but the CLASS-PREVENTION half is clean + backend-only + non-contested: the validators checked title/desc LENGTH (`meta.title_length` 50-60, `meta.description_length` 120-156) but NOT UNIQUENESS, so a build where every route has the homepage title PASSED every gate. **NEW `validateUniquePageTitles(files)`:** groups HTML pages by normalized (case+whitespace-collapsed) `<title>` and `<meta description>`; a title shared by ≥2 distinct pages → `meta.title_duplicate` (error), a shared description → `meta.description_duplicate` (warn); single-page builds no-op. Refactored `titleLength`/`metaDescLength` to derive from reusable `titleText`/`metaDescText` extractors (DRY, zero behavior change — length identical). Wired into `validateBuild` (runs in the site-generation `validate-build` step, currently `report` mode → logs to the D1 build audit, non-blocking; flips to `strict` with the rest when the template ships clean). **TDD RED→GREEN:** +5 tests (error on dup title, warn on dup desc, pass on all-unique, no-op single-page, case/whitespace-normalized) — RED proven by neutralizing the dup-title guard (2 tests failed) → restored via `cp` backup (NOT `git checkout` — the iter-145 lesson). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **82/82** (build_validators + secrets, +5) · **DEPLOYED `ac102b71`** · hot path + served-site 200 (build-time validator — no live serving path touched; unit-proven). commit `83882fad`. **⚠️ NEXT:** the title-uniqueness gate catches FUTURE builds; the LIVE sites with homepage-title-on-every-page still need a rebuild (Brian-gated $ — surface as Rec). Flip `build_validators` `report`→`strict` once the template benchmarks are clean (a separate coordinated gate-flip). Fresh veins: served-site interactive surfaces (contact-lead owner-review, design-gated), or a deferred deployable wave (ag-grid→TanStack, create→build engine #1).

**iter 147 (served-site SEO drift guards — added the sitemap↔routes gate; confirmed the iter-145 leak class is FULLY closed) — ✅ new `validateSitemapRoutesExist` build gate (worker deploy `b4ee2fa0`).** OBSERVE first CLOSED the iter-145 leak class: probed all platform paths on a customer subdomain vs apex — `/status` "leaks" but is **INTENTIONAL** (its own comment: "served on every hostname so customers can hit /status on their custom-domain.com/status and see live dependency health"), `/v1/models` is a host-agnostic API (fine, like /api/openapi.json), and `/sitemap.xml`+`/humans.txt`+`/.well-known/security.txt` correctly serve the CUSTOMER's own R2 copy (no leak). The 6 discovery routes gated in iter-145 were the only real leaks → class CLOSED. Detector suite ALL green. Verified megabytespace's sitemap is valid (10 routes, all `megabyte.space`, lastmods present). **THE GAP (same drift-guard class as iter-146's title gate):** `validateSitemapLastmod` proved the sitemap EXISTS + has lastmods, but NOT that each `<loc>` route has a PAGE. A sitemap listing `/services` when the build shipped no `services.html`/`services/index.html` is worse than a 404: `serveSiteFromR2`'s SPA fallback returns the HOMEPAGE shell with **200** for any sitemap-listed extensionless route (its soft-404 guard treats sitemap-KNOWN routes as 200, line ~849) → crawlers follow the sitemap, get 200, index DUPLICATE homepage content under that URL (self-competing, crawl-budget waste). **NEW `validateSitemapRoutesExist(files)`:** flags every sitemap `<loc>` with no dedicated HTML file (`sitemap.orphan_route`, error), mirroring `serveSiteFromR2`'s resolution order EXACTLY (`X/index.html` → `X.html` → flat `a-b.html` for `/a/b`) so zero false-positive vs live serving. Wired into `validateBuild` (report mode). **TDD RED→GREEN:** +4 tests (orphan flagged, all-3-conventions pass, no-sitemap no-op, dedupe) — RED proven by neutralizing the orphan guard (2 tests failed) → restored via `cp` backup. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **77/77** (build_validators, +4) · **DEPLOYED `b4ee2fa0`** · hot path + served-site 200. commit `75e77692`. **⚠️ NEXT:** the build_validators SEO-drift-guard set is now strong (required-files/asset-existence/meta-length/**title-uniqueness (146)**/**sitemap-routes (147)**/canonical/jsonld/h1). Still `report` mode — a `report→strict` flip is the next milestone (coordinated: verify the template + all 5 benchmark builds are clean FIRST, else it fails live builds). Fresh non-SEO veins: served-site interactive surfaces (contact-lead owner-review design-gated), or a deferred deployable wave.

**iter 148 (CHAOS pass — pivoted OFF served-site SEO to authed admin M2/M3 → found a 100%-BROKEN core feature) — ✅ AI domain search (`/admin/domains` "Search with AI") was returning an error for EVERY request; fixed two stacked bugs (worker deploy `833382fc`).** Full suite 69/69 green, so browser-explored the under-CRUD-tested admin sections (domains/seo/deliverability/apps/mcp/voice — chaos-4 never CRUD-tests these). All render alive; deliverability "Check" works (SPF/DKIM/DMARC). **THE BUG:** pressing "Search with AI" → `POST /api/sites/:siteId/domains/ai-search` → **404 "Site not found" for EVERY site** including OWNED (e2e-site-1/2/3/4 + a nonexistent id all 404 identically). Diagnosis: D1 ground-truth confirmed e2e-site-3 EXISTS + owned (readiness 200 same site/key) → ran the handler's exact SELECT vs prod D1 → **`no such column: business_type`**. Root cause = **swallowed-SQL-error-as-404** ([[swallowed-sql-error-masks-schema-drift-as-404]]): the ownership guard `requireOwnedSite(..., 'id, business_name, business_type, business_address')` selected a PHANTOM `business_type` column (sites has business_name/phone/email/address/website — NO type/category col); the SELECT threw, `dbQueryOne` swallowed it → null → 404 EVERY site. The unit test mocked `dbQueryOne` to return a row regardless of columns → 4/4 green while prod was 100% dead. **Fix 1:** select only real cols (default the type context). **Fix 2 (revealed after 1):** search then 502'd — `checkDomainAvailability` (CF Registrar) returns 404 in prod and THROWS `AppError(502)` on 404 (degrades on 5xx), and the handler's soft-failure only caught a non-array RETURN not the THROW → wrapped it in try/catch so availability (secondary) failure degrades to "unknown" and the AI candidates (primary) still ship. **TDD RED→GREEN (both, cp-backup restore):** schema-drift guard asserts the site-lookup SQL never selects `business_type`; +404-throw degradation test asserts 200 not 502. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **5/5** (domains-ai-search) · **DEPLOYED `833382fc`** · **LIVE causal:** e2e-site-3 ai-search **404→200** with real candidates (fitnessgym.com…), nonexistent site still 404 (IDOR intact). commit `de93d603`. Also compacted MEMORY.md 20.6→17.0KB (hook-flagged). **⚠️ NEXT:** (a) the swallowed-SQL-error-as-404 class has now bitten twice (this + the prior swallowed-SQL memory) — a detector could grep `requireOwnedSite`/`dbQueryOne` SELECT column-lists against the live `sites` schema, but it's schema-coupled (harder). (b) The CF Registrar availability API 404ing GLOBALLY is a deeper issue (the public Claim-modal search uses RDAP + works; `checkDomainAvailability` = CF Registrar = broken) — worth a root-cause look at why the registrar endpoint 404s (wrong endpoint/params, or account lacks registrar). Per [[ai-domain-search-phantom-column-404-and-registrar-502]].

**iter 149 (closed iter-148's NEXT-(b) — root-caused WHY `checkDomainAvailability` 404s globally, rewired it to RDAP → availability now WORKS live) — ✅ worker deploy `15cdd793`.** iter-148 fixed ai-search's ownership-404 + wrapped the 502-throw so the search 200s, but availability was still 100% `unknown` (every domain read "unavailable"). **ROOT CAUSE:** `checkDomainAvailability` hit CF Registrar `GET /accounts/{id}/registrar/domains/check?names=…` — that endpoint is **NOT a public bulk-availability API**. The registrar token only manages domains the account ALREADY OWNS, so an arbitrary-domain check 404s → the fn threw `AppError(502)` on EVERY call. Verified by direct probe (403/404, no public endpoint exists). **FIX:** rewired `checkDomainAvailability` to route through RDAP `checkBatch` (`rdap_availability.ts`) — the SAME keystone the public Claim-modal search (`/api/domains/search`) + `domain_suggester` + domain-picker already rely on. Removed the CF fetch + 5xx-soft-fail + 502-throw; now `available = rdap status==='available'` (conservative: `taken` AND transient `unknown` → false), pricing from the per-TLD table. Fixes BOTH callers in one edit: `POST …/domains/ai-search` (availability enrichment) + `GET …/domains/availability` (single-domain probe, api.ts:9979). **Reworked `domains-ai-search.test.ts`:** `jest.mock('../services/rdap_availability.js', {checkBatch})` (dynamic-import in domains.ts resolves to the same module → mock applies); success test now asserts real taken/available discrimination + `checkBatch` called once with the deduped candidate list; the old "5xx" test → "all-unknown" (checkBatch never throws); the old "404-throw" test → checkBatch-REJECTS (handler try/catch swallows → 200, availability is secondary). KEPT the iter-148 SQL schema-drift guard. Updated stale CF-Registrar JSDoc/comments (domains.ts fn doc + api.ts handler + single-domain route doc → RDAP). **TDD RED→GREEN (cp-backup restore, NOT git-checkout):** neutralized the RDAP availability mapping (`status==='available'` → `false`) → ONLY the availability-tags success test failed (other 4 green) → restored clean. **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · jest **5/5** (domains-ai-search) · **DEPLOYED `15cdd793`** · **LIVE causal** (`GET /api/sites/e2e-site-3/domains/availability` as e2e-test-org): `google.com`→**available:false** (taken), `qzxkvmbht7wplf.com`→**available:true** (unregistered) — DISCRIMINATES correctly (was 502-on-every-call before). Baseline via public `/api/domains/search`: fresh brandable → **9/10 TLDs `available`**, only `.xyz` flaky `unknown` → RDAP-from-Worker works. commit `PENDING`. **⚠️ RESIDUAL (pre-existing, NOT this regression):** some available domains intermittently return `unknown` under transient registry/egress pressure (`.xyz` flaky; one `.com` momentary → cached 60s) — checkBatch's honest degradation per [[cf-worker-egress-rate-limited-by-cf-fronted-third-party]]; the feature is now CORRECT (real taken/available), just occasionally conservative on a flaky-registry TLD. The ai-search **POST** is WAF-managed-challenged for headless curl (GET passes with Bearer+UA); its distinct AI-fan-out+soft-fail logic is unit-covered, and it shares the now-live-verified `checkDomainAvailability` — the shared browser was held by a concurrent session so I proved it via the GET path + units rather than contend. **⚠️ NEXT:** `registerDomain`/`purchaseDomain` STILL use the CF Registrar API for ACTUAL registration — that IS the registrar's real job (buying a domain the account will own), so it's likely fine, but the purchase flow is unverified end-to-end (needs a real Stripe checkout + a domain the account can register) — a Brian-gated $ probe. Otherwise the deferred waves stand.

**iter 151 (CHAOS pass — full chaos suite 69/69 GREEN vs prod; extended coverage → found + fixed an ignored MCP-connections scope filter) — ✅ worker deploy `fcbd4ae1`.** Ran all 5 chaos journeys vs prod (chromium): 69/69 pass (no journey regression). **iter 152 (applied the ignored-filter finder tree-wide → class CONFIRMED contained; mapped the apps-catalog tangle; reclassified a mis-labeled duplicate-route allowlist entry) — ✅ build-gate change (no deploy).** OBSERVE applied [[accepted-but-ignored-filter-param-serves-unscoped-data]] finder across all `c.req.query('…_id'|'scope'|'status'|'provider')` route reads: **voice.ts** (5 `siteId` reads — all require + `requireSiteMembership` + bind ✓), **env_vars.ts** (`GET /api/env-vars` + `/export` forward scope/siteId/mcpProvider/endpointId/agentId to `listEnvVars` which binds ALL ✓), **super_admin.ts** (`q` via `sanitizeLikeTerm` + `status` allowlist, bound ✓) — the ignored-filter class is **isolated to the mcp fix (iter 151)**; siblings clean. Also RECONCILED (verify-against-source-of-truth): **analytics** daily/sections/funnel all agree (conversions=1 everywhere); **credits ledger** sums PERFECTLY (Σ amount=111=balance, every `balance_after` continuous); **dead-shadow routes** — 0 normalized-path overlaps between api.ts's 117 routes and feature-module routes (readiness was the only one, deleted iter 150). **THE FINDING — apps-catalog three-source tangle (no live impact, documented):** THREE catalogs — the frontend `/admin/apps` UI + apps.ts `/api/apps/catalog/:id` DETAIL + `src/data/apps-catalog.ts` ALL use the MARKETPLACE catalog (umami/open-webui/… self-host), but `index.ts:578` (`app_launcher` feature) registered `/api/apps/catalog` FIRST and SHADOWS the marketplace list (`apps.ts:159`) with the CONTAINER catalog (plane/twenty/chatwoot, shape `{data}` vs marketplace `{apps}`). The sole API consumer `ApiService.getAppCatalog()` (expects `{apps}`) is **UNCALLED** → the frontend reads bundled data → **ZERO live user impact**. The existing `scripts/check-duplicate-routes.mjs` gate already grandfathers this dup but MIS-LABELED it `intentional` (a clean permanent override, like domain_purchase). **DELIVERABLE:** reclassified it `intentional`→**`review`** (its taxonomy for "tangled shadow pending resolution") + accurate comment + updated the detector's unit test. **VERIFY:** detector unit test **11/11**; `node check-duplicate-routes.mjs --ci` **exit 0** (both dups grandfathered — build UNBROKEN); the reclassify is label/tracking only (both `intentional`+`review` are allowlisted → no pass/fail change). No worker/frontend runtime change → no deploy (build-gate ships via commit per [[verification-loop]] self-application). commit `PENDING`. **⚠️ NEXT — resolve the apps-catalog tangle (needs the owner decision, likely the concurrent app_launcher session):** pick ONE owner for `/api/apps/catalog` — either (a) DROP `index.ts:578` (app_launcher's list is vestigial: `getAppCatalog()` uncalled, frontend uses bundled data) → un-shadows the marketplace `apps.ts:159` which matches `getAppCatalog()`'s `{apps}` shape + the detail + the frontend; then delete the allowlist entry; OR (b) move app_launcher's container catalog to a distinct path (`/api/app-launcher/catalog`). Both touch app_launcher (concurrent territory) + `apps_routes.test.ts` covers apps.ts:159 in isolation. NOT sliced this iter to avoid fighting the in-flight app_launcher migration. Deeper: `APPS_CATALOG` (apps.ts) + the frontend `apps-catalog.data.ts` are DUPLICATE marketplace definitions — consolidate to one SSOT when the owner is settled.

**iter 153 (deepened convergence verification; sharpened the apps-catalog next-target; drift-fixed stale comments) — ✅ no runtime change (comment/test-doc only).** MORE clean sweeps (backend data layer is DEEPLY converged): **D1-LIKE-injection class CONTAINED** — every `%${…}%` uses `sanitizeLikeTerm`/`boundLikePattern` (search/super_admin/media/log_query/ai_admin_features); the bare `LIKE ?` binds (api.ts:2775, audit.ts:174) wrap a VALIDATED site id (`siteRow.id` / owned `siteId`, `[a-z0-9-]` → no wildcards) — safe. **Orphaned-route class** — the frontend `/admin/X` strings are API paths via `ApiService` (prepends `/api`), NOT routerLinks; `/api/admin/api-keys` + `/api/admin/notifications` both exist + return real JSON (healthy; the concurrent `02f982ec` handled the cmdk `/admin/sites`+`/media` dead-ends). **Duplicate-route detector is ALREADY a hard gate** (`check:duplicate-routes` in `npm run check`). **SHARPENED the apps-catalog next-target (still deferred — genuine PRODUCT-INTENT ambiguity, NOT autonomously resolvable):** the tangle is `index.ts:578` (app_launcher, container apps, `{data}`, flag-gated) shadowing `apps.ts:159` (marketplace, self-host apps, `{apps}`, always-on). FIVE sources say the MARKETPLACE owns `/api/apps/catalog` (frontend bundle + apps.ts `:id` detail + `src/data` + `getAppCatalog()`'s `{apps}` shape + `apps_routes.test.ts`), the marketplace was RECENTLY trimmed to 9 curated apps (`594a1300` = actively maintained), and app_launcher's endpoints (578 catalog + 584 launch) are PROVABLY UNCONSUMED by the frontend (`getAppCatalog()` uncalled; no `/apps/launch` caller). BUT this can't disambiguate the two theories: (A) app_launcher is a SEPARATE feature that COLLIDED on the path (→ fix = rename app_launcher to a distinct path, un-shadow the marketplace) vs (B) app_launcher (containers) is INTENTIONALLY REPLACING the marketplace (self-host) (→ the shadow is transitional, a rename REVERTS the migration). An autonomous rename could revert an intentional migration → needs the owner. **DELIVERABLES (safe, no-runtime):** (1) fixed the now-inaccurate iter-151 mcp filter comments in `env-vars-attachment.component.ts` (endpoint DOES honor `site_id`/`provider`; `endpoint_id`/`agent_id` aren't `mcp_connections` columns so those kinds stay org-wide — per drift-detection false-docs-are-drift); (2) annotated `apps_routes.test.ts` GET-catalog describe that it exercises the SHADOWED-in-prod marketplace handler (proves logic, NOT live routing — prevents false confidence). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · **apps_routes 30/30** · frontend **tsc 0**. Comment/test-doc only → no bundle change → no deploy. commit `PENDING`. **⚠️ NEXT:** backend data layer is converged (5+ clean sweeps). The remaining real work is either (a) the apps-catalog product-intent decision (owner/concurrent app_launcher session), or (b) a FRESH FRONTIER (per loop-coverage-ledger: gen-site output quality, editor flows, billing) or the big deferred waves below. **iter 158 (CHAOS pass — triaged the "flaky" cmdk-audit chaos test → root-caused it to a REAL live product bug + fixed it) — ✅ worker deploy `4655eb79`, commit `9d843fbf`.** The `chaos-4-admin` "Cmd+K AI answer lands in the audit trail" test flaked under FULL parallel load (passed in isolation + on retry). Root-cause was NOT a test-timing flake — it exposed an **accepted-but-ignored-filter** bug in `GET /api/audit/rows` (ai_admin.ts:1732): the JSDoc documents `action`/`actor_id`/`target_type`/`from`/`to` query params + `limit` bounds (default 100, max 500), but the SQL bound ONLY `org_id` + limit — **every documented filter silently no-op'd** (a caller narrowing to one action got the whole feed) AND the limit ran default 500 / max 5000 (doc drift). The flake mechanism: the shared `e2e-test-org` audit feed floods with OTHER actions under parallel load → the fire-and-forget `cmdk.ai.answered` row got pushed past the unfiltered `limit=25` poll window before it was seen. **FIX (additive, zero-risk to no-filter callers):** bind each documented filter into WHERE conditionally (`if (action) where.push('action = ?')` …) + align limit to the documented 100/500 bounds. `created_at` is `new Date().toISOString()` (ms precision) so timestamp-collision was ruled out — it was purely the missing filter. **TDD:** +3 unit tests (filter-binding asserts SQL+binds; no-filter path unchanged = still `WHERE org_id = ?`; limit cap 500/floor 100) — **RED-proven** (3 fail against the reverted handler, existing test still green). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · `ai_admin_routes` **50/50** · **DEPLOYED `4655eb79`** · **live-verified** (bogus action→0 rows, `from`=future→0, `to`=past→0, positive action→single-action rows — before the fix bogus would've served the full feed). **Test-hardening (uses the fix):** scoped the chaos poll to `action=cmdk.ai.answered&limit=50` (immune to unrelated-action flooding) + widened window 8s→12s → **full chaos suite 142 passed (71×2 projects), retries=0, 7.9m** (was 1 flake). Memory: [[accepted-but-ignored-filter-param-serves-unscoped-data]]. **⚠️ CORRECTION to the iters-155-157 "surface saturated" narrative:** a fresh probe of an UNAUDITED endpoint (audit/rows filters) DID yield a real live bug — the auto-fixable surface is not fully exhausted; endpoints whose DOCUMENTED contract was never behaviorally tested remain a productive frontier. **Ran the documented-param-vs-WHERE finder tree-wide THIS iter → class CONTAINED:** the only other GET endpoints documenting filter params both bind them CORRECTLY — `audit_trail_export/service.ts:24` `buildAuditQuery` conditionally pushes `action = ?`/`created_at >= ?`/`created_at <= ?` (ironically the flag-gated-DARK audit CSV export did it RIGHT while the LIVE `/api/audit/rows` did it wrong), and `lead_store.ts:152` `listLeads` honors `onlyNoWebsite` (`AND has_website = 0`) + limit/offset. So `/api/audit/rows` was the SOLE live instance. **⚠️ NEXT:** the deferred wave-turns below stand (ag-grid→TanStack; create→build engine); the documented-contract frontier is now swept clean at this depth.

**iter 159 (probed the editor/publish endpoint contracts frontier → found + fixed a SEVERE cross-org site-takeover IDOR, 2 instances) — 🔒 worker deploy `b74d3e0d`, commit `c9079d22`.** Continued iter-158's "documented-contract-never-behaviorally-tested" vein onto the bolt.diy publish endpoints (a named UNTESTED frontier). Confirmed `by-slug/build-context`+`/chat` are intentional-`@auth NONE` (defensible: published site is already public, no-PII, text-only) and `research.json` is correctly org-scoped. **But found a SEVERE live security hole — write-to-attacker-controlled-slug (site takeover/defacement):** (1) **`POST /api/publish/bolt`** (reachable UNAUTHENTICATED — verified: unauthed empty-files POST → 400 from the handler, no auth/flag/WAF rejection) took the target `slug` from the request BODY and, when it named an EXISTING site, overwrote its R2 files + `_manifest.json` current_version + purged its hostname KV with **ZERO ownership check** — any caller could POST `{files:[…evil…], slug:'<victim>'}` and replace another org's LIVE site with arbitrary HTML/JS; `resolvePublishOrgId` even re-indexed the defaced content under the VICTIM's org. (2) **`POST /api/sites/:id/publish-bolt`** verified ownership of the `:id` path param but then wrote to a body-supplied `providedSlug || site.slug` (D1 UPDATE used `:id`, but R2/manifest/cache used the body slug) → an owner of site A could pass `slug:'<victim>'` and deface site B. **FIX:** (1) re-publishing over an existing site row now requires the caller's org to own it (404 non-leak, mirroring the guarded `:id` sibling) — brand-new slugs stay anonymously publishable (intended design preserved); (2) always publish to the verified-owned `site.slug`, ignore the body slug. **TDD:** new `publish_bolt_ownership.test.ts` +5 tests (cross-org block · unauth block · owner-allowed · new-slug-allowed · `:id` force-owned-slug) — **RED-proven** (3 fail with guards neutered, both happy paths pass in BOTH states → fix doesn't break legit flows). **VERIFY:** worker **tsc 0** · related suites **34/34** · eslint **0 errors** (clean `providedSlug` removal) · **DEPLOYED `b74d3e0d`** · **live-verified with blast-radius-minimization** (probed e2e-org's disposable draft `urban-fitness`, not a real customer): unauthed publish-over → **404 `{NOT_FOUND}`** (guard's own JSON) + **0 R2 objects written** to urban-fitness (guard throws before the uploads block). The Angular admin uses the safe `:id` variant; `/api/publish/bolt` is the standalone-editor anonymous path (`app/lib/stores/workbench.ts:807`, `Chat.client.tsx` fallback). **⚠️ DETECTOR GAP (new sub-class, NEXT candidate):** the existing `check-idor-handlers.mjs` (fire 19-20) only covers mutation handlers keyed by a `:id`/`:siteId` PATH param — it does NOT catch a handler that writes to a resource keyed by a **BODY-supplied slug/id** (this bug) or that writes to **R2** (not just D1). A durable guard would grep POST/PUT handlers that derive a write path from `body.slug`/`body.*id` + do an `SITES_BUCKET.put`/`dbUpdate` without an ownership lookup on that body key. Worth a dedicated detector slice. **⚠️ CORRECTION reinforced (iter 158's point):** the editor/publish frontier — NOT the "saturated" backend — yielded the single highest-severity bug of the last 10 iters. Untested contract frontiers >> re-sweeping converged surfaces.

**iter 160 (closed iter-159's DETECTOR GAP → built + wired the body-keyed write-IDOR regression guard; tree-swept clean) — ✅ script-only, no deploy (CLI gate = self-test + `--ci` clean is the verification per verification-loop §CLI-tools), commit `9b6f0d00`.** Turned the iter-159 severe site-takeover fix into a durable CI guard. `check-idor-handlers.mjs` (fire 19-20) is PATH-PARAM granular — blind to the class where the write TARGET is a BODY-supplied slug feeding an **R2** write (not a `:id` path param + D1 write). New **`scripts/check-body-slug-write-idor.mjs`** flags a POST/PUT/PATCH handler that (a) writes site files to R2 under a `${VAR}` slug (`SITES_BUCKET.put(\`sites/${VAR}/…\`)`), (b) derives that slug from the request BODY (`existingSlug`/`providedSlug`/`body.slug`/`slug:` off a `= body` destructure), and (c) has NO ownership idiom — while IGNORING path-param slugs (`c.req.param('slug')` = the intentional-public build-context/chat surface) + the public allowlist (contact-form/public-data/container-upload). Exported pure `scanHandler(path, body)` + main-module guard (matches `check-status-sweep-no-retry.mjs` shape). **Self-test (`scripts/__tests__/check_body_slug_write_idor.test.mjs`, node --test): 6/6** — POSITIVE flags the pre-fix publish/bolt shape; NEGATIVES clear the fix (ownership-gated), verified-`site.slug`, path-param, public-allowlist, and a body-slug-no-R2-write FP class. **Wired** `check:body-slug-idor --ci` into `npm run check` next to `check:idor-handlers`. **Regex-bug caught + fixed during authoring** (validator-precision): the handler-boundary regex matched `SITES_BUCKET.put(\`sites/${slug}/…\`)` as a fake handler → added a leading-`/` route-path filter (R2 keys aren't leading-slash routes). **Tree-wide sweep: CLEAN** — the two iter-159 handlers are the ONLY slug-keyed R2 site-writes; both now gated. **KNOWN GAP (documented in the detector + memory):** the "verify-X-write-to-Y" variant (ownership check on `:id` but write keyed by a different body slug) is CLEARED by (c) — needs data-flow, not regex; the `:id` fix removed the body slug so the tree is clean, and this guard covers the higher-value NO-ownership (unauthenticated) class. Memory [[publish-endpoint-body-slug-write-idor]] updated (detector now SHIPPED). **⚠️ NEXT:** contract-untested endpoint frontier remains the top yield (iters 158-159 proved it); candidates — other R2/KV write endpoints keyed by body input; the media-upload + env-vars-import write paths; OR the deferred wave-turns below.

**chaos-pass (browser-driven TDD vs prod — full 142-test suite ×2 browsers, 4 full runs) — fixed the SUITE-WIDE env-flake class (config-drift + one over-strict test); ZERO product bugs found.** Ran the 5 MASTER journeys at `--retries=0`. **Finding: environmental flakes, NOT product bugs** — every failing test passes single-worker (the memory's reliable verdict). TWO distinct classes: **(A) slow-journey timeout** — the deep persistence journeys (`AI Env Vars` :338 / `AI Endpoints` :1180 / `Timeline Notes` :1439; create → nav → hard-reload → verify → cleanup = 6+ prod navs, ~16-17s single-worker) cumulatively exceed the 30s default only under full-suite parallel contention; which one flakes is random per run. ROOT = **config drift**: `frontend/playwright.prod.config.ts` had NO explicit `timeout` (Playwright's 30s default) even though the sibling WORKER cert config uses 45s (per [[prod-e2e-ci-flakes-are-environmental]]). Fix = **one global `timeout: 60_000`** (not per-test/per-file whack-a-mole — a describe-level patch just moved the flake to a different test/file each run). **(B) auth-tarpit hang** — `chaos-3` password-signin (:336) hung its in-page `fetch('/api/auth/sign-in/magic-link')` to the test ceiling under sustained load (the documented per-IP auth rate-limiter). The fetch was UNBOUNDED + the assertion was over-strict `=== 200`. Fix = bound it (`AbortSignal.timeout(30_000)` → a true stall fails fast, not at the ceiling) + accept **`[200, 429]`** (a 429 under an E2E suite hammering auth is HONEST backpressure, NOT a dead-end — the test's whole point is "no dead-end"; a 5xx/hang/all-user-block still fails). Endpoint is healthy: **direct curl 1.0-1.6s**; the >15s was a rare browser-queue+email-blip anomaly, not a latency bug. Both fixed tests pass single-worker (chaos-3 both projects 11.7s). **Product verification (backend contract probes, the iters-158-160 vein): media surface FULLY CONVERGED + hardened** — every `/api/media/*` write org-scopes via `getOrgScope(c)` (session-derived) + `getAsset(env, orgId, id)` (`WHERE id=? AND org_id=?`): upload/raw/delete/send-to-bolt/stock-save clean; `/raw` ships nosniff+CSP+sandbox+Content-Disposition (user-content XSS); `stock/save` validates `isSafeCrawlUrl` (SSRF); `send-to-bolt` org-scopes before minting; `generate/video` is an honest async placeholder. **So the body-keyed-write frontier (iter-160 NEXT) is CLEAN** — no new IDOR. **VERIFY:** all flaky tests pass single-worker (= 0 deterministic reds = green-in-substance per the established doctrine); final full-suite re-run with the 60s + chaos-3 fix in-flight. Test-infra only → no deploy. **⚠️ NEXT:** the chaos-reachable + backend-probe surfaces are converged; the untested-by-chaos surface is the mutation-heavy **apps-instances** lifecycle (51KB component, install→provision→config) — but live probing has real provisioning side-effects (needs a disposable-instance strategy). Else the deferred wave-turns below. Per [[prod-e2e-ci-flakes-are-environmental]]: do NOT churn the rate-limiter/timeouts further; the environmental tail self-heals on retry.

**iter 161 (built the GET-read-IDOR detector → swept 91 GET-by-id handlers → found + fixed a real marketplace visibility leak) — 🔒 worker deploy `914d0c99`, commit `b8c20b82`.** OBSERVE confirmed the two "frontier" surfaces are EXEMPLARY: the **apps-instances** lifecycle (create has full saga-compensation — rollback on `resolveAppEnv`/`dbInsert` failure; every read/mutation org-scopes via `loadInstance(orgId,id)`; lying-success guards throw before restart/destroy) and **media** (all `/api/media/*` org-scope from session). So I built the missing durable guard: `check-idor-handlers.mjs` is MUTATION-only — GET-by-id **reads** (cross-org data leak) are a documented gap (fire-27 swept 9 manually). New **`scripts/check-get-read-idor.mjs`** flags a GET handler that reads a tenant row by an attacker-suppliable path id with NO org-ownership idiom. **Tree-wide sweep flagged 6** across 91 GET-by-id handlers: 5 FALSE POSITIVES (copilot via `adminGuard`→`assertSiteOwned`; snapshots via `resolveSnapshot(orgId,…)`; copilot.js public widget; catalog — added the helpers to OWNERSHIP + the public paths to the allowlist), and **1 REAL BUG**: `GET /api/templates/:slug` (no-auth) + `POST .../install-template` filtered only `slug=?` while the marketplace LIST enforces `visibility='public' AND status='live'` → a **`private`/`draft`/`archived` template was fully readable + installable by anyone knowing the slug** (metadata + `base_files_r2_prefix` R2 source path) — **list-vs-detail filter drift**. **FIX:** both by-slug lookups now gate `visibility IN ('public','unlisted') AND status='live'` (install additionally allows `author_org_id = <caller>` so an author installs their own private/draft); private/draft → 404 non-leak; public reads unaffected. **TDD:** +3 templates tests (SQL-gate on detail + install, 404-on-null) — RED-proven (detail test fails when the gate is neutered); +6-case detector self-test. **VERIFY:** templates **15/15** · detector self-test **6/6** · tsc 0 · wired `check:get-read-idor --ci` into `npm run check` · **DEPLOYED `914d0c99`** · **live-verified** (`GET /api/templates/nonprofit-warm`→200 public read intact, missing slug→404; 5 prod templates all public so the private-block is structural/latent). Memory [[list-vs-detail-filter-drift-leaks-hidden-rows]]. **⚠️ TEST GOTCHA (cost me ~6 probes):** `expect(sql, 'msg').toMatch(/regex/)` SILENTLY FAILS in this jest setup even when `regex.test(sql)===true` — the 2-arg message form breaks `.toMatch`. Use `.toContain` (normalize whitespace first) or drop the message. **⚠️ NEXT:** the org-IDOR classes (mutation path-param, body-slug write, GET-by-id read) now ALL have durable detectors + are tree-swept clean. Fresh veins: the list-vs-detail-filter-drift class tree-wide (any endpoint whose LIST filters a gate its DETAIL/by-id read drops — grep list `WHERE …status/visibility/enabled` vs the sibling by-id read); else the deferred wave-turns below.

**iter 162 (loop + chaos combined — chaos 142/142 green; found + fixed a calendar body-FK cross-owner IDOR) — 🔒 worker deploy `8057082b`, commit `2a1677c7`.** **Chaos browser pass: 142/142 passed (8.8m, retries:2)** — the 60s config-timeout fix (prior turn) holds; ZERO browser-surface bugs → the 5 MASTER journeys are converged. **Code-level OBSERVE (iter-161 NEXT — list-vs-detail sweep):** the class is CONTAINED — templates was the sole instance (`calendar_events.visibility` exists but no public feed uses it; `mcp_connections status`/`context_files enabled` are org/site-scoped toggles, not public gates). But the sweep surfaced a fresh unexamined feature — **calendar/bookings** (dashboard.ts, migration 0033) — where I found **a REAL cross-owner IDOR: `POST /api/calendar/events` trusted a BODY-supplied `calendar_id` and inserted the event with it WITHOUT verifying the caller owns that calendar** → a user could attach an event to ANOTHER user's calendar. (The `PATCH`/`DELETE` siblings correctly gate `id=? AND user_id=?`; create trusted the body FK.) Latent today — the list read scopes by `user_id` so the victim doesn't SEE it — but a real scoping gap that NEITHER the path-param IDOR detector (untrusted id is a BODY FK, not a path param) NOR the body-slug-write detector (it's a D1 relational write, not an R2 write) can catch. **FIX:** when `calendar_id` is provided, `SELECT id FROM calendar_calendars WHERE id=? AND user_id=?` → 404 (non-leak) when not owned; else the default-calendar path is unchanged. **TDD:** +2 dashboard tests (foreign calendar_id→404+no-insert; owned→201) — RED-proven (disabling the guard → 201 event on the foreign calendar). **VERIFY:** dashboard **39/39** · tsc 0 · **DEPLOYED `8057082b`** · **live-verified** (foreign calendar_id → 404 `{NOT_FOUND}`, nothing inserted). Memory [[body-supplied-fk-injection-needs-ownership-check]]. **Also:** corrected the `POST /api/calendar/bookings` JSDoc (it falsely claimed "Public / No caller auth" — the code has always required auth; it creates the owner's booking-LINK config). **⚠️ KNOWN GAP (documented, NOT built — a dormant feature, not a loop-sized fix):** the calendar-bookings PUBLIC flow is unbuilt — `GET/POST /api/calendar/bookings` return a `public_url: /book/:slug` that **live-404s** (no `/book/:slug` worker/frontend route, no visitor-appointment endpoint; the whole feature is backend-only with no admin UI). Completing it (public page + availability/slot logic + appointment table + email) is a >2h dedicated feature-build for a dormant surface — surfaced as a Rec, not built speculatively. **⚠️ NEXT:** the body-supplied-FK-injection class is a fresh vein — grep create/write handlers that store a body-supplied `*_id` FK (`parent_id`/`calendar_id`/`folder_id`/`campaign_id`/…) without an ownership `SELECT` on that FK; a detector for it complements the 3 existing IDOR guards. Else the deferred wave-turns below. **✅ iter-177 CLOSED the bookings SIBLING** — `POST /api/calendar/bookings` had the IDENTICAL body-`calendar_id` FK injection (iter-162 fixed events but MISSED its sibling create) → an authed user could attach a booking-LINK to another user's calendar. Mirrored the events ownership check (`SELECT id FROM calendar_calendars WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL` → 404 non-leak when unowned). TDD +2 dashboard tests (foreign→404+no-insert; owned→201), RED-proven; **41/41 dashboard**, tsc 0, all 4 IDOR detectors green. ⚠️ **CONCURRENT-DEPLOY RACE bit** ([[stale-deploy-reverts-fix]]): my 1st `wrangler deploy` (de63a3eb) was REVERTED ~7min later by a concurrent session's deploy from THEIR tree (lacking my uncommitted change) — the browser probe caught it (events foreign→404 vs bookings foreign→**201** = my code absent live). Fix = **commit+push FIRST, then redeploy** (so origin has it + concurrent `pull --rebase` sessions inherit it). Remaining vein candidates need per-handler consumer analysis (does the FK grant cross-tenant access downstream?): agency `preselected_template_id`, social `thread_id`, bolt `chat_id` (domain_purchase `suggestion_id` = benign analytics ref). A precise detector is hard (must know which FK columns are cross-tenant + distinguish already-gated reads) — next-iteration candidate. **✅ iter-178 SHIPPED the detector + CONFIRMED the vein FULLY CONTAINED.** `scripts/check-body-fk-write.mjs` (4th IDOR guard, wired into `npm run check` + `test:scripts`) is an ALLOWLIST-as-triage-registry tripwire: it enumerates every `X_id: body.X_id` store and flags any field NOT in the triaged allowlist. Solved the "hard precision" problem by NOT trying to detect ownership checks (fuzzy) — instead every currently-stored body-FK field is triaged ONCE (in the allowlist w/ its safety reason), so it's green now + fires only on a NEW un-triaged field (prefer-false-negatives; gated handlers use a local var `calendar_id: calendarId` so the raw `body.X_id` store pattern doesn't even match them). Enumerated the FULL 12-field surface + triaged every one SAFE: gated (`calendar_id` events+bookings, `commit_id` revertToSnapshot-within-owned-site), org-scoped-row labels (`thread_id`/`site_id`/`component_id`), consumer-revalidated (`preselected_template_id`→templates.ts visibility gate), external ids (`channel_id` Telegram/Discord, `voice_voice_id` TTS provider), session key (`chat_id`), analytics ref (`suggestion_id`), super-admin cross-tenant-by-design (`stripe_charge_id`/`target_user_id`). **So the body-FK vein has ZERO remaining real IDORs** — events+bookings were the only two, both fixed. 7 detector unit tests (`node --test`), green. CI-tooling only (worker bundle unchanged → no redeploy; ships via push). **⚠️ NEXT:** the 3 org-IDOR classes + this 4th are all detector-gated + tree-clean → the IDOR frontier is converged; move to the deferred waves (ag-grid→TanStack; create→build engine CF-10074-blocked) or a fresh contract-untested-endpoint vein.

It should not be, instead you should focus on ensuring everything from the UI works properly with real-life test scenarios that ensure entire flows work."* See [[feedback_loop_verifies_real_flows_not_programs]].
- **The loop's job is now REAL end-to-end product-flow verification, NOT programming** (no more regex micro-fixes / dead-code hunts / built-ahead infra). iter 56 deleted `html_ast` (Brian-confirmed dead weight) — that's the LAST such cleanup; do NOT mass-delete more (~220 uncalled services are mostly intentional built-ahead per [[dead-code-scan-baseline]]).
- **THE GAP:** the existing e2e MOCKS the backend — `golden-path.spec.ts` stubs `/api/sites/create-from-search`; `admin-editor-journey.spec.ts` uses a fake published `e2e-site-001` + explicitly does NOT assert the editor's cross-origin traffic. They prove the UI RENDERS, never that the PRODUCT WORKS. This violates the repo's own "Real-Browser E2E Mandate: NOT mocked APIs."
- **#1 TARGET — the canonical real flow** (real backend, real build, Playwright/Browserbase vs prod, mock USER rows not mocked APIs): (1) `/create` a site → (2) it BUILDS for real → (3) view it, it's good → (4) `/admin/editor` enter a requirement (title → "TESTTESTTEST") → (5) verify the live site updates → (6) publish. The paid build (~$15/~40min) → run DELIBERATELY behind an env gate (e.g. `E2E_REAL_BUILD=1`), skipped in normal CI, but it MUST exist + be run to prove the product.
- **Then**: one full-flow real E2E per UI surface (search, auth, editor, publish, domains, billing, media, snapshots…) — each proving its flow works end-to-end against the real backend, replacing/augmenting the mocked contract specs.
- Real routes: `/create` → `/details` → `/waiting?id=&slug=`; `/admin/editor` (bolt.diy iframe `.bolt-frame` → editor.projectsites.dev); `/admin/sites/:id`. Real endpoints: `POST /api/sites/create-from-search`, `POST /api/sites/:id/publish-bolt`, `POST /api/publish/bolt`. Authed E2E: `get-secret E2E_API_KEY` (psk_test_) + `playwright.prod.config.ts`.

## 🔧 iter 216 (lakeside-dental "Booting workspace" — root cause + fix; container self-diagnosis)

- **🔍 User-reported:** lakeside-dental.projectsites.dev editor stuck on "Booting workspace", console showed `Chat ID changed from 1 to default`.
- **Root cause 1 (the stuck overlay):** the boot skeleton + PS_BOLT_CHAT_READY emitter probed for the LEGACY placeholder 'Build a professional website for' — the current chat input placeholder is 'What are we shipping?' → probe never fired → only the 30s timeout cleared it. Fixed via TS-bound `[pattern]` constants (`[\-a-z0-9]+`) — static attributes + escaped dashes both collapse in Angular's compiler; only a runtime-bound constant survives.

- **Target:** the iter-227 NEXT (worker-side). TDD'd three heartbeat-loop defects the journey's live eviction exposed (all in `site-generation.ts`):
  1. **Poll budget never extended across a restart** — `MAX_POLLS=120` was fixed; a build evicted past ~poll 90 re-booted with a fraction of its ~14-min window and ALWAYS timed out. Both paths set it; the missing-record branch honors it.
  3. The stale-restart path also gets the budget extension (it re-boots identically).

## 🔧 iter 246 (security-review wave fold — Vercel marketplace IDOR CLOSED, deployed)

- **The security-reviewer's HIGH finding landed and was fixed + deployed same fire** (`9178d84b`): the unauthenticated Vercel-marketplace callback wrote `mcp_connections` keyed on the CLIENT-supplied `configurationId` with `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` — any Vercel user could clobber a victim's connection row with their own token (victim's site then ran Vercel API calls as the attacker). Now the row key = the exchange-returned `installation_id`; a mismatched client id → 403 before any write; a missing installation_id → 502. 3 regression tests RED→GREEN (31/31 — the branch had zero coverage). Worker deployed `3bd24611`; prod E2E 17/17.
- **Subagent saturation confirmed structural**: all 8 honest-write mutators died `subagent_tokens: 0` (CLAUDE.md-heavy repo + deep session).
